TL;DR: 本文提出了一个可解的高维生成对抗网络（GAN）训练模型，其中线性生成器从具有结构化潜协方差的数据中学习低维子空间。研究证明了微观随机训练过程在高维极限下收敛到由有效协方差驱动的确定性常微分方程，揭示了信号增强机制：低秩相关性可以提升弱方向的可学习性，而过强的相关性则会破坏恢复。数值模拟和MNIST、FashionMNIST、CIFAR-10实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有可解GAN分析假设无条件信号且潜协方差为对角形式，缺乏对类别依赖、相关性和非零均值潜结构的研究。本文旨在扩展多特征判别器设置，分析这些异质性结构对GAN训练动力学和学习能力的影响。

Method: 构建高维线性生成器GAN模型，引入含结构化潜协方差的数据生成过程；采用二次能量判别器；利用高维极限下的随机过程收敛理论，证明微观训练过程收敛到确定性ODE；通过有效协方差矩阵刻画各类异质性；进行匹配协方差特例下的稳定性分析。

Result: 所有异质性通过概率加权的有效二阶矩进入动力学。学习开始于有效特征值跨过下阈值，完全恢复需要所有相关有效模式保持在学习区间内。低秩相关性可增强弱方向信号，过强相关性导致不稳定。数值模拟和图像数据集实验验证了ODE、相边界和增强机制。

Conclusion: 本文为GAN训练中结构化协方差的影响提供了严格的理论框架，揭示了信号增强与稳定性之间的权衡，为设计更好的生成器协方差初始化提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We study a solvable high-dimensional model of generative adversarial network (GAN) training in which a linear generator learns a low-dimensional subspace from data with structured latent covariance. Prior solvable GAN analyses assume unconditional signals with diagonal latent covariance; we extend the multi-feature discriminator setting to class-dependent, correlated, and non-zero-mean latent structure. For the quadratic energy discriminator, all such heterogeneity enters the dynamics through a probability-weighted effective second moment. We prove that the stochastic microscopic training process converges, in the high-dimensional limit, to deterministic ordinary differential equations governed by this effective covariance. In the matched-covariance specialization, the stability analysis yields a mode-wise solvable interval determined by the learning rates and noise level: learning begins when the leading effective eigenvalue crosses the lower threshold, while full recovery requires all relevant effective modes to remain within the interval. This reveals a signal-boosting mechanism: low-rank correlations can lift weak directions above the learnability threshold, whereas overly strong correlations destabilize recovery. Numerical simulations validate the ODE, phase boundary, and boosting mechanism. Experiments on MNIST, FashionMNIST, and CIFAR-10 further show that informed generator covariance improves alignment with the data-driven reference subspace.

</details>


### [106] [RSPC: A Benchmark for Modeling Stress and Psychiatric Conditions in Digitally Mediated Relationships using Psychiatrist Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27247)
*Parmitha Vangapandu,Sai Ganesh Mokkapati,Sathwik Narkedimilli,MSVPJ Sathvik,Timothy Liu,Simon See,Johannes C. Eichstaedt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了关系压力与精神病学语料库（RSPC），包含1,799条由精神科医生标注的Reddit帖子，涵盖焦虑、抑郁等情绪障碍、关系压力触发因素及关系阶段信息。通过微调7个Transformer模型和5个大语言模型进行多标签分类、触发检测和阶段预测任务，发现Claude-3-Haiku在障碍分类上最优（Macro-F1=0.538），GPT-4o在触发检测上最优（Macro-F1=0.519），并揭示了焦虑障碍与关系不确定性之间的强关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前NLP心理健康研究常将心理疾病视为孤立现象，忽略人际背景和关系情境。本研究旨在通过长距离关系中的Reddit帖子，同时捕捉心理健康困扰及其相关的关系触发因素，推动从个体中心向情境感知的心理健康建模转变。

Method: 构建RSPC语料库（1,799条精神科医生标注的Reddit帖子），涵盖情绪障碍（焦虑、抑郁）、关系压力触发因素和关系阶段三类标注。对7个微调Transformer模型和5个大语言模型在多标签障碍分类、关系触发检测和时间阶段预测三个任务上进行基准测试。

Result: Claude-3-Haiku在障碍分类任务表现最佳（Macro-F1=0.538），GPT-4o在关系触发检测任务最优（Macro-F1=0.519），不同模型家族呈现明显的任务依赖性差异。同时发现焦虑障碍与慢性关系不确定性之间存在强关联。

Conclusion: RSPC为考虑关系情境的NLP任务建立了基准，支持从个体中心向情境感知的心理健康建模转变，能够捕捉困扰的社会和时态动态特征。

Abstract: In NLP, mental health conditions are often modeled as isolated phenomena, without interpersonal context. We use Reddit posts about long-distance relationships to capture both mental health distress and associated relational triggers. We introduce the Relational Stress and Psychiatry Corpus (RSPC) containing 1,799 Reddit posts annotated by psychiatrists for diagnostic categories, including the most prevalent mood disorders (anxiety and depression), relational stressor triggers, and indications of relationship phase. We benchmark seven fine-tuned transformer models and five large language models across multi-label disorder classification, relational trigger detection, and temporal phase prediction tasks. We find clear task-dependent differences between model families, with Claude-3-Haiku achieving the best disorder classification performance (Macro-F1 = 0.538) and GPT-4o obtaining the strongest relational trigger detection performance (Macro-F1 = 0.519), suggesting distinct model capabilities. We further find strong associations between anxiety disorders and chronic relational uncertainty. Overall, RSPC establishes a benchmark for NLP tasks that consider relational context and supports a shift from individual-centric to context-aware mental health modeling that captures the social and temporal dynamics of distress.

</details>


### [107] [BetXplain: An Explanation-Annotated Dataset for Detecting Manipulative Betting Advertisements on Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27274)
*MSVPJ Sathvik,Parmitha Vangapadu,Nishit Rane,Sathwik Narkedimilli,Mark Lee,Akrati Saxena*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了从Instagram和Reddit收集的博彩广告数据集，包含人工标注的操纵性和欺骗性广告及其解释，并分析了博彩广告中常见的说服策略及其对用户心理健康的影响，最终提出了用于检测此类广告的自动化框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体上博彩应用广告激增，这些广告使用说服技巧可能误导用户、鼓励风险行为并影响心理健康，但缺乏公开标注的数据集来研究自动化检测方法。

Method: 从Instagram和Reddit收集博彩广告，进行人工标注（包括分类标签和推理解释），分析常见说服策略及其对心理健康的影响，构建检测框架。

Result: 构建了包含人工标注和解释的博彩广告数据集，识别了常见的操纵性广告策略，并提出了可支持浏览器插件和网络爬虫等应用的检测框架。

Conclusion: 该数据集和框架可用于开发检测操纵性博彩广告的实用工具，辅助用户和监管机构识别此类有害内容。

Abstract: The promotion of betting applications on social media platforms has increased significantly in recent years. Many of these advertisements use persuasive techniques that may mislead users, encourage risky behavior, and potentially influence users' mental well-being. However, research on the automated detection of manipulative and deceptive betting advertisements remains limited due to the lack of publicly available annotated datasets. In this work, we introduce a new dataset of betting-related advertisements collected from two widely used social media platforms, Instagram and Reddit. The advertisements were manually annotated for manipulative and deceptive advertising practices. In addition to classification labels, the dataset includes human-provided explanations that describe the reasoning behind each annotation, enabling research into explainable approaches to detecting manipulative advertising. Furthermore, we analyze the strategies commonly used in betting advertisements and examine how these persuasive tactics may impact users' mental health. The proposed framework can also enable practical applications such as browser plugins that warn users about manipulative betting advertisements and automated web crawlers that help regulatory authorities monitor and detect such promotions online.

</details>


### [108] [How Good Can Linear Models Be for Time-Series Forecasting?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27282)
*Lang Huang,Jinglue Xu,Luke Darlow*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文挑战了时间序列预测中"更大模型带来更高精度"的主流假设，通过Ridge回归实验证明：优化预处理策略（上下文长度、归一化、正则化、数据增强）能以极低成本缩小与大型模型的性能差距。研究发现最优回溯长度呈序列特异性且非单调，局部归一化优于全局归一化，跨序列超参数共享程度因数据集而异。优化后的Ridge回归在6/8个基准上超越Transformer、MLP和CNN基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战时间序列预测领域日益追求大型架构（从专用Transformer到通用基础模型）的趋势，质疑"模型容量决定预测精度"的隐含假设，主张通过更经济的预处理调优来缩小与大型模型的性能差距。

Method: 以Ridge回归（闭式解、可解释权重）作为实验平台，对八个标准基准数据集进行超参数搜索，覆盖上下文长度、局部归一化、正则化和数据增强四个维度。通过分析最优超参数的模式，揭示数据本身的特性。

Result: (1) 最优回溯长度高度序列特异性，与预测窗口呈非单调关系，拟合幂律指数从+0.46（ETTm2）到-0.19（Exchange/Traffic）。(2) 在上下文的学习尾部进行局部归一化几乎总是优于全局归一化。(3) 同一数据集内的序列对超参数选择存在分歧，最优跨序列共享程度因数据集而异。优化后的Ridge回归优于先前线性预测器，并在6/8基准上超越Transformer、MLP和CNN基线。

Conclusion: 预处理优化能以极低计算成本接近甚至超越大型架构的性能，推翻"更大模型必然更好"的假设。同时，优化后的超参数可作为数据诊断工具，揭示大型模型隐式吸收的数据结构。

Abstract: Time-series forecasting research has been moving steadily toward larger architectures, from specialized transformers to general-purpose foundation models, on the assumption that capacity is what unlocks accuracy. We take the opposite position: most of the gap can be closed at far lower cost by tuning preprocessing rather than scaling models. We use Ridge regression as the testbed, since it has a closed-form solution and interpretable weights, which let the optimal hyperparameters be read off the search directly. We search over context length, local normalization, regularization, and augmentation on eight standard benchmarks and find three patterns. (1) Optimal lookback is strongly series-specific and often non-monotonic in forecast horizon, with fitted power-law exponents ranging from $+0.46$ on ETTm2 to $-0.19$ on Exchange and Traffic, challenging the convention that longer horizons need longer history. (2) Normalizing over a learned trailing fraction of the context, rather than its entirety, is almost universally preferred. (3) Series within the same dataset often disagree on hyperparameters; the optimal degree of cross-series sharing varies from fully shared to fully per-series. The resulting models beat prior linear forecasters on most dataset-horizon entries and exceed Transformer, MLP, and CNN baselines on six of eight benchmarks. The optimized hyperparameters also serve as a diagnostic on the data itself, revealing structures that larger models absorb silently into their learned parameters.

</details>


### [109] [Recovering Governing Equations from Solution Data: Identifiability Bounds for Linear and Nonlinear ODEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27285)
*Yang Pan,Helmut Bölcskei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对科学机器学习中从观测数据学习控制方程的理论空白，引入解集上的Hausdorff距离作为比较微分方程的自然度量，建立了线性到非线性ODE类的可辨识性界，并基于度量熵推导了样本复杂度下界，量化了可靠恢复控制方程所需的观测解数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献缺乏从多个解观测中唯一且稳定识别真实ODE的理论条件，也没有对该学习任务的样本复杂度进行定量分析。

Method: 引入解集上的Hausdorff距离作为比较微分方程的自然度量，该度量捕捉所有允许初始条件下两个方程的最坏情况分离，编码了辨识问题的极小化极大结构。利用该度量建立线性ODE到Lipschitz/Hölder连续非线性向量场等广泛结构方程类的可辨识性界，并推导度量熵估计和样本复杂度界。

Result: 建立了不同结构方程类的可辨识性条件，获得了度量熵估计，并量化了可靠恢复控制方程所需的样本复杂度上界。

Conclusion: Hausdorff距离是度量微分方程辨识问题的合适框架，为从解数据中学习控制方程提供了理论保障和样本复杂度分析工具。

Abstract: Learning governing equations from observed solution data is a fundamental challenge in scientific machine learning \cite{bruntonDiscoveringGoverningEquations2016,kovachkiNeuralOperatorLearning2023,longPDENetLearningPDEs2018,rudyDatadrivenDiscoveryPartial2017,raonicConvolutionalNeuralOperators2023}, yet the theoretical conditions under which a ground-truth ODE can be uniquely and stably identified from multiple solution observations remain largely undeveloped, and no quantitative analysis of the sample complexity of such learning tasks exists in the literature. To address this gap, we introduce the Hausdorff distance on solution sets as the natural metric for comparing differential equations, since it captures the worst-case separation between two equations over all admissible initial conditions and thus encodes the minimax structure of the identification problem. We establish identifiability bounds for governing ODEs across a wide class of structure equations--ranging from linear ODEs to nonlinear classes with Lipschitz (Hölder)-continuous vector fields--characterizing precisely when two distinct equations can be distinguished from solution data. Using this metric, we derive metric entropy estimates for the relevant ODE classes and analyze sample complexity bounds, quantifying how many solution observations are needed to reliably recover the governing equation.

</details>


### [110] [Designing Reward Signals for Portable Query Generation: A Case Study in Industrial Semantic Job Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27291)
*Ping Liu,Qianqi Shen,Jianqiang Shen,Wenqiong Liu,Rajat Arora,Yunxiang Ren,Chunnan Yao,Dan Xu,Baofen Zheng,Wanjun Jiang,Andrii Soviak,Kevin Kao,Jingwei Wu,Wenjing Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种端到端的RLAIF框架，用于生成可移植的求职搜索查询，发现训练成功根本上取决于奖励塑造（reward shaping）而非优化器选择，且GRPO对虚假奖励信号尤为敏感，引入基于规则的奖励下限可显著提升质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 求职搜索平台依赖低带宽查询接口，难以捕捉候选档案的高维复杂性，现有方法生成的查询缺乏可移植性。

Method: 提出端到端RLAIF框架生成可移植搜索查询；通过奖励工程对抗优化器对LLM评分标准的利用；引入确定性规则奖励下限纠正逐字复制的奖励偏差。

Result: 无评论基线优化器的性能主要由奖励塑造主导；GRPO对虚假奖励信号尤其敏感；引入规则奖励下限后质量提升+0.147；训练时奖励模型将增益放大2.4倍。

Conclusion: 训练成功依赖于奖励塑造纪律而非优化器选择；GRPO的组相对优势归一化使其易被利用；规则奖励可有效缓解奖励黑客攻击。

Abstract: Job-search platforms rely on low-bandwidth query interfaces that often fail to capture the high-dimensional complexity of candidate profiles. We present an end-to-end RLAIF (Reinforcement Learning from AI Feedback) framework to generate \emph{portable} job search queries, terms that abstract away seeker-specific identifiers while preserving generalizable qualifications. This task introduces a highly adversarial reward surface where policy optimization frequently exploits flaws in LLM-as-judge rubrics, resulting in degenerate verbatim-copying behaviors.
  We conducted comprehensive empirical experiments to isolate the impact of optimization mechanics against structured reward engineering. Our results demonstrate that for critic-free optimizers, performance is overwhelmingly dictated by robust reward shaping, rendering the specific choice of algorithm largely immaterial. While critic-free per-rollout baseline methods (RLOO and REINFORCE++) natively resist reward-hacking, the group-relative advantage normalization in GRPO appears uniquely sensitive to spurious reward signals, making it disproportionately susceptible to exploitation. We show that introducing a deterministic, rule-based reward floor to correct for rewards assigned to verbatim copying mitigates this failure mode, resulting in a substantial $+0.147$ quality improvement on a cross-family evaluation judge. Ultimately, we show that the training-time reward model inflates performance gains by $2.4\times$, confirming that the training success is fundamentally dependent on enforcing reward-shaping disciplines rather than selecting alternative optimizers.

</details>


### [111] [A Multi-Fidelity Convolutional Autoencoder-Transfer Learning Framework for Guided-Wave-Based Damage Diagnosis Using Large Simulated and Limited Experimental Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27304)
*Santosh Kapuria,Abhishek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多保真度迁移学习框架，结合轻量物理仿真、卷积自编码器深度特征学习、前馈神经网络和少量实验数据，实现了板状结构中损伤的精确定位与尺寸估计，在R²评分上分别超过0.93和0.99，且具有良好的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 导波结构健康监测中深度学习模型的实际部署受限于标记实验数据稀缺以及大规模高保真仿真数据集生成的高计算成本。

Method: 采用一维时域谱元模型生成大量合成数据进行预训练，通过卷积自编码器进行深度特征学习，结合前馈神经网络，并利用少量实验数据进行迁移学习微调。

Result: 损伤定位R²评分超过0.93，损伤尺寸估计R²评分超过0.99；在未见过的损伤场景中仍保持高预测精度；CAE框架在定位精度上显著优于CNN框架。

Conclusion: 该多保真度迁移学习框架被证明是一种准确、计算高效且具有实际可行性的真实世界导波结构健康监测解决方案。

Abstract: Guided wave-based structural health monitoring (GWSHM) with onboard transducers offers significant potential for the early diagnosis of damage in engineering structures. However, the practical deployment of deep learning models is often hindered by the limited availability of labelled experimental data and the high computational cost of generating large-scale high-fidelity simulation datasets. This study presents a multifidelity transfer learning framework that integrates lightweight physics-based simulations, convolutional autoencoder (CAE)-based deep feature learning, a feed-forward neural network, and limited experimental measurements for accurate damage localisation and sizing in plate-like structures instrumented with piezoelectric transducers. A computationally efficient one-dimensional time-domain spectral element model is employed to generate a large synthetic dataset for pretraining, while transfer learning adapts the model to experimental domains using only a small amount of labelled data. The CAE-based transfer learning framework significantly outperforms its CNN-based counterpart in damage localisation accuracy. The model achieves excellent predictive performance with $R^2$ scores exceeding 0.93 for damage localisation and 0.99 for damage sizing. Its generalisation capability is demonstrated on previously unseen data, showing high prediction accuracy for damage scenarios not represented during pretraining or fine-tuning. The results establish the proposed framework as an accurate, computationally efficient, and practically viable solution for real-world GWSHM applications.

</details>


### [112] [Blackwell Approachability and Gradient Equilibrium are Equivalent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27315)
*Brian W. Lee,Nika Haghtalab,Michael I. Jordan,Ryan J. Tibshirani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明梯度均衡（GEQ）在算法意义上等价于Blackwell可逼近性，进而等价于遗憾最小化和校准等经典在线学习框架，并建立了GEQ与有约束/无约束决策集之间的约简关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GEQ作为新兴在线优化框架，与遗憾最小化等经典框架的关系尚不明确，已有工作指出两者误差指标不可比，需精确理解GEQ在在线学习全景中的定位。

Method: 通过构造双向黑箱约简：证明任何Blackwell可逼近问题可通过黑箱GEQ oracle求解且无渐近损失，反之亦然；进而利用已知的可逼近性与遗憾最小化、校准之间的等价关系建立GEQ与它们的等价性。

Result: GEQ与Blackwell可逼近性算法等价，从而与遗憾最小化、校准等框架等价；实现了乐观性、强自适应性等精细化保证从遗憾最小化到GEQ的迁移；给出了GEQ的充要条件及有约束/无约束决策集间的约简。

Conclusion: GEQ并非独立于经典在线学习框架，而是与Blackwell可逼近性、遗憾最小化、校准等核心框架等效，为GEQ的理论分析和算法设计提供了更丰富的工具。

Abstract: Gradient equilibrium (GEQ) is a recently introduced online optimization framework that generalizes first-order stationarity from offline optimization and abstracts problems like online conformal prediction. While GEQ has curious similarities with known online learning frameworks, namely regret minimization, prior work has shown that GEQ error and regret are incomparable objectives, leaving open a precise understanding of how GEQ fits into the broader online learning landscape. In this work, we show that GEQ is equivalent to Blackwell approachability in the algorithmic sense. That is, a Blackwell approachability problem can always be solved using queries to a black-box GEQ oracle, with no asymptotic loss in the oracle's error rate, and vice versa. Taken together with known equivalences between approachability, regret minimization, and calibration, these results imply that GEQ is equivalent to these frameworks, as well. Our reductions are efficient and can be used to transfer refined guarantees, such as optimism and strong adaptivity, from regret minimization to GEQ. Along the way, we also identify necessary and sufficient conditions for GEQ, and establish reductions between different notions of GEQ with unconstrained and constrained decision sets.

</details>


### [113] [Beyond the Hard Budget: Sparsity Regularizers for More Interpretable Top-k Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27321)
*Nathanaël Jacquier,Maria Vakalopoulou,Mahdi S. Hosseini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种与Top-k稀疏自编码器架构兼容的稀疏正则化方法（ℓ1惩罚和ℓ1/ℓ2比率惩罚），在不牺牲重建质量的前提下提升了单义性，证明了硬性架构稀疏性与软性稀疏正则化具有互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Top-k SAE虽然通过激活函数强制实现了架构稀疏性，避免了传统ℓ1惩罚的缺陷，但仍存在固定预算k（不随输入复杂度变化）和对训练时k值过拟合的问题。现有方法未将显式稀疏正则化与Top-k架构结合。

Method: 在Top-k选择前对激活施加两种正则化：1）对未选中的（off-support）单元施加ℓ1惩罚；2）施加尺度不变的ℓ1/ℓ2比率惩罚，将编码集中到更少的有效单元。两种惩罚仅应用于batch-active单元（在batch内至少被Top-k选中一次的单元）。

Result: 在两个数据集、三个视觉基础模型以及不同k值下，两种正则化方法均一致提升了单义性且不损害重建质量。ℓ1/ℓ2惩罚进一步将信息集中在更少的隐单元上，使重建对推理时的k值选择更鲁棒，并提升了小预算线性探针的性能。

Conclusion: 硬性架构稀疏性与软性稀疏正则化并非互斥，而是互补的，二者的结合可以更有效地提升特征的可解释性。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) have become a leading tool for interpreting the representations of vision foundation models, decomposing their polysemantic activations into a larger set of sparse, more monosemantic features. The Top-$k$ SAE, a now-standard variant, enforces sparsity architecturally through its activation function, retaining only the $k$ most active latents per input. Because it was designed precisely to avoid the $\ell_1$ penalty used by earlier SAEs and its known drawbacks, it has not been combined with an explicit sparsity regularizer, despite retaining limitations of its own, such as a budget $k$ that is fixed regardless of input complexity and a tendency to overfit to the training value of $k$. We introduce two sparsity regularizers compatible with the Top-$k$ architecture, both acting on the activations before the Top-$k$ selection: an $\ell_1$ penalty on the unselected (off-support) units, and a scale-invariant $\ell_1/\ell_2$-ratio penalty that concentrates the code onto fewer effective units. Both penalties are applied only to the batch-active units, those selected by the Top-$k$ operator at least once within the batch. Across two datasets, three vision foundation models, and a range of $k$, both regularizers consistently improve monosemanticity at no cost to reconstruction quality. The $\ell_1/\ell_2$ penalty further concentrates information into fewer latents, making reconstruction more robust to the inference-time choice of $k$ and improving small-budget linear probing. Our central finding is that hard architectural sparsity and soft sparsity regularization are complementary rather than mutually exclusive.

</details>


### [114] [Error-Conditioned Neural Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27354)
*Haina Jiang,Liam Wang,Peng-Chen Chen,Min Seop Kwak,Seungryong Kim,Brian Bell,Jeong Joon Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出误差条件神经求解器（ENS），通过将PDE残差场作为网络直接输入而非优化目标，让网络学习迭代校正预测的策略，在四个PDE族上实现最高预测精度，相比混合方法避免了高计算成本，且在分布偏移下保持泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经代理模型将求解视为纯统计任务，无法在校正约束违反和分布外推；混合方法通过梯度下降最小化PDE残差来提升物理正确性，但继承了经典优化器的高计算成本和不稳定性，且在病态系统中残差最小化可能是重构精度的不可靠代理。

Method: 提出误差条件神经求解器（ENS）：不将PDE残差作为优化目标，而是将其作为网络每次迭代的直接输入，使网络能够读取自身误差的空间结构，学习迭代校正预测的更新策略。

Result: 在四个PDE族中，ENS在大多数设置下取得最高预测精度，在湍流Kolmogorov流上提升达10倍；避免了混合方法的昂贵计算成本；在分布偏移下（零样本参数变化、跨方程迁移）泛化良好，在病态区域优势最大。

Conclusion: 理论上和实验上证明了数值最小化PDE残差在病态系统中可能是重构精度的不可靠代理；ENS通过学习校正策略，在病态区域（残差最小化最不可靠时）展现出最大相对优势。

Abstract: Neural surrogate models offer fast approximate mappings from PDE parameters to solutions, but they typically treat solving as a purely statistical task: once trained, they struggle to correct their own constraint violations and extrapolate beyond the training distribution. Recent hybrid methods promote physical correctness by targeting the PDE residual via gradient descent or Gauss--Newton steps, but inherit the compute cost and instability of the underlying classical optimizers. We show, theoretically and empirically, that numerically minimizing the PDE residual can be an unreliable proxy for reconstruction accuracy in ill-conditioned systems, explaining why these methods often do not make accurate predictions despite achieving low residuals. We propose error-conditioned Neural Solvers (ENS), built on a different principle: rather than an optimization target, the PDE residual field is passed as a direct input to the network at each iteration, enabling it to read the spatial structure of its own errors and learn an update policy to iteratively correct its predictions. Across four PDE families, ENS attains the highest prediction accuracy in the large majority of settings, with gains reaching $10\times$ on turbulent Kolmogorov flow, while avoiding the expensive compute cost of hybrid methods. ENS's learned correction policy generalizes under distribution shift, including zero-shot parameter changes and cross-equation transfer, where its relative advantage is largest in the ill-conditioned regimes where residual minimization is least reliable. Project website: https://neuralsolver.github.io/.

</details>


### [115] [Autoregressive Boltzmann Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27361)
*Danyal Rehman,Charlie B. Tan,Yoshua Bengio,Avishek Joey Bose,Alexander Tong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自回归玻尔兹曼生成器（ArBG），通过使用自回归建模框架替代基于流的玻尔兹曼生成器，克服了归一化流在表达性和计算效率上的局限性，在分子平衡态采样任务上取得显著提升，并训练了1.32亿参数的迁移模型Robin，在零样本能量误差上降低超过60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有玻尔兹曼生成器主要依赖归一化流（NFs），但离散时间NFs受严格可逆性约束导致表达性有限，连续时间NFs则面临计算昂贵的似然估计问题。亟需一种能突破这些拓扑约束的新框架。

Method: 提出自回归玻尔兹曼生成器（ArBG），采用自回归建模框架替代流模型范式，规避了流的拓扑约束，支持推理时的顺序干预，并利用在大语言模型中有效的架构提升可扩展性。

Result: ArBG在所有基准测试中均显著优于基于流的模型，尤其在10残基Chignolin等较大肽系统中表现突出。训练了1.32亿参数的Robin迁移模型，在8残基系统的零样本能量误差E-W$_2$上相比此前最优方法降低超过60%。

Conclusion: 自回归框架是玻尔兹曼生成器的一个有前途的新方向，能够突破流模型的拓扑限制，实现更好的可扩展性和迁移性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Efficient sampling of molecular systems at thermodynamic equilibrium is a hallmark challenge in statistical physics. This challenge has driven the development of Boltzmann Generators (BGs), which allow rapid generation of uncorrelated equilibrium samples by combining a generative model with exact likelihoods and an importance sampling correction. However, modern BGs predominantly rely on normalizing flows (NFs), which either suffer from limited expressivity due to strict invertibility constraints (discrete time) or computationally expensive likelihoods (continuous time). In this paper, we propose Autoregressive Boltzmann Generators (ArBG) -- a novel autoregressive modelling framework -- that overcomes these limitations by departing from the flow-based BG paradigm. ArBG circumvents the topological constraints of flows and enables sequential inference-time interventions, while offering enhanced scalability by leveraging architectures effective in Large Language Models. We empirically demonstrate that ArBG leads to significant improvements over flow-based models across all benchmarks, but particularly in larger peptide systems such as the 10-residue Chignolin. Furthermore, we introduce Robin, a 132 million parameter transferable model trained with the ArBG framework which improves over the previous state-of-the-art, reducing the zero-shot energy error, E-W$_2$, on 8-residue systems by over 60$\%$. The code can be found at the following link: https://github.com/danyalrehman/autobg.

</details>


### [116] [Reinforcement Learning without Ground-Truth Solutions can Improve LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27369)
*Yingyu Lin,Qiyue Gao,Nikki Lijing Kuang,Xunpeng Huang,Kun Zhou,Tongtong Liang,Zhewei Yao,Yi-An Ma,Yuxiong He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RiVER框架，通过校准的连续奖励信号（而非真实解标签）训练LLM在基于分数的优化任务上，不仅提升了启发式竞赛性能，还意外地改善了对精确求解基准（如LiveCodeBench、USACO）的泛化能力，而使用原始分数训练的基线则无法迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法依赖真实答案来分配奖励，限制了其在真实解未知的任务（如分数优化任务）中的应用。作者希望在没有真实解的场景下也能有效训练LLM。

Method: 提出RiVER框架，使用确定性执行反馈作为连续监督信号。针对连续奖励下的两个挑战——尺度主导（不同实例的分数量级未校准导致策略更新失真）和频率主导（反复采样的次优解压倒罕见但更强的候选解），RiVER通过实例级比较进行校准奖励塑形，强调排名靠前的求解者，同时为其他有效解保留有界反馈。

Result: 在12个AtCoder启发式竞赛任务上训练，在ALE-Bench上将Qwen3-8B和GLM-Z1-9B-0414分别提升8.9%和9.4%；在LiveCodeBench和USACO上绝对平均提升2.4%和3.5%。使用原始执行分数的基线在ALE上有所提升，但无法迁移到精确求解基准。

Conclusion: 基于分数的优化任务结合适当的奖励校准，可以在没有真实解的情况下作为通用编码能力的有效训练环境。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) for training LLMs typically rely on ground-truth answers to assign rewards, limiting their applicability to tasks where the ground-truth solution is unknown. We introduce a \textbf{R}anking-\textbf{i}nduced \textbf{VER}ifiable framework (RiVER) that trains LLMs on score-based optimization tasks without ground-truth solutions, using deterministic execution feedback as continuous-valued supervision. When applying group-relative RL to such continuous rewards, we identify two key challenges: \emph{scale dominance}, where uncalibrated score magnitudes across test instances distort policy updates, and \emph{frequency dominance}, where repeatedly sampled suboptimal solutions can outweigh rare but stronger candidates. RiVER addresses these challenges with calibrated reward shaping that uses instance-wise comparisons and emphasizes top-ranked solvers while retaining bounded feedback for other valid solutions. We train on 12 AtCoder Heuristic Contest tasks and evaluate on Algorithm Engineering Benchmark (ALE-Bench), LiveCodeBench, and USACO. RiVER advances Qwen3-8B and GLM-Z1-9B-0414 by 8.9\% and 9.4\% in ALE rating rank. More importantly, despite training exclusively on score-based tasks without any ground-truth solutions, RiVER also improves the backbones across exact-solution benchmarks such as LiveCodeBench and USACO by an absolute average improvement of 2.4\% and 3.5\%. By contrast, baselines trained with raw execution scores improve ALE rating but fail to transfer to exact-solution benchmarks. These results suggest that score-based optimization tasks, combined with proper reward calibration, can serve as effective training environments for general coding ability without ground-truth solutions.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [MIRAGE: Protecting against Malicious Image Editing via False Moderation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26199)
*Anshul Nasery,Ramnath Kumar,Cho-Jui Hsieh,Sewoong Oh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MIRAGE是一种针对AI图像编辑系统的防御方法，通过在图像中添加对抗性扰动，触发商业编辑系统的预生成安全审核模块，使其将图像标记为违反政策，从而阻止任何编辑操作，无需访问模型权重或编辑提示，成功率超过88%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像免疫方法需要访问模型权重和图像编辑提示，这限制了它们对商业图像编辑系统（如GPT-Image、Gemini Flash Image、Grok Imagine）的防护能力。

Method: 从系统级视角识别商业图像编辑系统的共同攻击面——预生成安全审核模块，通过添加对抗性扰动，使图像在开源嵌入和审核模型的表示空间中与违反政策的类别对齐，从而触发审核拒绝。

Result: 针对多个闭源图像编辑API的测试中，MIRAGE的成功率超过88%。

Conclusion: 该方法简单、与提示无关且有效，为保护个人图像免受未经授权的AI编辑提供了一条实用路径。

Abstract: The proliferation of AI-powered image editing systems raises serious concerns because it allows personal images to be arbitrarily manipulated at scale, with minimal effort, and a lower barrier to entry. Prior work on image immunization adds imperceptible perturbations to an image to protect against unauthorized manipulations. However, these methods usually require access to the model weights and the image manipulating prompt. This significantly limits their use, especially against powerful commercial image-editors such as GPT-Image, Gemini Flash Image (Nano Banana), and Grok Imagine. To address this, we take a system-level view of the problem and identify a previously unexplored attack surface common to all major commercial image editing systems: pre-generation safety moderation.Rather than disrupting the generative model itself, we propose to immunize images by causing these moderation classifiers to flag images as policy-violating, triggering an automatic refusal regardless of the editing prompt. We operationalize this by adding adversarial perturbations to align our image to policy-violating concepts in the representation space of an ensemble of open-source embedding and moderation models. We call our method MIRAGE, which stands for Moderation Induced Resistance Against Generative Editing. We evaluate MIRAGE against multiple closed-source image editing APIs and demonstrate success rates of more than 88%. Our approach is simple, prompt-agnostic, and effective, offering a practical path towards protecting personal images from unauthorized AI-powered editing.

</details>


### [118] [Data Facts: A Metadata Schema for Structured Data Exchange in the NANDini Multi-Agent Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26211)
*Jin Gao,Maria Gorskikh,Pradyumna Chari,Brittany Box,Mukul Kemla,Pratik Behera,Abhishek Mehta,Ramesh Raskar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出Data Facts元数据方案，填补自主代理间结构化数据交换的空白，实现从代理发现到数据访问的闭环，经实验验证在决策准确性、数据新鲜度、完整性校验和安全防护方面均取得显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代理协议（如NANDA、A2A、MCP）仅支持身份发现与通信，缺乏结构化数据交换机制；而企业级数据共享框架（如IDS-RAM、Gaia-X、Ocean Protocol）依赖人工参与，无法满足自主代理实时交互的需求。

Method: 在Agent Facts注册记录中新增`data_facts_url`指针，指向轻量JSON元数据（含数据集身份、访问层级、端点、TTL、SHA-256校验和）；对私有和半私有数据实现三层安全流水线：JWT认证、能力范围网关授权、A2A凭证委托协议。

Result: 840次决策评估中，有数据访问的代理准确率100%（vs. 无数据访问35.2%，p<0.001）；TTL将过期数据错误率从37.6%降至8.8%；校验和实现100%损坏检测；安全流水线阻断全部46次伪造攻击，零数据泄露。

Conclusion: Data Facts作为NANDini生态的核心概念，有效解决了自主代理间结构化数据交换的缺失问题，为大规模自动化数据驱动决策提供了可行的基础设施方案。

Abstract: NANDini (Networked Agents Natural Distillation of Interconnected Nodal Intelligence) envisions an automated ecosystem where intelligent agents independently create, process, and exchange data to drive decisions at scale. Realizing this vision requires infrastructure beyond agent discovery and communication: agents must be able to advertise, evaluate, and verify the datasets they hold.
  Current protocols, including NANDA for federated registry and A2A and MCP for inter-agent messaging, address identity and communication but provide no mechanism for structured data exchange. Existing enterprise data-sharing frameworks, such as IDS-RAM, Gaia-X, and Ocean Protocol, assume human-in-the-loop governance that is incompatible with autonomous, real-time agent interactions.
  We introduce Data Facts, a core NANDini concept: a lightweight JSON metadata schema that bridges agent discovery and data access through a single pointer, `data_facts_url`, added to an existing Agent Facts registry record. The linked document encodes dataset identity, access tier, whether public, semi-private, or private, endpoint, a time-to-live for freshness validation, and a SHA-256 integrity checksum.
  For private and semi-private data, we implement a three-layer security pipeline: JWT authentication, capability-scoped gateway authorization, and an A2A credential delegation protocol. Across 840 decision-making evaluations, data-informed agents achieve 100% accuracy versus 35.2% without data access (p < 0.001); TTL enforcement reduces stale-data errors from 37.6% to 8.8%; checksum verification achieves 100% corruption detection at all injection rates; and the security pipeline blocks all 46 forgery attempts with zero data leakage.

</details>


### [119] [CyberChainBench: Can AI Agents Secure Smart Contracts Against Real-World On-Chain Vulnerabilities?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26216)
*Jintao Huang,Fengqing Jiang,Radha Poovendran,Zhiqiang Lin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了CyberChainBench基准，用于评估LLM智能体在智能合约安全方面的三类任务（漏洞检测、漏洞利用生成、补丁合成）。基于541个真实DeFi安全事件构建，通过历史状态分叉和隔离环境进行端到端评估。最佳配置在检测、利用和补丁上的得分分别为37.5%、43.7%和23.4%，顶级智能体在200个案例中实现5740万美元的总利用利润，每案例成本2.39美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准缺乏对LLM智能体在智能合约安全领域真实、端到端、经济可量化的评估体系，尤其是缺乏基于真实历史漏洞、可交互验证的评估方法。

Method: 从DeFiHackLabs收集541个真实EVM链上的DeFi安全事件，构建三类任务基准。利用Harbor搭建隔离评估环境，通过主网分叉和工具交互（读代码、追踪交易、验证利用）实现端到端评估。漏洞利用按经济影响评分，补丁通过回放历史攻击和合法交易作为测试预言机来验证。定义了五种漏洞分类体系。

Result: 最佳配置在检测任务上得分37.5%，利用任务43.7%，补丁任务仅23.4%。顶级智能体Codex+GPT-5.5在200个案例中实现5740万美元总利用利润，平均每个案例花费2.39美元。任务难度存在明显梯度。

Conclusion: 当前LLM智能体在智能合约安全任务上表现有限，特别是在补丁合成方面远未达到实用水平；检测和利用任务仍有较大提升空间。该基准为未来研究提供了标准化的评估框架。

Abstract: We present CyberChainBench, a benchmark for evaluating LLM-based agents on smart contract security across three complementary tasks: vulnerability detection, exploit generation, and patch synthesis. Built from 541 real-world exploit incidents from DeFiHackLabs spanning 9 EVM chains, the benchmark provides end-to-end on-chain evaluation where agents interact with historical blockchain state through isolated evaluation environments orchestrated by Harbor, using tools to read code, trace transactions, and validate exploits on mainnet forks. Each case is anchored to a specific block and includes structured ground truth covering vulnerability type, localization, and attacker profit. Exploits are graded by economic impact on historical forks; patches are validated by replaying historical attacks and legitimate transactions as fail-to-pass test oracles on a proxy-upgradeable subset. We define a five-type vulnerability taxonomy and evaluate multiple agent--model configurations. Results reveal a clear difficulty gradient: the best configuration scores 37.5% on detection, 43.7% on exploitation, but only 23.4% on patching, with the top agent (Codex with GPT-5.5) realizing \$57.4M in total exploit profit across the 200-case exploit set at a cost of $2.39 per case.

</details>


### [120] [Expecting (Targeted Ads)? Network Analysis of User Health Data Leakage in Fertility Tracking Apps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26276)
*Yeeun Jo,Shahanaasree Sivakumar,Mahnoor Jameel,Camille Cobb,Adam Bates,Brad Reaves*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 通过对20款Android生育追踪App的网络测量研究，发现其中5款应用存在用户健康数据显式泄露及通过上下文广告URL的隐式泄露，同时部分同类型应用采用广告盈利模式但未发现数据泄露，为用户隐私担忧提供了技术依据并强调了消费者选择的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究大量关注生育追踪App中的人为隐私因素，但缺乏对应用数据处理实践的技术层面分析，特别是与第三方广告服务共享用户数据的具体机制。

Method: 对Google Play商店中20款Android生育追踪App进行基于网络的测量研究，在记录TLS剥离网络流量的环境中执行标准化用户交互，系统化分析App功能并检测数据泄露行为。

Result: 在5款App中发现用户健康数据的显式泄露以及通过高度定向上下文广告URL的隐式泄露；同时发现部分App采用广告盈利模式但无明显数据泄露，另有部分App与广告服务交互极少。

Conclusion: 研究为用户普遍的隐私担忧提供了技术层面的实证支持，同时强调了消费者在选择生育追踪App时对隐私影响进行权衡的重要性。

Abstract: While human factors in the privacy of fertility tracking apps -- health trackers that record user's menstrual or pregnancy data -- has been the subject of extensive study, little attention has been paid to the technical aspects of apps' data handling practices. We conduct a network-based measurement study of a corpus of 20 Android fertility tracking apps from the Google Play Store, focusing on how user data is shared with third party advertising services. After systematizing app features, we conduct a series of standardized user interactions across all apps in an environment that records TLS-stripped network traffic. In a subset of apps (n=5) we identify explicit leakage of user health data as well implicit leakage through highly targeted contextual advertising URL's. Equally importantly, we observe additional apps that use an ad-based monetization model without apparent leakage of user data, as well as several apps the interact only minimally with ad services. These findings provide technical grounding for widespread user concerns, but also underscore the importance of consumer choice in the privacy implications of app-based fertility tracking.

</details>


### [121] [TEMPO-Diffusion: Temporally Exposed Malicious Poisoning of Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26285)
*William Aiken,Paula Branco,Guy-Vincent Jourdan,Iosif-Viorel Onut*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出TEMPO-Diffusion，一种针对扩散模型的时间条件后门攻击框架，支持类特定目标攻击、多子图像后门和时序条件触发；同时引入CALISA数据集。实验表明该方法能有效毒化类特定合成数据并导致下游分类器高攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于噪声的后门攻击依赖输入时触发注入、非目标激活和分布外目标生成，降低了隐蔽性和实际相关性。需要一种更隐蔽、更具实践意义的后门攻击方法。

Method: 提出TEMPO-Diffusion框架，将恶意分布偏移定位到时间上的分布内暴露。支持：(i) 针对特定类的目标攻击，(ii) 多子图像后门（在多个不同输出图像的多个位置重构特定特征），(iii) 基于时间条件触发器的图像修复。同时引入CALISA数据集（平衡的区域感知交通标志数据集）。

Result: 在CIFAR10、GTSRB和CALISA数据集上的实验表明，TEMPO-Diffusion能够可靠地毒化类特定合成数据生成，并导致训练在该数据上的下游分类器具有高攻击成功率。

Conclusion: TEMPO-Diffusion是一种更具隐蔽性和实用性的后门攻击框架，通过时间条件触发和分布内暴露实现了高效的类特定攻击。

Abstract: Noise-based backdoor attacks on diffusion models typically rely on input-time trigger injection, untargeted activation, and out-of-distribution target generation. Such assumptions reduce both the stealthiness and the practical relevance of these attacks. In this work, we present TEMPO-Diffusion, a targeted backdoor framework that localizes the malicious distribution shift to a temporal, in-distribution exposure. TEMPO-Diffusion supports: (i) targeted attacks on and to specific classes, (ii) multiple sub-image backdoors that reconstruct specific features within multiple, different output images and at multiple locations, and (iii) in-painting with time-conditioned triggers. To study relevant, practical security concerns in leveraging backdoored diffusion models for synthetic training data, we also introduce CALISA: a balanced, region-aware traffic-sign dataset emphasizing Canadian and U.S. road signs. Across CIFAR10, GTSRB, and CALISA, our experiments show that TEMPO-Diffusion can reliably poison class-specific synthetic data generation and induce high attack success rates in downstream classifiers trained on that data.

</details>


### [122] [Beyond Takedown: Measuring Malicious Go Module Persistence in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26291)
*Minjae Bae,Carter Yagemann*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对Go生态系统中的自动化供应链攻击活动进行了测量分析。攻击者将被篡改的恶意模块以自动化方式重新打包并嵌入混淆代码，实现导入触发的下载器。通过手动搜索（2,113个仓库）和基于AST去混淆扫描器（12.3M索引条目）两种互补分析，共识别出2,289个恶意版本的合法Go模块。研究发现，仅依赖GitHub的搜索无法完全发现攻击范围，且仅对平台上尚存的代码有效。代理测量显示，在GitHub已不可见的恶意组件中，至少有99.4%仍可通过Go代理检索。披露后，GitHub移除了684个恶意仓库，Google Go团队修复了1,377个模块版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Go生态系统中存在自动化供应链攻击，攻击者将恶意代码嵌入合法模块，威胁开发者安全。现有基于GitHub的检测方法存在盲区，需要更全面的测量方法以评估攻击的真实范围和影响。

Method: 采用两种互补分析方法：1）对2,113个GitHub仓库进行手动搜索；2）实现基于AST的去混淆扫描器GOAST，对12.3M索引条目进行大规模扫描。同时通过代理测量评估takendown-remediation gap（下架-修复差距）。

Result: 识别出2,289个恶意版本的合法Go模块，发现GitHub不可见的组件中99.4%仍可通过Go代理获取。GitHub移除684个恶意仓库，Google修复1,377个模块版本。

Conclusion: 仅依赖GitHub的检测方法存在严重局限，无法覆盖已被平台移除但仍可通过代理访问的恶意模块。需要建立跨平台、多层次的供应链安全检测机制。

Abstract: We measure an automation-based supply chain campaign in the Go ecosystem. The attackers repackage legitimate Go modules under attacker-controlled owners, and embed them with obfuscated code for an import-triggered downloader. Our results come from two complementary analyses: a) a manual search on GitHub across 2,113 repositories and b) a large-scale scan of 12.3M index entries using a deobfuscating AST scanner (GOAST) that we implemented. As a result, we identified 2,289 malicious versions of legitimate Go modules. We demonstrate that purely GitHub-centric searches fail to identify the full extent of the compromise and are only effective for as long as the affected code is present on the platform. Moreover, our proxy-based measurements of the takedown-remediation gap reveal that among artifacts later found to be GitHub-unobservable (i.e., removed or suspended), at least 99.4% remained retrievable via Go proxy. Following our disclosure, GitHub has removed 684 malicious repositories and the Google Go team has remediated 1,377 module versions.

</details>


### [123] [Hybrid privacy-aware semantic search: SVD-truncated document geometry and CKKS-encrypted query reranking under a restricted threat model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26373)
*Sergey Kurilenko*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合保护方案：文档向量通过SVD截断+秘密旋转进行几何保护，查询向量通过CKKS同态加密进行密码学保护，在百万级文档规模下实现亚秒级延迟，并严格证明了攻击者的重构误差下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有向量数据库的嵌入反转攻击可重构原始文本，而同态加密虽安全但速度太慢，隐私噪声则严重损害排序质量。作者希望找到一条兼顾安全性与实用性的中间路径。

Method: 利用静态集合与动态查询的不对称性：集合向量被截断到低维SVD子空间并用秘密正交变换旋转（几何保护），查询向量在CKKS同态加密下重排序（密码学保护）。CKKS参数通过离线小规模基准测试确定。

Result: 在百万文档和五个编码器上，方案保持甚至略微提升排序质量（作为线性去噪器），延迟亚秒级。标准反转攻击在受保护空间内退化为噪声。已知明文攻击可通过正交Procrustes恢复旋转，所需泄露对数量约等于保留维度。

Conclusion: 查询保密是密码学级别的，但文档保护是经验性的混淆层（SVD截断+秘密旋转），而非密码学原语。作者明确划定了每种声明的威胁模型边界。

Abstract: Dense embeddings power semantic search and retrieval-augmented generation, but embedding-inversion attacks can reconstruct source text from a vector: when a vector database leaks, the documents behind it leak too. The textbook defences are extremes - encrypting the whole search homomorphically is sound but too slow at million-document scale, while privacy noise degrades ranking long before it protects. We study a middle path exploiting the asymmetry between the static collection and the dynamic query. The collection is protected geometrically: each vector is truncated onto a lower-dimensional SVD subspace and rotated by a secret orthogonal transform known only to the owner. The query is protected cryptographically: it is reranked under CKKS homomorphic encryption, so an honest-but-curious server never sees the query or the scores. CKKS parameters come from a small offline benchmark.
  We prove a tight lower bound on the reconstruction error of any attacker confined to the protected subspace. On one million documents and five encoders the scheme preserves ranking quality (slightly improving it on strong encoders, as a linear denoiser) at sub-second latency, and an off-the-shelf inversion attack on the protected space collapses to the noise floor. We then test stronger adversaries: a known-plaintext attacker recovers the rotation by orthogonal Procrustes from about as many leaked pairs as the retained dimension; the public product-quantization codes preserve most nearest-neighbour structure; and random-projection, calibrated-noise and BEIR baselines show the truncation is an encoder-dependent accuracy cost, not a free denoiser. We state the limits: query confidentiality is cryptographic, but document protection is an empirical obfuscation layer (SVD truncation plus a secret rotation), not a cryptographic primitive, and we delimit the threat model for each claim.

</details>


### [124] [Verifying Intent and Harm: A Unified Defense Against LLM-Generated Threats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26377)
*Poojitha Thota,Yun Lei,Santhosh Thangaraj,Siddhartha Reddy Jonnalagadda,Shirin Nilizadeh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种验证为中心的防御框架，联合评估提示意图和响应危害性，在五种威胁类别上优于单一侧防御和单智能体推理基线，平均F1从0.90提升至0.95，攻击成功率降至4.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御要么只分析用户提示，要么只分析生成输出，但许多真实攻击利用提示中的对抗意图与响应中的可操作危害之间的分离，导致单侧防御经常漏检不安全交互。

Method: 提出验证为中心的防御框架，联合评估提示意图和响应危害，采用专门分析器分别评估意图与危害，并设法官进行冲突裁决。形式化了提示-响应攻击的威胁模型。

Result: 在越狱、提示注入、钓鱼、网络滥用和有害内容五类威胁上，联合验证一致优于单侧防御和单智能体推理基线。平均F1从0.90提升至0.95，攻击成功率降至4.1%，假阳性率从0.12降至0.06。

Conclusion: 提示-响应联合验证为保护LLM应用抵御不断演变的对抗威胁提供了实用基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in interactive applications, yet they remain vulnerable to adversarial interactions that induce harmful, deceptive, or policy-violating outputs. Existing defenses typically analyze either user prompts or generated outputs, but not both. However, many real-world attacks exploit a separation between adversarial intent expressed in the prompt and actionable harm manifested only in the response. As a result, prompt-only and response-only defenses frequently miss unsafe interactions that appear benign when viewed from either side in isolation. We present a verification-centric defense framework that jointly evaluates prompt intent and response harm before an LLM response is delivered to a user. The framework employs specialized analysts for intent and harm assessment together with a Judge for conflict resolution. We formalize a threat model for prompt-response attacks and evaluate the framework across five threat categories: jailbreaks, prompt injection, phishing, cyber abuse, and harmful content. Experiments on multiple benchmark datasets show that jointly verifying prompt intent and response harm consistently outperforms single-sided defenses and single-agent reasoning baselines. Across threat categories, the framework improves average F1 from 0.90 for the strongest applicable baselines to 0.95 while reducing the average attack success rate to 4.1 percent. Compared with a Single-Agent+CoT baseline, it improves average F1 from 0.87 to 0.95 and reduces the false positive rate on benign-sensitive requests from 0.12 to 0.06. We further evaluate architecture-aware adaptive attacks in which the attacker knows the verifier structure and attempts to bypass individual verification components. Our results suggest that prompt-response verification provides a practical foundation for securing LLM applications against evolving adversarial threats.

</details>


### [125] [Lessons from the Adoption and Deprecation of the Privacy Sandbox Web APIs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26390)
*Yohan Beugin,Paul Barford,Patrick McDaniel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对Google Privacy Sandbox项目进行长达七年的纵向测量研究，分析各API在Chrome生态中的采用与废止情况。结果表明，该计划的采纳率始终有限且不均衡，仅有少数网络行为者以不同方式实现特定API。作者从激励因素（兴趣、资源、时间线等）和风险因素（隐私权衡、法律暴露等）角度解释这一现象，并为下一代Web隐私提案提出可行建议。文章还指出"在浏览器中修复"方案的局限性——Chrome对跟踪和第三方Cookie的限制仍多为自愿选择，而其他浏览器已默认启用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管多年来多个网络行为者试图减少Web跟踪，但如何在效用和隐私之间取得理想平衡仍不明确。Google于2019年启动Privacy Sandbox项目以探索这一权衡，然而该项目于2025年底被取消。这为研究生态系统如何应对这些变化以及项目失败的原因提供了独特机会。

Method: 利用历史HTTP Archive爬取数据和公开的Chrome遥测数据，对Privacy Sandbox各API在其整个生命周期（部分超过5年）内的采用情况进行大规模纵向测量分析，覆盖CrUX排名前10万的流行网站以及Chrome用户的真实浏览体验。

Result: Privacy Sandbox API的采用率始终有限且不均衡，仅少数网络行为者实现了非常具体的API，且实现方式各异。Chrome对跟踪和第三方Cookie的限制仍主要依赖用户主动选择（opt-in），而Brave、Firefox、Safari等浏览器已默认启用这些限制。

Conclusion: 本文通过对激励因素（兴趣、资源、时间线等）和风险因素（潜在权衡、隐私侵犯、法律暴露等）的分析，为下一代Web隐私提案提出了可行建议。更广泛地看，Privacy Sandbox案例揭示了"在浏览器中修复"方案的局限性和跨浏览器差异。

Abstract: While several web actors have been trying to reduce web tracking for years, it remains unclear how to achieve both desirable levels of utility and privacy. In 2019, Google launched the Privacy Sandbox initiative to balance that trade-off and find privacy alternatives to common use cases such as advertising. Yet, in late 2025, Google canceled the project and deprecated most of the newly introduced APIs. Despite its end, the Privacy Sandbox represents a unique opportunity to learn about how the ecosystem reacted to the proposed changes and make observations about why and how it failed. In this paper, we present a longitudinal measurement and analysis study of the Privacy Sandbox APIs to characterize their adoption and deprecation over the past seven years by different web actors. Leveraging historical HTTP Archive crawls and public Chrome telemetry data, we offer the largest study of its kind into the prevalence of each Privacy Sandbox feature, during their entire respective lifetime (5+ years for some), on popular websites (CrUX top 100k), and as experienced by Chrome users during their browsing journey. Our results showcase an adoption that remained limited and uneven across the years; only few web actors implemented very specific APIs, and in disparate manners. We motivate our interpretation of these results by considering the incentives (interest, resources, timeline, etc.) and risks (potential trade-offs, privacy violations, and legal exposure, etc.) for these actors. Finally, our analysis also yields actionable recommendations for the next generation of web privacy proposals. More broadly, the Privacy Sandbox illustrates the limitations and disparities across browsers of ``fix it in the browser'' remedies: today, tracking and third-party cookies limitations in Chrome still remain largely opt-in, while they have been enabled by default on other browsers like Brave, Firefox, or Safari.

</details>


### [126] [What Browsers Do in the Shaders: A Measurement Study of WebGPU Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26412)
*Igor Santos-Grueiro*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: WGPULens是一个评估WebGPU隐私泄露的框架，通过可控实验、参与者研究和网页爬取三种方式测量GPU状态暴露的信号。研究发现流水线编译状态是最清晰的泄露面，原生GPU活动可从浏览器可见信号推断，WebGPU在真实网页中使用有限。研究提出源代码密钥分离作为流水线缓存分区的代理评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WebGPU虽然通过验证模型保证了内存安全，但共享的浏览器、驱动、操作系统和GPU状态仍然可能暴露隐私相关信号，需要系统化的测量框架来评估这些风险。

Method: 提出WGPULens框架，包含三个测量维度：(1) 可控场景测量泄漏、边界和缓解策略；(2) 参与者实地研究测量部署、兼容性和可指纹性；(3) Tranco爬取测量真实网页中WebGPU的使用情况。

Result: (1) 流水线编译状态是最清晰的隐私泄露面，冷/热管道探测可揭示先前编译状态；(2) 原生GPU活动可从浏览器可见信号推断；(3) 参与者研究中WebGPU行为高度独特，确定性组件在单次运行内稳定；(4) 网页爬取发现WebGPU主要用于适配器探测和静态支持代码，未观察到页面加载时的着色器、流水线、队列等动态活动。

Conclusion: WebGPU隐私分析必须是表面特定的：浏览器需要测量哪些GPU状态跨越了哪些边界、哪些浏览器可见信号揭示了它们以及相应缓解措施的成本。源代码密钥分离可作为流水线缓存分区的代理评估方法。

Abstract: WebGPU lets ordinary web pages run GPU workloads through a validated programming model. Validation protects memory safety, but shared browser, driver, OS, and GPU state can still expose privacy-relevant signals. We present WGPULens, a framework for measuring those signals across controlled scenarios, browser-native co-residency, a participant field study, public page loads, and mitigation policies. Our framework separates measurements: controlled scenarios support leakage, boundary, and mitigation claims; participant runs support deployment, compatibility, and fingerprintability; and a Tranco crawl measures WebGPU exposure in real-world pages.
  Our controlled results identify persistent pipeline compilation state as the clearest surface. Cold/warm pipeline probes reveal prior compilation state across selected origin, profile, and browser placements. Controlled browser/native experiments also show native GPU activity can be inferred from browser-visible observables under labeled workloads. Other resource probes provide weaker positive results and negative controls.
  The participant field study shows active WebGPU behavior is highly distinctive within the sample, with deterministic components stable within runs and lower exact stability across repeated visits. A page-load crawl finds WebGPU use mainly as adapter probing and static support code, with no observed page-load shader, pipeline, queue, query, or map activity. Mitigation pilots identify source-level key separation as a proxy for evaluating pipeline-cache partitioning. Overall, WGPULens shows that WebGPU privacy analysis must be surface-specific: browsers need to measure which GPU state crosses which boundary, which browser-visible signals reveal it, and what the corresponding mitigations cost.

</details>


### [127] [Adaptive Evaluation of Out-of-Band Defenses Against Prompt Injection in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26479)
*Praneeth Narisetty,Shiva Nagendra Babu Kore,Uday Kumar Reddy Kattamanchi,Jayaram Kumarapu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文梳理了针对工具使用LLM智能体的间接提示注入的“带外防御”方法（如CaMeL、FIDES、Progent等），指出这些方法本质上是经典完整性保护（Biba）、引用监控和最小权限原则的实例。同时警告所有现有验证仅基于静态基准，缺乏自适应攻击评估。作者在AgentDojo上对Progent进行了独立复现和自适应攻击扩展实验，结果表明防御有效（攻击成功率从25.8%降至4.2%），但仅是小规模数据点，强白盒攻击仍待研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对工具使用LLM智能体的间接提示注入防御（2024-2026年）趋于采用确定性策略强制执行安全（带外防御），但缺乏系统性的理论归类，且验证方法存在严重缺陷——仅依赖静态基准，而历史上同类方法曾因自适应攻击而崩溃。作者旨在提供结构化比较，并揭示验证方法论的不足。

Method: 首先将CaMeL、FIDES、Progent等系统归类为经典Biba完整性保护、引用监控和最小权限原则的实例，进行结构化对比。然后明确自适应评估所需的威胁模型和协议，并在AgentDojo基准上使用开源模型（Qwen2.5-7B）自托管于单张H200上，对Progent进行独立复现和自适应攻击扩展实验。

Result: Progent将平均攻击成功率从25.8%降至4.2%（约六倍降低），手工构建的自适应攻击并未提升成功率（2.6%）。但这是小规模数据点，基于弱模型和单一黑盒攻击模板，更强的白盒GCG攻击仍未测试。

Conclusion: 结果与“确定性带外强制比带内检测更难被自适应攻击者攻破”的假设一致，但尚不能确立该假设。需更强攻击和更大规模验证。

Abstract: Recent work (2024 to 2026) has converged on a strategy for defending tool-using LLM agents against indirect prompt injection: rather than training the model to refuse malicious instructions, enforce security outside the model with a deterministic policy that mediates the agent's actions. Systems such as CaMeL, FIDES, Progent, RTBAS, and FORGE realize this with capabilities, information-flow labels, and reference monitors, and several report near-elimination of attacks on the AgentDojo benchmark. We make two contributions. First, we organize these out-of-band defenses as instances of classical integrity protection (Biba), reference monitoring, and least privilege, yielding a structured comparison of what they do and do not cover. Second, we warn that every one of them is validated only on static benchmarks (a fixed set of injection attempts), the same methodology that made in-band defenses look strong until adaptive, defense-aware attacks broke twelve of them at over 90% success; we specify the threat model and protocol an adaptive evaluation requires. We then run that protocol as an independent reproduction and extension of Progent's own adaptive-attack analysis, on AgentDojo, with an open-weight agent (Qwen2.5-7B) self-hosted on a single H200, a setting its authors did not test. Averaged over three runs, the defense held: Progent cut mean attack success roughly sixfold (25.8% to 4.2%), and a hand-crafted adaptive attack did not raise it (2.6%). This is one small-scale data point on a weak model with a single black-box attack template; a stronger optimized (white-box GCG) attack remains open. The result is consistent with, but does not establish, the hypothesis that deterministic out-of-band enforcement is a harder target for an adaptive attacker than in-band detection.

</details>


### [128] [DKVE: Decentralized Key Validation for End-to-End Encrypted Messaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26486)
*Subin Song,Taekyoung Kwon*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: DKVE提出了一种利用社交图谱进行隐私保护交叉验证的公钥分发协议，通过OPRF和OKVS保护查询隐私，使用SPRT聚合响应检测MitM攻击，在强社交网络中检测率超过97%，可将KT查询频率降低两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端加密消息系统的公钥分发面临严峻权衡：带外验证安全性高但缺乏可扩展性，密钥透明性系统支持自动化验证但存储成本和操作复杂度过高。需要一种兼具安全性、可扩展性和低成本的公钥验证机制。

Method: DKVE协议的核心方法包括：(1) 客户端通过密钥服务器获取联系人公钥后，向共同联系人发起交叉验证查询；(2) 利用不经意伪随机函数(OPRF)和不经意键值存储(OKVS)保护查询隐私和联系人列表隐私；(3) 采用序贯概率比检验(SPRT)聚合多方响应，以用户可配置的误差界检测服务器恶意行为。

Result: 在真实社交网络数据集上的仿真表明：强到中强连接网络中对MitM攻击的检测率超过97%，剩余3%需通过KT或OOB等替代方法验证。概念验证实现证实了在商品硬件上作为后台运行的延迟和带宽可行性。可将KT查询频率降低两个数量级。

Conclusion: DKVE无法完全替代现有方法（存在启动问题，在弱连接网络中性能下降），但作为一种实用的互补性密钥验证机制，可显著降低KT系统负担，使KT目录从快速但空间低效的Merkle树迁移到高效数据结构（如RSA累加器），从而提升十亿用户级系统的安全消息部署可行性。

Abstract: End-to-end encrypted messaging systems depend on authentic public key distribution to prevent man-in-the-middle (MitM) attacks. Current solutions present a stark trade-off: out-of-band (OOB) verification provides strong security but lacks scalability for large contact lists, while key transparency (KT) systems enable automated verification at high storage costs and operational complexity.
  We propose DKVE, a protocol that validates public keys through privacy-preserving cross-validation within users' social graphs. When obtaining a contact's public key from a key server, clients query mutual contacts to verify they hold the same key, combining Oblivious Pseudorandom Functions (OPRF) and Oblivious Key-Value Stores (OKVS) to preserve privacy of both queries and contact lists. DKVE employs a Sequential Probability Ratio Test (SPRT) to aggregate responses and detect server misbehavior with user-configurable error bounds.
  We evaluate DKVE through simulations on real social network datasets, demonstrating DKVE can detect MitM attacks with exceeding 97% for strong-to-moderate-tie networks. The remaining 3% of cases require validation through alternative methods such as KT and OOB verification. Our proof-of-concept implementation confirms feasibility for background operation on commodity hardware, in terms of the latency and bandwidth.
  As DKVE can reduce the frequency of KT queries by two orders of magnitude, it enables fundamental architectural shifts: KT directories can migrate from fast but space-inefficient Merkle trees to space-efficient data structures like RSA accumulators. While DKVE cannot replace existing methods entirely -- suffering from bootstrapping problems and degraded performance on weak-tie networks -- it provides a practical complementary key validation mechanism, making secure messaging more deployable for billion-user systems.

</details>


### [129] [VIGIL: Runtime Enforcement of Behavioral Specifications in AI Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26524)
*Ying Li,Yanju Chen,Hongbo Wen,Bosi Zhang,Hanzhi Liu,Peiran Wang,Yu Feng,Yuan Tian*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: VIGIL是一个面向智能体系统的端到端运行时强制框架，通过将行为策略转化为SMT约束来检测跨多步骤的违规行为，在真实LLM智能体任务中实现了超过95%的召回率和低于10%的假阳性率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体系统通过第三方技能执行模型生成的决策，这些技能包含自然语言规范定义权限与前提条件，但缺乏可执行的运行时强制机制。现有方案采用固定事件模型或强制点，无法解决上下文粒度挑战：监控器需决定观察哪些事件、保留何种状态、推理多远以及何处干预，粒度不当会阻塞合法执行或遗漏跨多动作的违规。

Method: VIGIL提出一种策略语言，可捕获智能体-工具事件上的上下文特定强制需求，包括时间依赖、参数约束和值流条件。配合符号评估规则将策略转化为有限迹上的SMT约束，通过约束求解检测跨事件顺序、参数关系或跨调用值流的违规，而非依赖固定的单调用过滤器。

Result: 在办公文档、运维和工程任务等真实LLM智能体运行中，VIGIL检测策略违规的召回率超过95%，假阳性率低于10%。

Conclusion: VIGIL通过符号化策略语言与SMT约束求解，有效解决了智能体系统中策略执行的上下文粒度挑战，实现了高精度、低误报的运行时强制。

Abstract: Agentic systems increasingly act through third-party skills, allowing model-generated decisions to affect files, communication channels, and cyber-physical devices. These skills often include natural-language specifications that define access permissions, disclosure limits, execution privileges, and required preconditions. Although such specifications describe the intended boundaries of skill behavior, they do not by themselves provide executable runtime enforcement. Enforcing them raises a contextual granularity challenge: even when a policy is written for a particular task context, a monitor must still decide which events to observe, what state to retain, how far across the execution to reason, and where to intervene. Choosing the wrong granularity can either block benign executions or miss violations that emerge only across multiple actions. Most existing enforcement mechanisms, however, assume a fixed event model or enforcement point.
  In this work, we present VIGIL, an end-to-end runtime enforcement framework for agentic systems. VIGIL checks an agent's actual execution trace against behavioral policies from skill specifications, operator-defined constraints, and global rules spanning multiple skills. To make such policies executable, VIGIL introduces a policy language that captures context-specific enforcement requirements over agent-tool events, including temporal dependencies, argument constraints, and value-flow conditions. The language is paired with symbolic evaluation rules that translate policies into SMT constraints over finite traces, allowing VIGIL to detect violations that depend on event order, argument relationships, or cross-call value flow rather than relying on fixed single-call filters. On real LLM-agent runs spanning office-document, operational, and engineering tasks, VIGIL detects policy violations with over 95% recall and a false-positive rate below 10%.

</details>


### [130] [TESLA-for-5G: Broadcast Authentication for 5G Networks Using TESLA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26528)
*Subin Song,Michael K. Reiter,Taekyoung Kwon*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 针对5G基站广播未认证系统信息（SI）的安全漏洞，提出TESLA-for-5G (TF5)协议，结合TESLA与GG09 Schnorr类身份基签名，实现轻量级的SIB1广播认证，在稳态下用对称MAC和延迟密钥披露替代逐消息数字签名，并通过Tamarin形式化验证及OpenAirInterface实现证明了其高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 5G基站广播的SI未经认证，攻击者可伪造SI构建虚假基站（FBS）诱骗UE驻留。现有方案要求UE对每个SIB1接收进行计算密集的数字签名验证，对资源受限的UE造成严重负担。

Method: 提出TF5协议，结合TESLA（定时高效流丢失容忍认证）与GG09 Schnorr类身份基签名（IBS）。稳态下使用对称MAC和延迟密钥披露认证每个SIB1消息，避免逐消息数字签名；初始信任通过轻量级GG09 IBS在小区进入时引导TESLA参数，免去证书分发开销。使用Tamarin在Dolev-Yao敌手模型下进行形式化验证，并在OpenAirInterface 5G协议栈上实现及轨迹驱动分析评估。

Result: 形式化验证表明TF5在Dolev-Yao敌手下安全；实现与轨迹驱动分析显示TF5在计算、通信和存储开销方面具有优越性。

Conclusion: TF5有效解决了5G SIB1广播认证的开销问题，兼顾安全性与效率，适用于资源受限的UE。

Abstract: 5G base stations broadcast unauthenticated system information (SI) that every user equipment (UE) reads during cell selection. This enables attackers to broadcast forged SI from a fake base station (FBS), deceiving UEs into camping on it. Prior approaches require UEs to authenticate System Information Block 1 (SIB1) using digital signatures. This necessitates computation-heavy verification for every SIB1 reception, imposing a significant burden on resource-constrained UEs. We propose TESLA-for-5G (TF5), a broadcast authentication protocol for 5G SIB1 that combines TESLA with GG09 Schnorr-like identity-based signatures (IBS). In the steady state, TF5 enables UEs to authenticate each SIB1 message using a symmetric MAC and delayed key disclosure, eliminating the need for per-message digital signatures. Initial trust is bootstrapped during cell entry using a lightweight GG09 IBS over the TESLA parameters, avoiding certificate distribution overhead. We formally verify TF5 in Tamarin under a Dolev-Yao adversary and demonstrate its favorable computation, communication, and storage costs through both an implementation on the OpenAirInterface 5G stack and trace-driven analysis.

</details>


### [131] [Adversarial Diffusion Across Modalities: A Fusion Survey of Attacks, Defenses, and Evaluation for Text, Vision, and Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26566)
*Abrar Alotaibi,Moataz Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对AI系统对抗性评估中四个分散的研究方向（基于扩散的文本/LLM攻击、图像分类器攻击、视觉语言模型越狱管线、以及基于扩散的输入净化防御）进行了元研究层面的信息融合，提出了统一的概念框架、分类体系和研究路线图，重点聚焦LLM侧，并识别了当前LLM文献的五个系统性弱点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗性AI评估在四个方向上各自独立发展，形成了不同的术语体系、威胁模型和基准，但去噪扩散模型作为共享的生成机制正被各社区积极移植。缺乏统一框架导致知识碎片化，阻碍了跨方向的比较和协同进步。

Method: 采用元研究层面的信息融合方法：1）整合四个方向（文本/LLM攻击、图像分类器攻击、视觉语言模型越狱、扩散防御）进入统一概念框架；2）提出六类扩散角色分类法，辅以威胁模型轴（攻击者知识、查询预算、目标可及性）；3）应用五维评估框架（攻击成功率、可迁移性、查询预算、困惑度、防御规避）；4）采用攻击者-防御者双视角分析，并识别文献弱点。

Result: 编录了50篇已发表论文（覆盖四个范围领域+4篇扩散-LLM受害者条目+10个非扩散基线），提出了统一分类法，识别出当前LLM侧文献的五个重复性弱点，并发布了配套目录和电子表格。

Conclusion: 本文是带有质量评估的叙事性综述而非PRISMA合规的系统性综述，强调了可复制性的含义，并以开放问题和具体实验设计的研究路线图收尾，为跨方向对抗性评估提供了统一的元研究基础。

Abstract: Adversarial evaluation of AI systems has matured along four largely disconnected tracks: diffusion-based attacks on text and large language models (LLMs), diffusion-based attacks on image classifiers, jailbreak pipelines against vision-language models, and diffusion-based input purification defenses. Each has developed its own vocabulary, threat models, and benchmarks, with denoising diffusion models emerging as a shared generative mechanism whose recipes are now actively ported between communities. This survey performs an information-fusion exercise at the meta-research level: we integrate these four tracks into a single conceptual framework with a unified taxonomy, evaluation criteria, and research agenda, focusing on the LLM-side slice. We catalog fifty published papers across four scope areas (text/LLM, image classifier, vision-language model, defense), plus four diffusion-LLM-as-victim entries and ten non-diffusion baselines against which any new attack must be compared. We propose a six-class taxonomy of diffusion roles in adversarial pipelines, augmented by a threat-model axis recording attacker knowledge, query budget, and target accessibility, and apply a five-dimension framework (attack success rate, transferability, query budget, perplexity, defense-evasion) uniformly across modalities. The review adopts a dual attacker-defender perspective: alongside the attack catalog we cover four diffusion-based defenses that form the natural evaluation backdrop for new attacks. Our critical analysis identifies five recurring weaknesses of the current LLM-side literature, and we close with a research agenda of open questions and concrete experimental designs. The companion catalog and spreadsheet are released with the paper. We are explicit that this is a narrative review with quality assessment, not a PRISMA-compliant systematic review, and discuss the implications for replication.

</details>


### [132] [Agents That Know Too Much: A Data-Centric Survey of Privacy in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26627)
*Nada Lahjouji,Ashwin Gerard Colaco*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文从数据为中心的视角系统综述了LLM智能体的隐私风险，梳理了智能体接触的数据源、各数据源带来的隐私风险、相应的治理机制及现有基准测试，发现信息流控制是唯一能同时覆盖组合推理和跨会话推理泄漏的治理机制，且缺乏统一的跨数据面隐私策略基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体从回答问题转向处理敏感数据，查询数据库、调用API、记忆交互并代表用户行动，隐私问题变得复杂且难以执行。现有研究分散在不同领域（检索增强生成、Text-to-SQL、智能体记忆、提示注入、访问控制、上下文隐私），缺乏统一视角和系统性梳理。

Method: 采用数据中心的视角，不是按攻击类型而是按智能体接触的数据源来组织研究领域；对数据源进行分类，梳理每种数据源的隐私风险，映射治理机制，并评估现有基准测试的覆盖情况。

Result: 发现两个核心发现：(1) 在治理机制中，只有信息流控制能同时覆盖组合推理泄漏和跨会话推理泄漏这两个保护最薄弱的风险；(2) 缺乏一个能在统一隐私策略下驱动智能体跨越所有数据面的基准，这是该领域最缺失的工具。

Conclusion: 本文为分散的文献提供了统一参考框架，帮助未来研究建立共同语境，并指出了信息流控制和统一隐私策略基准这两个关键开放问题。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly query databases, search document collections, call external APIs, remember past interactions, and act on a user's behalf. As they move from answering questions to operating over sensitive data, privacy becomes harder to enforce. An agent touches many data sources, runs multi-step workflows, keeps state across sessions, and acts with delegated permissions. Sensitive information can therefore leak not only through its final answer but through the queries it issues, the intermediate results it handles, the memory it writes, and the messages it exchanges with other agents. We survey the privacy of LLM agents from a data-centric view, organizing the field around the data an agent touches rather than by attack type, and we use data agent as shorthand for an LLM agent that works with data. Research on these risks is active but scattered across retrieval-augmented generation, text-to-SQL interfaces, agent memory, prompt injection, access control, and contextual privacy. This survey brings that work together: we taxonomize the data sources an agent touches, the privacy risks each source creates, and the governance mechanisms that address them; we map the benchmarks used to measure these risks and identify what is missing; and we set out the open problems. Two findings recur: among governance mechanisms only information-flow control covers both compositional and cross-session inference leakage, the two least-protected risks; and no benchmark drives an agent across its data surfaces under one privacy policy, the instrument the field most lacks. Our goal is a reference that situates the scattered literature and gives future work a common framing.

</details>


### [133] [TGHE: Template-based Graph Homomorphic Encryption for Privacy-Preserving GNN Inference in Edge-Cloud Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26664)
*Ngoc Bao Anh Le,Thai T. Vu,John Le,Heath Cooper,Jun Shen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出TGHE框架，利用交易图中局部计算树的结构模板现象，将同构的ego-graph打包到共享CKKS密文中进行SIMD并行加密推理，在370万节点的大规模金融图上实现66.9倍加速且精度损失极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于同态加密的GNN系统采用图中心范式，将每次查询成本与全局图规模耦合，最多只能处理约2万节点，无法兼容动态、大规模金融图。

Method: 提出TGHE框架，采用以自我为中心（ego-centric）范式，利用交易图中局部计算树收敛为少量结构模板的现象；在边缘对ego-graph进行规范化，将结构相同的树打包到共享CKKS密文中实现SIMD并行加密推理；并设计了两个长尾优化器（近似模板拟合和拓扑折叠）确保完全SIMD覆盖。

Result: 在DGraphFin（370万节点，430万边）上，TGHE-Collapse相比顺序加密基线实现66.9倍加速，AUC损失小于0.002。

Conclusion: TGHE通过模板化ego-graph和SIMD并行加密推理，有效解决了HE-GNN在大规模金融图上的可扩展性问题，实现了数量级的性能提升。

Abstract: Existing homomorphic encryption (HE)-based GNN systems adopt a graph-centric paradigm that couples per-query cost to global graph size, limiting evaluations to at most ~20k nodes and making them incompatible with dynamic, large-scale financial graphs. We propose TGHE (Template-based Graph Homomorphic Encryption), an ego-centric framework that resolves this by exploiting a template phenomenon: local computation trees in transaction graphs converge into a small set of structural shapes. TGHE canonicalizes ego-graphs at the edge and packs structurally identical trees into shared CKKS ciphertexts for SIMD-parallel encrypted inference, with two long-tail optimizers (Approximate Template Fitting and Topology Collapse) ensuring full SIMD coverage. On DGraphFin (3.7M nodes, 4.3M edges), TGHE-Collapse achieves a 66.9x speedup over the sequential encrypted baseline with less than 0.002 AUC loss.

</details>


### [134] [The Fungible Reserve Standard: A Deterministic Framework for Encoding Carrying Costs in Asset-Backed Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26704)
*JJ Jia Jing Tan,Eva Meng,Josh Ng,Zack Zhang,September Liu,Teelet Wang,Ludwig Zhang,Seth Yan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Fungible Reserve Standard（FRS）的确定性代币设计框架，用于将实物资产（如贵金属、大宗商品等）的结构性负持有成本（托管、保险、审计费用）透明地编码到链上逻辑中。FRS通过一个随时间递减的资产-代币比率变量q(t)和供应协调机制，在保持代币持有者余额不变且兼容ERC-20标准的前提下实现持有成本的链上管理，避免使用代币重基等破坏可互换性和DeFi可组合性的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RWA代币化模型虽然成功建立了数字黄金和国债市场，但通常只在发行方层面管理运营成本，未解决实物资产因托管、保险和审计等费用产生的结构性负持有成本问题。现有机制如代币重基会破坏代币的可互换性和与DeFi协议的可组合性，因此需要一种新的链上成本处理框架。

Method: FRS框架引入一个资产-代币比率变量q(t)，该变量根据预定义的年化持有成本率按时间递减；同时设计了一个供应协调机制，在不改变持有者代币余额的前提下实现成本的透明链上核算，从而保持ERC-20的可组合性。该设计数学上受传统资产管理中日费用率累积的启发，但仅编码实际运营成本而非嵌入中心化利润空间。

Result: 提出了一个资产无关的确定性代币设计框架，能够处理任何具有正向、可预测持有成本的实物资产的代币化问题，在保持DeFi兼容性的同时提供机构级的会计透明度。

Conclusion: FRS为RWA代币化中持有成本的链上管理提供了一种标准化解决方案，在不牺牲代币可互换性和DeFi可组合性的前提下，将实际运营成本透明地编码到链上逻辑中，具有跨资产类型的通用适用性。

Abstract: The tokenization of real-world assets (RWAs) has emerged as a transformative application of blockchain technology, with market projections estimating trillions of dollars in tokenized assets within the coming decade. However, a fundamental challenge remains unaddressed: physical assets such as precious metals, stored commodities, and warehoused goods incur structural negative carry -- custody, insurance, and audit costs that accumulate over time. While existing tokenization models have successfully established the market for digital gold and treasuries, they typically manage operational costs at the issuer level. The FRS introduces a framework to bring these economics directly on-chain, avoiding mechanisms such as token rebasing that compromise fungibility and composability with decentralized finance (DeFi) protocols. This paper proposes the Fungible Reserve Standard (FRS), a deterministic token design framework that encodes carrying costs transparently into on-chain logic. The FRS introduces an asset-per-token variable q(t) that decreases according to a predefined annualized carrying cost rate, coupled with a supply reconciliation mechanism that preserves holder balances and ERC-20 composability. While mathematically inspired by the daily expense ratio accrual in traditional asset management -- which often embed centralized profit margins -- the FRS design specifically encodes actual operational carrying costs to provide pure institutional-grade accounting clarity without compromising DeFi compatibility. The framework is asset-agnostic and applicable to any real-world asset with positive, predictable holding costs.

</details>


### [135] [DroidBreaker: Practical and Functional Problem-Space Attacks on Machine-Learning Android Malware Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26707)
*Christian Scano,Diego Soi,Angelo Sotgiu,Luca Demetrio,Davide Maiorca,Giorgio Giacinto,Fabio Roli,Battista Biggio*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出DROIDBREAKER，一种实用且语义保持的对抗性APK攻击框架，通过细粒度、构建安全的操作（注入API调用、模块、权限和URL）以及查询高效的白盒/黑盒攻击，在保持运行时语义等价的前提下实现高逃避率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗性APK攻击方法存在三大缺陷：1）粗粒度的良性模块移植引入大量副作用特征且常导致构建失败；2）细粒度方法效果有限，基于混淆的方法依赖脆弱的字节码重写，生成的APK语义不可用；3）先前工作仅通过安装和执行的基本冒烟测试评估攻击成功率，未验证修改后APK是否保持预期行为。

Method: DROIDBREAKER框架包含三个关键组件：1）查询高效的白盒和黑盒攻击，仅操纵对目标模型最具影响力的APK组件；2）一组细粒度、构建安全的操作手段（包括API调用、应用模块、权限和URL的注入与混淆），最小化副作用；3）语义保持的功能测试，通过比较原始APK和修改后APK的执行日志及API级轨迹来强制执行运行时等价性。

Result: 在最新的Android应用语料库上，DROIDBREAKER在白盒和黑盒设置下均以少量查询实现高逃避率，并显著降低了VirusTotal上商业恶意软件扫描器的检测率。

Conclusion: DROIDBREAKER克服了现有问题空间攻击的局限性，提供了兼具构建安全性和语义保持性的实用对抗攻击框架，为评估机器学习恶意软件检测器的鲁棒性提供了更可靠的基准。

Abstract: Adversarial APKs are Android applications modified in the problem space to evade machine-learning malware detectors. In this work, we first show that, despite claims, existing problem-space attacks remain largely impractical. Most techniques leverage software transplantation to inject entire benign modules, introducing many side-effect features and often causing build-time failures. Fine-grained methods that inject only a narrow subset of components exhibit limited effectiveness, while those that also use obfuscation rely on brittle bytecode rewriting, producing APKs that are syntactically valid but semantically unusable. Prior work further overestimates attack success rates by running smoke tests that only validate installation and basic execution, without assessing whether the modified APK still preserves its intended behavior. To overcome these limitations, we present DROIDBREAKER, a practical (build-safe) and functional (semantics-preserving) problem-space attack framework that provides: (i) query-efficient white- and black-box attacks by manipulating only the APK components most influential to the target model; (ii) a set of fine-grained, build-safe manipulations (including injection and obfuscation of API calls, app modules, permissions, and URLs) with minimal side effects; and (iii) a semantics-preserving functionality test that enforces runtime equivalence by comparing execution logs and API-level traces between the initial and the modified APK. Evaluated on a recent corpus of Android applications, DROIDBREAKER achieves high evasion rates with few queries and minimal side effects in both white-box and black-box settings, and drastically reduces detections by commercial malware scanners hosted on VirusTotal.

</details>


### [136] [MergeLLL: A Hierarchical Divide-and-Conquer Framework for LLL-Based Lattice Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26784)
*Niharika Gauraha*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MergeLLL是一种基于分治策略的格基约简算法，通过分层合并和PotLLL式深度插入，在保持格结构的同时实现了更好的正交性、更少的交换操作和改进的Hermite因子，并支持并行化执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统格基约简算法（如LLL）的复杂度随维度快速增加，现有方法在高维场景下效率低下，需要一种可并行化且计算成本更低的高质量格基约简方法。

Method: 受归并排序的分治策略启发，结合PotLLL风格深度插入，将格基拆分为子基，独立进行局部约简，然后通过分层合并重构完整基；约简过程先改善局部格结构再优化全局基性质。

Result: 在子集和与NTRU衍生格上的实验表明，MergeLLL相比经典格基约简算法实现了更好的正交性、更少的昂贵交换操作次数以及改进的Hermite因子，即更高质量的约简基。

Conclusion: MergeLLL通过分治合并策略有效降低了计算成本，改善了Gram-Schmidt正交性和数值稳定性，具有良好的并行扩展性（对数级并行深度），且约简与合并步骤通过幺模变换保持了格结构完整性。

Abstract: Lattice basis reduction algorithms have various applications in computational number theory and lattice-based cryptography, but their complexity increases rapidly with the dimension. Motivated by the divide-and-conquer strategy of merge sort and incorporating PotLLL-style deep insertions during recombination, MergeLLL is proposed. In this framework, a lattice basis is split into sub-bases, local reductions are performed independently, and the full basis is reconstructed through hierarchical merging.
  The approach is focused on improving local lattice structure first before global basis properties are refined, resulting in enhanced Gram-Schmidt orthogonality and numerical stability, while overall computational cost is reduced. The method is naturally parallelizable, allowing efficient multicore and distributed execution. It is shown that the reduction and merging steps preserve the lattice structure through unimodular transformations and achieve logarithmic parallel depth. In experiments on subset-sum and NTRU-derived lattices, improvements over classical lattice reduction algorithms are demonstrated, including better orthogonality, a reduced number of expensive swap operations, and an improved Hermite factor, indicating higher-quality reduced bases.

</details>


### [137] [MIRROR: Novelty-Constrained Memory-Guided MCTS Red-Teaming for Agentic RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26793)
*Inderjeet Singh,Andrés Murillo,Motoyoshi Sekiya,Yuki Unno,Junichi Suga*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MIRROR是一个统一的跨攻击面红队框架，通过记忆引导的蒙特卡洛树搜索和显式新颖性约束，在文本、图像、直接查询和编排器四种攻击面上实现了比专用基线更优的攻击成功率（ASR）和更低的跨面方差，并发布了包含超过41,000条记录的多模态RAG安全基准ART-SafeBench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对多模态智能体RAG系统的红队攻击方法通常只针对单一攻击面（如文本投毒），且存在攻击模板高度重复的问题（文本投毒基准上73-84%的精确重复），缺乏统一的跨面框架来系统性地测试系统的安全性。

Method: 提出MIRROR框架，核心包括：(1) 记忆引导的蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS），利用检索上下文指导搜索先验；(2) 确定性新颖性门控（Novelty Gate），通过归一化比较拒绝与检索集合匹配的候选，防止提示复制；(3) 跨文本投毒、图像注入、直接查询和编排器工具操纵四种攻击面的统一设计。

Result: 在四种攻击面上：图像注入ASR达76%（基线52%），编排器攻击ASR达97%且查询成本减半，跨面变异系数最低（0.47）；专用基线跨面性能崩溃，如前缀优化在文本投毒上79%但在直接查询上仅1%。发布ART-SafeBench基准，包含41,815条包内记录及运行时适配器产生的41,991+条总记录。

Conclusion: MIRROR证明了统一跨面红队框架的有效性，通过新颖性约束和检索增强搜索能够在不同攻击面上实现高效且一致的攻击性能，揭示了专用方法的局限性。

Abstract: Multimodal agentic retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems expand the attack surface beyond prompt injection to include text poisoning, image injection, direct-query attacks, and orchestrator-level tool manipulation. Existing red-teaming approaches are typically surface-specific and often recycle known attack templates; on text-poisoning benchmarks we measure 73-84% exact duplication. We present MIRROR, a unified cross-surface framework that performs memory-guided Monte Carlo tree search while conditioning candidate generation on retrieved context under an explicit novelty constraint. A deterministic Novelty Gate rejects any candidate matching the retrieval set under normalized comparison, allowing retrieval to inform search priors without enabling prompt copying. Across four attack surfaces on a multimodal agentic RAG target, MIRROR attains 76% ASR on image poisoning compared with 52% for baselines, 97% ASR on orchestrator attacks at half the query cost, and the lowest cross-surface variance (coefficient of variation 0.47). In contrast, specialized baselines collapse across surfaces: suffix optimization reaches 79% ASR on text poisoning but 1% on direct queries. We release ART-SafeBench with 41,815 in-package records and runtime adapters yielding 41,991+ total records across four surfaces.

</details>


### [138] [SpikeTimer: Exploring Active Copyright Protection in Spiking Neural Networks via Temporal Backdoor Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26841)
*Xiao Yang,Gaolei Li,Jun Wu,Jianhua Li,Zhiquan Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SpikeTimer，一种基于时间背门学习的脉冲神经网络主动版权保护框架。通过将神经形态数据分区为时间片，仅在授权时间片内嵌入授权令牌，实现时间依赖的授权机制。在多个神经形态数据集上，SpikeTimer对未授权数据准确率约为10%，对授权数据仅降低约1.5%的性能，且能抵抗微调和剪枝攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SNN在能效计算领域具有巨大潜力，但其版权保护问题因时间编码复杂性和脉冲驱动计算特性而尚未充分研究，不同于传统DNN已有大量版权保护方案。

Method: 提出SpikeTimer框架，将神经形态数据划分为指定时间片，仅在授权时间片内嵌入授权令牌，利用SNN的时间分割特性支持多用户授权和任意形态令牌嵌入，建立时间依赖的授权机制。

Result: 在多个神经形态数据集上，SpikeTimer对未授权数据准确率约10%，对授权数据仅降低约1.5%性能，且在微调和剪枝攻击下具有鲁棒抵抗能力。

Conclusion: SpikeTimer通过时间背门学习有效保护SNN版权，在保持对授权数据高性能的同时对未授权数据产生错误响应，并在对抗性攻击下保持防御效力，填补了SNN版权保护的空白。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNN) have emerged as a revolutionary paradigm compared to traditional Deep Neural Networks (DNN) in energy-efficient computing, showcasing exceptional capabilities in processing event-driven sensory data for real-time applications like robotics and edge AI systems.
