Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一套面向人形机器人的、可在运行时编辑的本地化行为编排与运行系统。该系统遵循Coactive Design原则，结合了物本位的Affordance Templates、行为树逻辑以及运行时可编辑的感知机制，支持双臂行走、并发动作分层等能力。已在多种人形机器人上部署了20多种实际任务变体，实验表明可在数分钟到数小时内完成新行为的改编、扩展与组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人需要在人类空间中执行体力要求高、危险且重复的工作，但现有系统难以协调 locomotion、全身运动、感知、接触与操作员监督。因此需要一种可观察、可预测、可引导的行为编排与运行时系统。

Method: 系统采用Coactive Design原则设计操作界面，实现与机器人的持续同步以支持运行时编排、监控和修复。行为架构创新性地结合了物本位的Affordance Templates、行为树启发的组织逻辑，以及通过行为场景和原始场景动作实现的运行时可编辑感知。动作基元基于全身控制器（支持行走时移动双臂），并使用并发动作分层算法提升速度。

Result: 行为库涵盖了20多种真实机器人任务变体，包括推/拉各种门机构、多步探索、障碍物清理、反应式桌间操作等。系统已部署于多种人形机器人平台（Atlas、Valkyrie、Nadia、H1-2、Alex）。实验表明可在数分钟到数小时内完成现有行为的改编、扩展与组合，以创建新颖的locomotion-manipulation行为。

Conclusion: 所提出的系统在能力、速度、可靠性及行为创建/改编/扩展/组合速度方面均表现良好，验证了面向人形机器人的运行时可编辑行为编排方法的有效性。

Abstract: Humanoid robots could take on physically demanding, hazardous, and repetitive work in spaces built for humans. However, a useful robot for these spaces must coordinate locomotion, whole body motion, perception, contact, and operator supervision. This thesis presents a robot-local, runtime-editable behavior authoring and runtime system.
  Our system strives to be maximally observable, predictable, and directable following Coactive Design principles developed during the DARPA Robotics Challenge. Our operator interface remains continuously synchronized to the robot for runtime authoring, monitoring, and repair. Our behavior architecture uniquely combines object-centric Affordance Templates, organization and logic inspired by Behavior Trees, and runtime-editable perception through a behavior scene and primitive scene actions. Action primitives build on a whole-body controller that supports moving the arms while walking, and use a concurrent action layering algorithm for speed.
  The behavior library developed during this work covers more than twenty real-robot task variants, including push and pull doors with knob, push-bar, and lever-handle mechanisms, multi-step exploration sequences, obstacle clearing, and reactive table-to-table manipulation tasks. This behavior system has been deployed on many humanoid robots, such as Boston Dynamics' DRC Atlas, NASA's Valkyrie, IHMC and Boardwalk Robotics' Nadia, Unitree's H1-2, and IHMC's Alex.
  We evaluate our system across capability, speed, reliability, and speed of behavior creation, adaptation, extension, and combination. Our experiments demonstrate that we can adapt, extend, and combine existing behaviors to create novel loco-manipulation behaviors in minutes or hours. Videos: https://www.youtube.com/playlist?list=PLJK5CTyotYqsfgfnXb-09YNFeBose6uEY.

</details>


### [175] [Play2Perfect: What Matters in Dexterous Play Pretraining for Precise Assembly?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26428)
*Tyler Ga Wei Lum,Kushal Kedia,C. Karen Liu,Jeannette Bohg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Play2Prefect框架，通过任务无关的“玩耍”预训练获得可复用的操作先验（抓取、手中重定向、位姿到达），再微调用于高精度装配任务，相比从零训练的强化学习提升了33倍样本效率，并在真实机器人上实现了60%的紧配合插入成功率（间隙0.5mm）和50%以上的多部件装配与拧螺丝成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多指灵巧手在高精度装配任务（接触密集、奖励稀疏）上表现不佳；传统模仿学习难以收集接触丰富的演示数据，强化学习直接探索又不可行；已有的方法依赖专用夹爪、工具附件和环境固定装置来简化问题。

Method: 提出Play2Prefect框架：先通过任务无关的强化学习预训练（在多样化的物体和目标上“玩耍”）学习可复用的操作先验（抓取、手中重定向、位姿到达），再针对具体装配任务进行微调，将探索聚焦于最终的接触密集、高精度交互阶段。系统研究了物体多样性、训练目标、轨迹多样性和目标精度等关键设计选择。

Result: 预训练先验比从零训练的强化学习（即使提供密集多阶段奖励）样本效率高33倍；零样本从仿真到真实迁移，紧配合插入（0.5mm间隙）成功率达60%，长时序多部件装配和拧螺丝成功率超过50%。

Conclusion: “先学玩耍，再做精确装配”是一种有效的范式；通过多样化的玩耍预训练获得可复用的操作先验，再微调适应高精度任务，能够大幅提升样本效率并实现零样本sim-to-real迁移。

Abstract: Multi-fingered robots promise the speed and dexterity of human hands, yet challenging problems such as precise assembly have remained out of reach. These tasks are contact-rich, making data collection for imitation learning difficult, and sparse-reward, making direct exploration with reinforcement learning (RL) intractable. Consequently, prior work has made progress by structuring the problem with specialized grippers, tool attachments, and environment fixtures. In this work, we argue that before a robot can perfect precise assembly, it must first learn to play. We further ask the question: what factors in the process of learning to play matter for precise assembly? We propose Play2Perfect, an RL framework for task-agnostic pretraining through play on diverse objects and goals, which is then perfected on precise assembly. The goal of play is to acquire reusable manipulation priors, such as grasping, in-hand reorientation and pose reaching. Finetuning then adapts this general prior to assembly, focusing exploration on the final contact-rich, high-precision interactions needed for success. We systematically study key design choices in play pretraining, including object diversity, training objective, trajectory diversity, and goal precision. We show that our prior is 33x more sample-efficient than RL training from scratch, even when provided with dense, multi-stage rewards. We demonstrate zero-shot sim-to-real transfer, achieving 60% success on tight insertions with only 0.5 mm contact clearance, and over 50% success on long-horizon multi-part assembly and screwing.

</details>


### [176] [WatchAct: A Benchmark for Behavior-Grounded Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26443)
*Baiqi Li,Ce Zhang,Yu Fang,Yue Yang,Shangzhe Li,Mingyu Ding,Gedas Bertasius*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WatchAct是一个基于观察人类行为进行机器人操作的基准测试，包含3,000个长时域实例，覆盖事件解析、程序推理、隐式意图推理和情节推理四个认知领域。实验表明，当前最优系统（Gemini-3.1-Pro + π₀.₅）在仿真中仅达16.3%成功率，真实机器人上仅14.0%，远未解决该基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作基准仅将指令与单张当前图像配对，无法评估机器人对观察到的人类行为进行推理的能力。机器人需要理解人类做了什么、顺序如何以及意图是什么，而视频包含了语言所忽略的空间布局、物体历史和手势信息。

Method: 构建WatchAct基准：将真实世界人类动作视频与语言指令配对，对齐模拟器场景和可执行的LIBERO任务。设计解耦评估协议：分别测量视频到规划的推理（VLM）、oracle规划下的策略执行、以及集成规划器-策略管线的完整任务完成。涵盖事件解析、程序推理、隐式意图推理和情节推理四个认知领域，共14个任务的3,000个实例。

Result: 最佳管线Gemini-3.1-Pro + π₀.₅在仿真中仅16.3%成功率，真实机器人上14.0%。Gemini-3.1-Pro规划成功率36.8%（人类97.1%），π₀.₅在oracle规划下任务成功率21.5%，域外场景降至10.6%。

Conclusion: 当前视觉-语言模型和机器人策略系统在基于观察人类行为进行推理的操作任务上表现远不及人类，WatchAct揭示了该领域亟待解决的挑战。

Abstract: A robot working alongside people must reason about what they have done, in what order, and with what intent. Video carries the spatial layouts, object histories, and gestures that language leaves underspecified, yet today's manipulation benchmarks pair an instruction with a single current image, offering no way to evaluate reasoning over observed human behavior. We introduce WatchAct, a benchmark for robot manipulation grounded in observed human behavior. Each instance pairs a real-world human-action video and a language instruction with an aligned simulator scene and an executable LIBERO task, enabling scalable and reproducible evaluation. WatchAct comprises 3,000 long-horizon instances across 14 tasks in four capability domains drawn from the cognitive demands of watching another agent: parsing events (Event Grounding), recovering procedural structure (Procedural Reasoning), inferring unstated intent (Implicit Intent Inference), and tracking how the scene was changed (Episodic Reasoning). We further propose a disentangled evaluation protocol that separately measures (i)~video-to-plan reasoning by vision-language models, (ii)~policy execution under oracle plans, and (iii)~full task completion by integrated planner--policy pipelines. In both simulation and on a Franka Research 3 robot, current systems remain far from solving WatchAct. The best pipeline, Gemini-3.1-Pro with $π_{0.5}$, reaches only 16.3% Success Rate (SR) in simulation and 14.0% on the real robot. Gemini-3.1-Pro attains just 36.8% Plan SR (vs. 97.1% for humans), while $π_{0.5}$ reaches only 21.5% Task SR under oracle plans and drops to 10.6% on out-of-domain scenarios. Dataset and code are available at https://baiqi-li.github.io/watchact_page/.

</details>


### [177] [OSC2Runner: OpenSCENARIO 2.x Compliant High-Fidelity AV Simulation in CARLA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26533)
*Thoshitha Gamage,Lasanthi Gamage*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OSC2Runner首次实现了OpenSCENARIO v2.x DSL到CARLA的原生映射，通过多遍转译器架构将场景编译为确定性行为树，解决了v2.x场景在传统仿真框架中执行的时空漂移、异步延迟和运动学突兀问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于场景的测试框架主要依赖ASAM OpenSCENARIO 1.x XML标准，缺乏对已成熟的v2.x DSL的原生执行支持。使用传统解释器评估v2.x逻辑会引入时空漂移、异步事件延迟和人为的运动学突兀，阻碍了v2.x标准的实际应用。

Method: 将OpenSCENARIO v2.x DSL映射到CARLA的过程形式化为一个编译流水线，通过多遍转译器架构实现：绕过静态轨迹回放，将类型安全的抽象语法树直接合成为动态确定性的行为树（py_trees），并原生映射到CARLA的原子API上。

Result: 在高并发对抗性案例研究中实现了逐时钟确定性、精确的空间触发评估和100.0毫秒的跨智能体黑板同步；运动学分析证明严格遵从连续环境边界。

Conclusion: 该架构将基于场景的测试从近似行为解释转变为数学上严格的执行，为协同仿真、硬件在环测试和自动化LLM驱动的生成流水线奠定了确定性后端基础。

Abstract: Scenario-Based Testing predominantly relies on the legacy ASAM OpenSCENARIO 1.x XML standard because existing continuous simulation frameworks lack native execution support for the recently matured v2.x Domain-Specific Language (DSL). Adapting legacy interpreters to evaluate v2.x logic introduces spatiotemporal drift, asynchronous event latencies, and artificial kinematic snapping. Addressing this execution gap, OSC2Runner introduces the first orchestration framework capable of natively mapping the OpenSCENARIO v2.x DSL to CARLA. The framework achieves this by formalizing scenario translation as a compilation pipeline through a multi-pass transpiler architecture. Bypassing static trajectory playback, the architecture synthesizes type-safe Abstract Syntax Trees directly into dynamic deterministic behavior trees (py_trees) natively mapped to CARLA's atomic APIs. Empirical validation in highly concurrent adversarial case studies demonstrates tick-by-tick determinism, exact spatial trigger evaluation, and 100.0 ms cross-actor blackboard synchronization. Kinematic analysis proves the strict adherence to continuous environmental boundaries. This architecture transitions Scenario-Based Testing from approximate behavioral interpretation to mathematically rigorous execution, establishing the deterministic backend required for co-simulation, hardware-in-the-loop testing, and automated LLM-driven generation pipelines.

</details>


### [178] [IDEA: Insensitive to Dynamics Mismatch via Effect Alignment for Sim-to-Real Transfer in Multi-Agent Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26575)
*Chenlong Liu,Zhuohui Zhang,Xinyan Chen,Zhipeng Wang,Bin Cheng,Bin He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种通过效果对齐（effect alignment）实现动力学失配不敏感的 sim-to-real 多智能体控制方法，结合随机环境结构、离散语义动作和动作同步机制，在多个导航任务中显著提升了训练效率和真实场景成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的方法在多智能体控制中因依赖精确动力学建模或系统辨识，在低层控制空间学习策略对动力学失配高度敏感，导致 sim-to-real 迁移成本高、脆弱性大。

Method: 1) 将随机环境结构与离散语义动作通过闭环控制结合，将策略学习提升至语义抽象层面；2) 开发动作同步机制，缓解智能体间动作时序不匹配，增强系统时间一致性。

Result: 在四个多智能体导航任务上，该方法相比主流迁移方法显著提升训练效率，并在真实场景中取得更高成功率。

Conclusion: 通过效果对齐和语义抽象，能够有效提升多智能体系统在动力学失配下的鲁棒性和部署稳定性。

Abstract: Complex multi-agent control tasks remain challenging for traditional rule-based and model-based approaches, motivating the adoption of learning-based methods. However, learning-based methods often struggle with sim-to-real transfer because they rely on accurate dynamics modeling or system identification and learn policies in low-level control spaces that are highly sensitive to dynamics mismatch, making them costly and fragile in complex environments. To address this issue, we propose a sim-to-real method for multi-agent control, which is insensitive to dynamics mismatch via effect alignment. Our method combines random environmental structure with discrete semantic actions through closed-loop control, elevating policy learning to a semantic abstraction level. Additionally, we develop an action synchronization mechanism that mitigates inter-agent action timing mismatches, thereby enhancing the temporal consistency of the system. Experiments on four multi-agent navigation tasks demonstrate that our method substantially improves training efficiency over mainstream transfer methods and achieves higher success rates in real-world scenarios, thereby improving the robustness and deployment stability of multi-agent systems under dynamics mismatch.

</details>


### [179] [Inference-Time Robot Behavior Steering through Physically-Aware Reconfiguration of Task-Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26588)
*Yiyuan Pan,Hanjiang Hu,Shangtao Li,Xusheng Luo,Changliu Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReStruct提出了一种基于神经自动机策略的方法，通过将高层任务结构的状态机骨架与低层残差控制器解耦，利用同步乘积将用户偏好注入骨架中，在不重新训练控制器的情况下实现推理时的行为引导，在任务成功率和偏好遵循上超越VLA模型达25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习方法在推理时无法灵活引导/重定向已学策略以满足训练时未预见的用户偏好：端到端方法需要微调或专家级引导，而神经符号方法依赖预定义符号，其编辑可能导致逻辑合理但物理不可行的规划。

Method: ReStruct基于神经自动机策略，将视觉运动策略分解为高层状态机骨架（捕获任务结构）和低层连续控制器（残差策略）。通过同步乘积将用户偏好表示为自动机并融入骨架中，重新配置任务结构；保持控制器冻结，更新骨架提供的动作先验，实现修改后任务结构下的物理感知控制。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界的广泛实验中，ReStruct能够引导从面向对象的规格到时序逻辑约束等多种偏好，在任务成功率和偏好遵循上超越现有方法，比VLA模型高出25%。

Conclusion: ReStruct通过神经自动机策略的分解与同步乘积机制，实现了无需重新训练即可在推理时灵活注入用户偏好，兼顾了任务结构的逻辑可编辑性与底层控制的物理可行性，显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: A central challenge in deploying learned robot policies is inference-time behavior steering: redirecting a policy at test time to satisfy user preferences not anticipated during training, without retraining. Existing methods fail in two modes: end-to-end methods require fine-tuning or expert-level guidance, while neuro-symbolic methods rely on predefined symbols whose edits can result in logically reasonable but physically infeasible plans. To address this challenge, we propose ReStruct, which builds upon a neural automaton policy that decomposes a visuomotor policy into a high-level state-machine skeleton capturing task structure and a low-level continuous controller represented as a residual policy. Specifically, ReStruct adopts the automaton to represent the preference and incorporates it into the skeleton through a synchronous product, thereby reconfiguring the task structure. With the controller kept frozen, the action priors provided by the skeleton are updated accordingly to enable physically-aware control under a modified task structure. Extensive experiments from simulation and real-world show that ReStruct steers a wide range of preferences, from object-centric specifications to temporal-logic constraints, and after steering surpasses existing methods, exceeding VLA models in both task success and preference-following by up to 25%.

</details>


### [180] [Bridging Handheld and Teleoperated Supervision for Contact-Rich Manipulation via State-Gated Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26603)
*Vidullan Surendran,Neehar Peri,David Watkins*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出BRIDGE方法，通过混合手持数据与少量遥操作数据，并利用门控专家混合策略在接触敏感阶段使用期望动作，显著提升接触式操作任务的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手持数据采集系统（如UMI）虽能大规模收集数据但仅捕获观测动作，而遥操作虽能直接获取期望动作但耗时巨大。作者希望找到一种高效混合策略，在保留手持数据规模化优势的同时，利用少量遥操作数据解决接触敏感阶段的可行性问题。

Method: 提出BRIDGE（Bi-modal Routing for Imitation Data via Gated Experts），一种门控扩散策略专家混合方法，根据当前机器人状态在不同任务阶段头之间路由，使接触敏感阶段能使用遥操作的期望动作，自由空间阶段使用手持观测动作。

Result: 在三个接触式操作任务上，BRIDGE相比纯手持基线成功率提升高达36.7%。

Conclusion: 混合手持数据与少量有针对性的遥操作演示是一种高效策略；直接混合两阶段数据效果反而更差，而BRIDGE通过门控专家机制解决了观测与期望动作之间的不匹配问题。

Abstract: Handheld data collection systems, such as the Universal Manipulation Interface (UMI), enable scalable data collection across diverse environments but only capture observed actions rather than the desired actions executed by a robot controller. In contrast, teleoperation captures desired actions directly, but is prohibitively time-consuming to collect. We revisit this trade-off through the lens of action validity across task phases. We observe that handheld trajectories provide valid supervision in tolerant, free-space phases, but lack dynamic feasibility in contact-sensitive phases, where tracking observed trajectories at high stiffness produces large, unsafe contact forces. We study the interaction between these two supervision types for contact-rich manipulation and find that training policies that combine handheld data with a small number of targeted teleoperated demonstrations provide an efficient hybrid strategy. Specifically, rather than teleoperating the entire task, we only collect partial teleoperated demonstrations for task segments where base handheld policies fail. However, naively mixing handheld and teleoperated phase-specific data yields worse performance than training on handheld data alone. To address this mismatch between observed and desired supervision, we propose Bi-modal Routing for Imitation Data via Gated Experts (BRIDGE), a mixture of diffusion policy experts that routes between specialist task phase heads conditioned on the current robot state. Notably, our approach enables task-phase specific use of desired actions during contact sensitive segments and improves success rates over handheld-only baselines by up to 36.7% across three contact-rich manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [181] [A Closed-Form 4-DoF Inter-Robot Pose Estimator using Bearing-only Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26616)
*Qixin De,Ao Zhuang,Yechen Zhang,Zhuozhou Qian,Danping Zou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对基于轴承-里程计的6自由度协同定位中观测性退化问题，提出了一种4自由度闭式相对位姿估计器，通过松弛非线性旋转约束和误差投影实现高效估计，并引入观测性测试模块自适应确定最优估计时刻。仿真与实验表明该方法在低计算成本下实现高精度估计，且观测性测试模块保证了估计可靠性并缩短数据采集间隔。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有6自由度协同定位方法在特定运动模式下存在观测性退化问题，难以快速获得准确且可靠的机器人间位姿估计。

Method: 提出闭式4自由度相对位姿估计器（松弛非线性旋转约束+误差投影用于平移估计）；进行系统观测性理论分析，识别共线队形和保形队形两种退化模式；引入观测性测试模块自主确定最优估计时刻。

Result: 仿真与实机实验表明：所提算法在显著降低计算成本的同时实现更高估计精度；观测性测试模块保证了估计可靠性并最小化数据采集间隔。

Conclusion: 所提4自由度系统相比6自由度系统对运动激励的要求更低，能在更广泛的协同运动模式下实现可靠估计，且无需预定义固定长度滑动窗口。

Abstract: Bearing-odometry-based cooperative localization has attracted increasing research interest due to its minimal infrastructure requirements, low communication bandwidth and broad applicability in complex environments. However, existing 6-DoF approaches still face challenges in rapidly obtaining accurate and reliable inter-robot pose estimation, as the system is prone to observability degeneracy under specific motion patterns. To address these issues, we first propose a closed-form 4-DoF inter-robot pose estimator, which relaxes nonlinear constraints for rotations estimation and employs error projection for translations estimation. We then conduct a theoretical analysis of the system's observability, identifying degeneracy under two typical motion patterns: collinear and shape-preserving formations. The analysis further shows that the proposed 4-DoF system requires less stringent motion excitation for observability, enabling reliable estimation under a broader range of cooperative maneuvers. Furthermore, an observability test module is introduced to autonomously determine the optimal estimation instant, eliminating reliance on a predefined fixed-length sliding window. Extensive simulations and real-world experiments demonstrate that the proposed algorithm achieves higher estimation accuracy with significantly low computational cost, and the observability test module ensures estimation reliability while minimizing the data collection interval.

</details>


### [182] [Hardware Design for Table Tennis Robot Capable of Beating Professional Players](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26643)
*Nobuhiko Mukai,Pavel Adodin,Stefan Heusser,Alexander Sigrist,Divij Grover,Guillem Torrente,Farshad Khadivar,Takekazu Kakinuma,Peter Dürr*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文定义了击败专业选手的乒乓球机器人硬件规格，开发了8自由度机器人"Ace"，通过拓扑优化减轻质量、逆动力学优化驱动选型，并集成低阶动力学模型与强化学习控制策略。实验表明机器人可实现0.8秒全行程挥拍、峰值拍速22 m/s，并成功击败多名专业选手。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有乒乓球机器人无法击败专业选手，核心瓶颈在于硬件性能不足。本文旨在明确击败专业选手所需的硬件规格，并据此设计实现该目标的机器人系统。

Method: 1) 分析精英运动员运动数据，定义工作空间、负载、抗外力、物理性能、发球能力和末端精度等目标规格；2) 开发8自由度机器人"Ace"，采用拓扑优化减重保刚度，基于逆动力学扭矩模型优化电机与减速器选型；3) 建立含延迟补偿的低阶单关节动力学模型并集成到仿真中，使能强化学习控制策略。

Result: 机器人实现0.8秒全行程挥拍周期，拍心峰值速度22 m/s，并成功在实战中击败多名职业乒乓球选手。

Conclusion: 通过系统化的硬件规格定义与优化设计，所开发的"Ace"机器人具备击败专业乒乓球选手的能力，验证了硬件性能是突破机器人运动能力上限的关键因素。

Abstract: This paper focuses on the hardware specifications required for a table tennis robot to beat professional players. After analyzing the motions of elite players, we defined target specifications for the workspace, payload, external-force resistance, physical performance, serve capability, and end-effector accuracy. Based on these specifications, we developed "Ace", a custom 8-DoF robot. The mechanical structure was improved through topology optimization to minimize mass while preserving stiffness. Motor and gearbox selection was optimized using an inverse-dynamics torque model. Low-order per-joint dynamics models with delay compensation were identified and integrated into simulation to enable the use of an RL control policy. Experiments demonstrated repeated full-stroke swings with a cycle time of 0.8 s and a peak racket-center velocity of 22 m/s. The robot successfully defeated multiple professional players.

</details>


### [183] [LAMP: Lane-Aligned Motion Primitives for Feasible Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26661)
*Sangjin Han,Hoseong Jung,Jeongtae Her,Changhyun Choi,H. Jin Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LAMP框架，通过VQ-VAE学习与车道拓扑对齐的运动基元作为离散意图查询，结合可行性感知意图选择器，在保持预测精度的同时显著提升轨迹的拓扑一致性和多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹预测方法过度关注最小化位移误差，忽略了多模态预测对车道拓扑的遵从性，导致低概率模态的预测轨迹可能违反物理和逻辑约束，无法可靠用于安全关键规划。

Method: 1) 使用VQ-VAE学习形状感知的运动基元作为离散意图查询，捕获超越端点意图的时空模式；2) 引入训练了车道拓扑先验的可行性感知意图选择器，过滤不可达意图查询，引导解码器优先选择拓扑一致的意图同时保持行为多样性。

Result: 在Argoverse 2数据集上，LAMP在预测精度上达到与SOTA基线相当的水平，同时在可行性和多样性指标上显著超越现有方法。

Conclusion: 拓扑感知的预测框架能够在保证精度的前提下，显著提升轨迹的物理可行性和逻辑合理性，为安全关键的自驾驶规划提供更可靠的预测集。

Abstract: Motion forecasting is essential for autonomous driving systems to enable safe decision-making and planning in complex driving scenarios. While existing predictors excel at minimizing standard displacement errors, they often overlook the adherence to lane topology of multimodal predictions, particularly for lower-probability modes. Consequently, predicted trajectories may violate physical and logical constraints, making the prediction set unreliable for safety-critical planning. In this paper, we propose LAMP (Lane-Aligned Motion Primitives), a topology-aware forecasting framework that anchors multimodal prediction to structured motion primitives aligned with lane topology. Specifically, we use a VQ-VAE to learn shape-aware motion primitives as discrete intention queries, capturing spatiotemporal patterns beyond endpoint-based intentions. We further introduce a feasibility-aware intention selector trained with a lane-topology prior for filtering unreachable intention queries, guiding the decoder to prioritize topology-consistent intentions while preserving behavioral diversity. Extensive experiments on the Argoverse 2 dataset demonstrate that LAMP achieves prediction accuracy comparable to state-of-the-art baselines while outperforming them in feasibility and diversity metrics.

</details>


### [184] [Tactile-WAM: Touch-Aware World Action Model with Tactile Asymmetric Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26663)
*Siyu Wu,Linjing You,Junjie Zhu,Yaozu Liu,Changhao Zhang,Jian Liu,Weiqiang Wang,Qi Li,Jituo Li,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Tactile-WAM，通过非对称触觉注意力机制(TAAM)解决触觉污染问题，在接触密集型操作任务中将平均成功率提升38.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在接触密集型操作中，仅依赖视觉预测未来状态存在物理不完整性（如滑动、卡住、接触法线等难以通过RGB捕捉），而直接注入触觉token会导致"触觉污染"——未受约束的触觉token注入会迫使视觉动力学模型吸收稀疏、局部的触觉信号，从而降低视频和动作预测质量。

Method: 提出Tactile-WAM框架，核心是触觉非对称注意力机制(TAAM)，包含两个组件：(1) VideoClean掩码——阻止视频查询访问触觉键/值token，同时保留动作查询的访问权限；(2) 触觉感知偏置——基于预测的触觉变化调制去噪过程中动作注意力对触觉token的关注。

Result: 在ManiFeel基准上，Tactile-WAM整体平均成功率提升38.9%，在接触密集型任务上提升86%。

Conclusion: 非对称的触觉-视觉信息融合策略能有效避免触觉污染，在保护视觉预测质量的同时利用触觉信息提升动作生成性能，尤其适用于接触密集的机器人操作场景。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) generate actions together with predicted futures, offering a powerful interface for robot decision making. In contact-rich manipulation, however, visually plausible futures can be physically incomplete: insertion, assembly, search, and reorientation often depend on slip, jamming, contact normals, or small alignment errors that are weakly visible or hidden in RGB. A natural solution is to predict future tactile states, however, we identify tactile pollution, a failure mode where unconstrained tactile-token injection degrades video and action prediction by forcing a visual dynamics model to absorb sparse, local, event-driven contact signals. To address this, we propose Tactile-WAM, a touch-aware WAM with a Tactile Asymmetric Attention Mechanism (TAAM). TAAM combines a VideoClean mask, which blocks video-query access to tactile key/value tokens while preserving action-query access, with a touch-aware bias for action attention. The VideoClean mask protects visual prediction while keeping contact information available for action generation; the touch-aware bias is derived from predicted touch changes and modulates action attention to tactile tokens during denoising. On ManiFeel, Tactile-WAM improves the mean success rate by 38.9% overall and by 86% on contact-rich tasks.

</details>


### [185] [Learning Motion Feasibility from Point Clouds in Cluttered Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26700)
*Sajid Ansari,Arthi,Girish Varma,Antony Thomas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出首个大规模运动可行性预测基准数据集（270万抓取可行性标签，覆盖88个物体和190个杂乱场景），并系统比较了MLP、体素CNN和点云Transformer三类架构，其中点云Transformer模型GRASPFC-PTX在新型物体上AUROC达0.996，预测速度显著快于基于采样的运动规划器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于采样的运动规划器在杂乱环境中进行运动可行性判定时计算开销大，现有不可行性认证方法局限于低维构型空间且依赖已知参数的简化几何环境。因此需要直接从原始RGB-D观测中高效预测运动可行性。

Method: 构建包含270万抓取可行性标签的大规模基准数据集，覆盖88个扫描物体和190个杂乱桌面场景；在匹配的训练条件下系统比较三类分类器架构：MLP、体素CNN和基于点云的Transformer；其中最佳模型GRASPFC-PTX采用点云Transformer架构。

Result: 最佳模型GRASPFC-PTX在新型物体上AUROC达到0.996，预测速度显著快于基于采样的运动规划器。

Conclusion: 基于点云Transformer的方法能够从原始RGB-D观测中高效准确地预测7自由度机械臂在杂乱场景中的运动可行性，为替代昂贵的基于采样的运动规划提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Motion feasibility prediction plays a central role in robotics, particularly in task and motion planning and manipulation. A major bottleneck for this problem in cluttered environments is that infeasible planning attempts by Sampling-based motion planners (SBMPs) can incur substantial computational cost. Also existing approaches for infeasibility certification are limited to low-dimensional configuration spaces and often assume simplified geometric environments represented by primitive objects with known parameters. We study the complementary problem of learning motion feasibility prediction directly from raw RGB-D observations for a 7-DOF manipulator operating in realistic cluttered scenes. We introduce the first large-scale benchmark for this setting, comprising 2.7M grasp feasibility labels over 88 scanned objects and 190 cluttered tabletop scenes. We benchmark three representative classifier families spanning MLP- based, volumetric-CNN, and point-cloud-based Transformer architectures under matched training conditions. Our best model, GRASPFC-PTX (a point-cloud transformer), achieves an AUROC of 0.996 on Novel objects while providing predictions significantly faster than SBMPs.

</details>


### [186] [PressMimic: Pressure-Guided Motion Capture and Control for Humanoid Robot Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26741)
*Yi Lu,Shenghao Ren,Tianyu Xiong,Zhaoxiang Li,Jiaqi Li,He Zhang,Tao Yu,Qiu Shen,Xun Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PressMimic 是一个将压力感知作为统一模态集成到从动作捕捉到人形机器人控制全流程的框架，通过 FRAPPE++ 多模态模型融合 RGB 和压力数据估计3D姿态和全局运动，以及压力监督策略（PSP）用于强化学习控制，有效解决了现有视觉驱动方法中的脚滑、地板穿透等物理不一致问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人形运动模仿主要依赖视觉动作捕捉和运动学模仿，忽略了接触动力学，导致脚滑、地板穿透和不稳定行为等伪影。作者希望从物理接地（physical grounding）的角度重新审视人形运动模仿，将压力作为连接感知和控制的统一模态。

Method: 提出 PressMimic 框架，包含两部分：1）感知阶段：FRAPPE++ 多模态模型融合 RGB 和压力数据联合估计3D姿态和全局运动，压力提供接触和支持约束以解决视觉估计的模糊性；2）控制阶段：压力监督策略（PSP）将压力信号引入强化学习，实现物理一致接触模式。同时构建了 MotionPRO 大规模数据集，包含同步 RGB、压力和动作捕捉数据。

Result: 实验表明压力感知能够提高运动估计精度、轨迹一致性和执行稳定性，验证了压力作为物理接地信号在连接感知与控制方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 压力是一种有效的物理接地信号，能够桥接感知与控制，实现物理一致的人形运动模仿。

Abstract: Humanoid motion imitation requires not only accurate perception of human kinematics but also faithful reproduction of physical interactions with the environment. However, existing pipelines rely primarily on vision-based motion capture and kinematic imitation, largely ignoring contact dynamics, leading to artifacts such as foot sliding, floor penetration, and unstable behaviors. In this work, we revisit humanoid motion imitation from the perspective of physical grounding and leverage pressure as a unified modality across perception and control. We present PressMimic, a framework that integrates pressure into the full pipeline from motion capture to humanoid control. In the perception stage, we introduce FRAPPE++, a multimodal model that fuses RGB and pressure to jointly estimate 3D pose and global motion, where pressure provides explicit contact and support constraints to resolve ambiguity in vision-based estimation. In the control stage, we propose a pressure-supervised policy (PSP) that incorporates pressure-derived signals into reinforcement learning, enabling physically consistent contact patterns during execution. We further construct MotionPRO, a large-scale dataset with synchronized RGB, pressure, and motion capture data. Experiments show that pressure improves motion estimation accuracy, trajectory consistency, and execution stability. These results demonstrate that pressure serves as an effective physical grounding signal, bridging perception and control for physically consistent humanoid motion imitation.

</details>


### [187] [SSI-Policy: Learning Structured Scene Interfaces for Vision-Language Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26800)
*Kaijun Wang,Zikai Ouyang,Xuping Wu,Jinyi Hong,Wei Pan,Haibo Lu,Jia Pan,Wei Zhang,Linfang Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SSI-Policy提出了一种基于结构化场景接口(SSI)的模块化框架，通过统一RGB-only中间表示（联合单目深度特征、语言锚定对象布局和指令条件化2D运动轨迹），实现从少量演示中学习空间感知、任务推理和精确控制的机器人操作策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实机器人操作需要空间定位、任务感知推理和精确控制，但在低数据场景下学习这些能力极具挑战。现有方法在可扩展任务级推理与显式物理结构之间存在权衡：基于视频的方法在长程任务中会产生几何漂移，3D方法通常需要深度传感器，而流/轨迹接口强调运动却缺乏明确的RGB-only几何表示。

Method: 提出SSI-Policy，一个以结构化场景接口(SSI)为核心的模块化框架。SSI是统一、机器人无关的RGB-only中间表示，联合编码单目深度特征、语言锚定对象布局和指令条件化2D运动轨迹。该接口可从无动作视频中训练，将感知与控制解耦，使下游策略能够从少量演示中学习。

Result: 在LIBERO基准上仅用每个任务10个演示，SSI-Policy比最强先前方法提升近15%，且与使用大规模外部预训练的50演示方法竞争。在13个真实世界任务（空间推理、跨本体迁移、接触丰富操作）上进一步验证有效性。

Conclusion: 消融实验表明几何线索和运动线索在共享接口内提供互补优势。SSI-Policy通过解耦感知与控制、利用RGB-only统一表示，在低数据场景下实现了显著性能提升，验证了结构化中间表示对机器人操作学习的有效性。

Abstract: Real-world robotic manipulation demands spatial grounding, task-aware reasoning, and precise control. Learning such capabilities becomes particularly challenging in the low-data regime. Prior methods often trade off scalable task-level reasoning and explicit physical structure: video-based approaches can drift geometrically over long horizons, 3D approaches often require depth sensing, and many flow/trajectory interfaces emphasize motion without an explicit RGB-only geometric representation. We introduce SSI-Policy, a modular framework built around a Structured Scene Interface (SSI) -- a unified, RGB-only intermediate representation that jointly encodes monocular depth features, language-grounded object layouts, and instruction-conditioned 2D motion trajectories. Critically, SSI is robot-agnostic and trainable from action-free video, decoupling perception from control so that the downstream policy can learn from few demonstrations. On the LIBERO benchmark with only 10 demonstrations per task, SSI-Policy improves over the strongest prior method by nearly 15\% and remains competitive with 50-demo methods that leverage large-scale external pretraining. Ablations show that geometric and motion cues provide complementary benefits within the shared interface. We further validate on 13 real-world tasks spanning spatial reasoning, cross-embodiment transfer, and contact-rich manipulation.

</details>


### [188] [Improving Vision-Language-Action Model Fine-Tuning with Structured Stage and Keyframe Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26801)
*Yuan Xu,Yixiang Chen,Kai Wang,Jiabing Yang,Peiyan Li,Qisen Ma,Yan Huang,Liang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出StaKe，一种即插即用的辅助监督框架，通过动作阶段分类器和关键帧预测器两个轻量辅助头，在不改变基础VLA策略架构和推理流程的前提下，为机器人操作微调提供结构化监督，有效提升长时域操作任务的成功率（仿真双臂任务提升14%，单臂Franka真实任务提升56%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在微调时对所有时间步施加相同的动作监督，缺乏对操作阶段和夹爪事件目标的结构化指导，导致失败集中在夹爪事件转换的困难阶段。

Method: 从演示数据中的夹爪状态自动推导两个互补信号：阶段分类器识别当前操作阶段，关键帧预测器估计下一个夹爪转换的目标关节动作。两者作为轻量辅助头在训练时丰富表示学习，不改变推理过程。

Result: 在仿真双臂任务上相对提升14%，在单臂Franka真实任务上相对提升56%，长时域任务（更多夹爪事件）提升更显著。消融实验验证了各设计选择的有效性。

Conclusion: 结构化监督是增强VLA长时域操作微调的有效通用策略，能够自动从演示数据中学习操作阶段结构，提升模型的泛化能力和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown strong potential for generalizable robotic manipulation. During fine-tuning, however, action supervision applies equally across all timesteps, without structured supervision on which manipulation stage the robot is in or what the next gripper-event target should be. This causes failures to concentrate around challenging gripper-event transitions. To address this, we propose StaKe, a plug-in auxiliary supervision framework that automatically derives two complementary signals from demonstration gripper states without manual annotation: a stage classifier that identifies the current manipulation stage, and a keyframe predictor that estimates the target joint action at the next gripper transition. Both are modeled as lightweight auxiliary heads that enrich the learned representations during training, while leaving the base VLA policy architecture and inference loop unchanged. Experiments on bimanual simulation and single-arm Franka real-robot tasks show that StaKe consistently improves success rates (relative gains of 14% and 56%, respectively), with larger improvements on longer-horizon tasks that involve more gripper-event transitions. Ablation studies validate each design choice, and qualitative analysis confirms that the learned representations faithfully track manipulation stages. These results indicate that structured supervision is an effective and general strategy for enhancing VLA fine-tuning in long-horizon manipulation. Project website: https://hi-yuanxu.github.io/StaKe-Web/

</details>


### [189] [Ordinal Neural Collapse as a Representation Prior for Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26839)
*E-In Son,Jung-Taak Kim,Seung-Woo Seo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ORION方法，通过序数神经坍缩显式组织视觉编码器的表示空间，使导航动作类别按序排列，从而解决端到端模仿学习中编码器学习到模糊、动作无关表征的问题，在仿真和真实实验中显著提升导航成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端模仿学习中，视觉编码器和动作解码器仅通过单一动作损失联合优化，编码器只能接收间接监督信号，导致学习到模糊且与动作无关的表征。这一问题在场景结构变化大、视觉干扰物多的真实导航环境中尤为严重，使得导航策略在歧义决策点产生不一致动作，导致导航失败。

Method: ORION方法显式组织编码器的表示空间，使其符合导航动作的序数结构。具体地，利用目标导向导航中自我中心控制类别（从Far Left到Far Right）固有的序数关系，鼓励类别表征沿单一判别轴顺序排列，同时抑制每个类别的离轴方差。预训练编码器集成到基于扩散的导航框架中，进行端到端微调。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中的大量实验表明，ORION在导航成功率和目标进展上持续优于端到端和神经坍缩基线方法，在复杂多路交叉口等视觉挑战场景中尤其显著。

Conclusion: 通过显式引入序数结构约束来组织视觉表征空间，可以有效解决端到端模仿学习中编码器监督不足的问题，提升视觉导航策略的鲁棒性和一致性。

Abstract: Learning robust navigation policies directly from visual observations remains a fundamental challenge in vision-based robotic navigation. In end-to-end imitation learning approaches, the visual encoder and action decoder are jointly optimized using a single action loss, which provides only an indirect supervisory signal to the encoder. This indirect supervision frequently results in the encoder learning ambiguous, action-agnostic representations. The problem is further complicated by substantial variations in scene structure and appearance across diverse environments, as well as the prevalence of visual distractors inherent to real-world navigation settings. Such action-agnostic features cause the navigation policy to produce inconsistent actions at ambiguous decision points, leading to navigation failure. To overcome these limitations, we propose ORION (Ordinal Neural Collapse for Visual Navigation), a method that explicitly organizes the encoder's representation space according to the ordinal structure of navigation actions. In the context of goal-directed navigation, ego-centric control categories from Far Left to Far Right exhibit a natural ordinal relationship in which neighboring classes share similar visual contexts, while semantically opposing classes differ substantially in appearance. We encourage class representations to be arranged sequentially along a single discriminative axis, while suppressing off-axis variance within each class. The pretrained encoder is then integrated into a diffusion-based navigation framework, and the full pipeline is fine-tuned end-to-end. Extensive experiments in both simulation and real-world settings show that ORION consistently outperforms end-to-end and neural collapse baselines in navigation success rate and goal progress, with notable gains in visually challenging scenarios such as complex multi-way intersections.

</details>


### [190] [Humanoid-DART: Humanoid Loco-Manipulation using Diffusion-guided Augmentation through Relabeling and Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26855)
*Pranav Debbad,Kanish Thiagarajan,Victor Dhédin,Shafeef Omar,Majid Khadiv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自监督框架，从稀疏的人类 demonstrations 出发，结合扩散模型生成轨迹和强化学习跟踪，逐步扩展行为库，以最小专家监督学习人形机器人全身操控策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模仿学习方法依赖大量多样化 demonstrations 且需要持续人工干预来纠正策略失败，扩展性差且成本高。

Method: 结合扩散模型生成目标条件轨迹和强化学习跟踪轨迹，实现从稀疏 demonstrations 自举并逐步探索目标空间。

Result: 通过大量消融实验和与最先进方法的比较，证明了该框架在多种人形机器人全身操控技能上的有效性。

Conclusion: 该自监督框架能以最小专家监督有效学习人形机器人的 loco-manipulation 策略，具有良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Imitating human demonstrations has emerged as a dominant paradigm for learning humanoid loco-manipulation policies. However, scaling these approaches remains challenging due to the high cost of collecting diverse demonstrations and the need for continual human intervention to correct policy failures. In this paper, we present a self-supervised framework that bootstraps from sparse demonstrations and progressively expands its behavioral repertoire, enabling the learning of a goal-conditioned policy that automatically explores the goal space with minimal expert supervision. Our approach combines diffusion-based trajectory generation with reinforcement learning, where the latter is used to track goal-conditioned trajectories produced by the diffusion model for a range of loco-manipulation skills. Through extensive ablation studies and comparisons with state-of-the-art methods, we demonstrate the effectiveness of our framework on multiple humanoid loco-manipulation skills.

</details>


### [191] [PlanRL: A Trajectory Planning Architecture for Reinforcement Learning-based Driving Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26858)
*Joonhee Lim,Yongjae Lee,Jangho Shin,Dongsuk Kum*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种结合强化学习策略与多项式轨迹规划器的自动驾驶轨迹规划架构，通过Frenet坐标系简化道路几何，并加入运动学可行性检查，在CARLA基准测试中显著优于现有基于控制的RL专家方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的驾驶专家直接输出油门、转向等控制命令，缺乏可解释性、学习道路几何的空间复杂性高，且与现代端到端规划架构兼容性差。

Method: 1) 将RL策略与基于多项式的轨迹规划器集成；2) 采用Frenet坐标系将复杂道路几何简化为曲线框架，提供结构化坐标先验促进策略学习；3) 在规划阶段加入运动学可行性检查，确保生成轨迹在车辆物理极限内，缓解规划系统的累积跟踪误差。

Result: 在CARLA Offline Leaderboard v1上驾驶分数提升5%，成功率提升8%；在NoCrash基准上驾驶分数提升11%，成功率提升19%。

Conclusion: 提出的轨迹规划架构显著优于现有基于控制的RL专家方法，为RL驾驶专家提供了更具解释性、空间效率更高且与现代规划架构兼容的解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a prominent framework for developing driving experts in autonomous vehicles. However, most existing RL-based experts are designed to output direct control commands (e.g., throttle, steering), which suffer from a lack of interpretability, high spatial complexity in learning road geometries, and poor compatibility with modern end-to-end planning architectures. To address these limitations, we propose a novel trajectory planning architecture for RL driving experts that integrates an RL policy with a polynomial-based trajectory planner. By employing a Frenet-frame coordinate system, our method simplifies complex road geometries into a curvilinear framework, offering a structured coordinate prior that facilitates policy learning. Furthermore, we incorporate a kinematic feasibility check into the planning stage to ensure that generated trajectories remain within the vehicle's physical limits, effectively mitigating cumulative tracking errors typically found in planning-based systems. We evaluate our approach on key CARLA benchmarks, where it significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art control-based RL experts. On the CARLA Offline Leaderboard v1 and NoCrash benchmarks, our method improves the driving score by 5% and 11%, respectively, and increases the success rate by 8% and 19%.

</details>


### [192] [Risk-Aware Selective Multimodal Driver Monitoring with Driver-State World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26922)
*Daosheng Qiu,Haozhuang Chi,Hao Su,Shu Long,Xinyue Miao,Yongle Dong,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种成本感知的选择性推理框架，用于自动驾驶中的多模态驾驶员监控。核心系统结合轻量级RGB-生理学生网络与学习门控机制，在低延迟推理（3.08ms）下将不安全假阴性率从17.37%降至约5%，同时指出可靠监控需要风险感知的选择性控制与群体鲁棒校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶中需要持续低延迟的驾驶员监控，但大型视觉语言模型延迟高且可靠性有限，不适合作为常驻车载监控系统。现有轻量级模型在不确定状态下可能做出不安全决策。

Method: 提出成本感知选择性推理框架：（1）轻量级RGB-生理学生网络（融合车内视觉与心率/EDA信号）；（2）学习门控机制决定接受快速预测或放弃并触发安全干预；（3）驾驶员状态世界模型模块用于预测未来误差和反事实系统级行动成本。

Result: RGB-生理学生网络在驾驶员需求识别任务上达到0.7440 Macro-F1和0.9099平衡准确率，参数量11.39M，延迟3.08ms。成本感知选择性推理将不安全假阴性从17.37%降至约5%。

Conclusion: 可靠的边缘端驾驶员监控不仅需要改进感知骨干网络，还需要风险感知的选择性控制和群体鲁棒校准。当前生理同步精度有限，最差组评估显示存在操作点校准漂移。

Abstract: Continuous driver monitoring in automated vehicles requires low-latency inference while avoiding unsafe decisions under uncertain driver states. Large vision-language models provide broad multimodal priors, but their latency and limited reliability in this setting make them unsuitable as always-on in-cabin monitors. We propose a cost-aware selective inference framework for deployable multimodal driver monitoring. The core system is a lightweight RGB-physiological student that combines in-cabin visual observations with window-level HR/EDA signals, and a learned gate that decides when to accept the fast prediction or abstain for safety intervention. Additional controls show that the learned scores contain sample-level information beyond scenario priors, while exact physiological synchronization remains a limitation. To incorporate predictive evidence, we further study a compact driver-state world modeling module that rolls out latent driver-state features and estimates future fast-model errors and counterfactual system-level action costs. On scenario-induced driver-demand recognition, the RGB-physiological student improves over RGB-only and physiology-only baselines, reaching 0.7440 Macro-F1 and 0.9099 balanced accuracy with 11.39M parameters and 3.08ms inference latency. Cost-aware selective inference reduces unsafe false negatives from 17.37% under always-fast inference to approximately 5% across seeds, while maintaining deployment-level latency. While driver-state world modeling offers valuable predictive signals, worst-group evaluations highlight persistent operating-point calibration drift. Ultimately, reliable edge driver monitoring requires advancing not only perception backbones, but also risk-aware selective control and group-robust calibration.

</details>


### [193] [UAV-MapFusion: RTK-Aligned Uncertainty-Aware Coarse-to-Fine Multi-Session UAV Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26928)
*Feng Pan,Chunran Zheng,Bing Xue,Yukang Cui,Jiayu Wen,Zhiyu Chen,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种不确定性感知的多会话点云地图合并与粗到精优化系统，通过场景图初始化合并、RTK时空对齐（DTW时间偏移估计+MOGP连续约束恢复）以及不确定性感知因子图与平面因子迭代优化，解决了无人机大规模点云建图中的长距离漂移与局部几何精度保持问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机因续航和存储限制难以单次飞行完成大规模场景建图，现有跨会话地图合并方法在无人机场景中无法同时抑制长距离漂移和保持局部几何精度。

Method: 1) 基于场景图的多会话点云地图初始合并；2) RTK时空对齐模块，使用DTW估计时间偏移，MOGP恢复不完整采样和帧丢失下的连续RTK约束；3) 构建统一的不确定性感知因子图；4) 通过迭代平面因子优化提升局部几何精度。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上验证了所提方法的有效性和鲁棒性，代码与数据集将公开发布。

Conclusion: 所提的不确定性感知多会话点云地图合并与粗到精优化系统能够有效解决无人机大规模建图场景下的长距离漂移与局部几何精度难以兼顾的问题。

Abstract: Large-scale point cloud maps are essential for robotics and spatial intelligence tasks. UAVs provide an efficient means for large-scale map acquisition; however, due to limited flight endurance and onboard storage, mapping a large-scale scene within a single flight remains difficult. Existing multi-session map merging methods can extend the mapping range, yet in UAV scenarios they still struggle to simultaneously suppress long-range drift and preserve local geometric accuracy. To address this issue, an uncertainty-aware multi-session point cloud map merging and coarse-to-fine optimization system is proposed. The proposed method first performs initial multi-session map merging based on a scene graph, and then incorporates RTK observations through an RTK spatiotemporal alignment module, where temporal offsets are estimated using Dynamic Time Warping (DTW), and continuous RTK constraints are recovered using Multi-Output Gaussian Processes (MOGP) under incomplete sampling and frame dropouts. On this basis, a unified uncertainty-aware factor graph is constructed, and local geometric accuracy is further improved through iterative plane-factor refinement. Experiments on real-world datasets validate the effectiveness and robustness of the proposed method. To facilitate further research and development in the community, our code and dataset will be publicly released.

</details>


### [194] [RobOralScan: Learning Active Intraoral Scanning for Robotic Dental Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26955)
*Jinhyung Lee,Haeun Yun,Siwon Kim,Gihyun Baek,Sungho Moon,Sehyun Hwang,Sunghoon Im*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RobOralScan，首个基于强化学习的机器人自动口内扫描流程，通过几何记忆观测空间和牙齿级覆盖学习，实现高覆盖、高质量的全牙弓扫描，并在0-shot sim-to-real实验中验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 口内扫描在口腔狭小空间中依赖人工操作，全牙弓和长跨度扫描工作量大、自动化程度低，重建质量易受缺失牙面影响且操作者负担重，亟需自动化解决方案。

Method: 1) 基于强化学习（RL）的自动扫描流程；2) 几何记忆观测空间：将累积的部分扫描观测编码为三态几何表示，使策略能推理扫描历史和未充分观测区域；3) 牙齿级覆盖学习：结合覆盖感知奖励信号和渐进式训练方案，提升全局重建覆盖率并减少单颗牙齿间的覆盖不均；4) 策略从累积几何记忆和机器人本体感知中选择相对扫描器运动，实现闭环控制。

Result: Chamfer距离0.00838，平均覆盖率92.58%，单颗牙齿低尾覆盖率88.45%，归一化AUC 0.6674，10次评估中8次完成扫描标准；0-shot sim-to-real实验验证了物理机器人-扫描仪系统的实际可行性。

Conclusion: RobOralScan首次证明了基于RL的自动化口内扫描在重建质量和覆盖率上的有效性，且具备从仿真到真实物理系统的零样本迁移能力，为口内扫描自动化提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Intraoral scanning is widely used for digital optical impressions in prosthodontic, implant, and orthodontic treatment, but full-arch and long-span scanning remain labor-intensive tasks with limited automation. In the confined oral cavity, operators must continuously adjust scanner motion while accumulating narrow field-of-view observations, making reconstruction quality sensitive to missing tooth surfaces and operator workload.
  We propose RobOralScan, which, to the best of our knowledge, is the first reinforcement learning (RL)-based pipeline for robotic automatic intraoral scanning. RobOralScan introduces a geometric memory-based observation space that accumulates partial scan observations into a tri-state geometric representation, allowing the policy to reason over scan history and insufficiently observed regions. It further introduces tooth-wise coverage learning, combining coverage-aware reward signals and a progressive training scheme to improve global reconstruction coverage while reducing uneven coverage across individual teeth. The learned policy selects relative scanner motions from accumulated geometric memory and robot proprioception for closed-loop scan control within the oral workspace.
  RobOralScan achieves a Chamfer Distance of 0.00838, an average coverage of 92.58%, a lower-tail per-tooth coverage of 88.45%, and a normalized AUC of 0.6674, completing the scan criterion in 8 of 10 evaluation episodes. Furthermore, zero-shot sim-to-real experiments demonstrate its practical feasibility on a physical robot-scanner setup.

</details>


### [195] [In-Context Model Predictive Generation: Open-Vocabulary Motion Synthesis from Language Models to Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26981)
*Xiaomeng Fu,Junfan Lin,Yang Liu,Yaowei Wang,Guanbin Li,Liang Lin,Ziliang Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ICMPG框架，将大语言模型规划与推理时物理反馈相结合，通过闭环模型预测控制方式生成既语义忠实又物理合理的人体运动，无需任务特定策略重训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在语义忠实度与物理真实感之间存在持久性权衡：基于LLM的方法能理解开放词汇指令但常违反物理约束，物理感知模型提升真实感但难以处理语义复杂性和新颖概念。

Method: ICMPG将运动合成重构为类模型预测控制过程，包含两个模块：CAMG模块利用LLM作为规划器分解文本指令并生成候选运动序列；MPG模块通过物理模拟和语义对齐评估候选序列，计算复合奖励并选择最优序列引导后续生成。

Result: 在标准与零样本开放词汇设置下的广泛实验中，ICMPG能鲁棒泛化到多样化指令，在物理合理性和语义忠实度上均优于代表性基线方法。

Conclusion: ICMPG桥接了语义理解与物理模拟之间的鸿沟，同时保持灵活性以兼容不同LLM骨干网络，实现了更通用和可控的文本驱动运动合成。

Abstract: Synthesizing human motion from textual descriptions is essential for immersive digital applications, yet existing methods face a persistent trade-off between semantic fidelity and physical realism. Large language model (LLM)-based approaches can interpret diverse open-vocabulary instructions and compose high-level action plans, but they often generate motions that violate physical constraints. Physics-aware models improve realism through simulation or control, but they struggle with semantic complexity, fine-grained instructions, and novel concepts. To address this gap, we propose In-Context Model Predictive Generation (ICMPG), a framework that integrates language-model planning with inference-time physical feedback. ICMPG reformulates motion synthesis as a Model Predictive Control (MPC)-like process with two modules. The Context-Aware Motion Generation (CAMG) module uses an LLM as a planner to decompose textual commands and generate candidate motion sequences from motion tokens. The Model Predictive Generation (MPG) module evaluates these candidates through physical simulation and semantic alignment, estimates a composite reward, and selects the best sequence to guide subsequent generation steps. Unlike open-loop generation, this closed-loop refinement enables ICMPG to adapt motions to both the input semantics and the simulated physical environment without task-specific policy retraining. Extensive experiments across standard and zero-shot open-vocabulary settings show that ICMPG generalizes robustly to diverse commands and produces motions that are more physically plausible and semantically faithful than representative baselines on the evaluated benchmarks. The framework bridges semantic interpretation and physical simulation while remaining flexible enough to incorporate different LLM backbones, enabling more versatile and controllable text-driven motion synthesis.

</details>


### [196] [RelAfford6D: Relational 6D Affordance Graphs for Constraint-Driven Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27036)
*Guodong Zhang,Qichen He,Wenyuan Xie,Shaokai Wu,Yanbiao Ji,Qiuchang Li,Bayram Bayramli,Yue Ding,Hongtao Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RelAfford6D，一种无需训练、基于关系型6D可操作图的框架，通过将语义指令转化为SE(3)位姿拓扑并建模为运动学约束满足问题，实现零样本机器人操作复杂铰接物体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动策略依赖孤立接触点或隐式可操作嵌入，缺乏处理复杂铰接物体所需的严格运动学约束，难以桥接抽象语义与精确物理控制之间的鸿沟。

Method: 1) 从自由形式指令推导主交互部件与其物理锚点之间的语义拓扑（关系型6D可操作图）；2) 利用视觉基础模型将拓扑节点提升为精确SE(3)位姿；3) 将下游执行建模为运动学约束满足问题，通过跟踪严格定义的物理流形（如旋转或棱柱轨道）合成连续轨迹；4) 耦合闭环跟踪机制实现动态重规划。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中，零样本成功率、跨类别泛化能力和执行鲁棒性均优于现有数据驱动基线。

Conclusion: 基于物理约束的几何方法比数据驱动方法在零样本泛化和鲁棒性方面更具优势，为开放世界机器人操作提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: Bridging abstract semantics and precise physical control remains a fundamental challenge in open-world robotic manipulation. While recent data-driven policies show promise, their reliance on isolated contact points or latent affordance embeddings lacks the rigorous kinematic constraints necessary for complex articulated objects.To overcome the limitation, we introduce RelAfford6D, a novel training-free framework centered on a Relational 6D Affordance Graph. Given a free-form instruction, our system deduces a semantic topology linking a primary interacting part to its physical anchor. By elevating these topological nodes into precise metric $SE(3)$ poses via vision foundation models, we analytically formulate downstream execution as a kinematic constraint satisfaction problem. The robot synthesizes continuous trajectories by tracking strictly defined physical manifolds (e.g., revolute or prismatic orbits). Coupled with a closed-loop tracking mechanism for dynamic replanning against disturbances, our physically grounded approach achieves superior zero-shot success rates, cross-category generalization and execution robustness in both simulation and the real world environments, outperforming existing data-driven baselines.

</details>


### [197] [ForesightSafety-VLA: A Unified Diagnostic Safety Benchmark for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27079)
*Mingyang Lyu,Yinqian Sun,Yiyang Jia,Sicheng Shen,Moquan Sha,Huangrui Li,Feifei Zhao,Yi Zeng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ForesightSafety-VLA基准，系统评估VLA模型在具身智能中的安全性，发现即使最强的策略也存在显著安全风险，且结构性和视觉变化比语言变化更易导致安全退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型向通用任务能力不断进步，但其具身安全边界尚不清晰，缺乏以安全为首要评估目标的诊断性基准。

Method: 定义13类安全分类体系（Safe-Core/Safe-Lang/Safe-Vis），引入累计安全成本(CC)和风险暴露时间(RET)作为过程级风险指标，以及四象限安全/不安全成功与失败分解方法，在RoboTwin中构建66个安全增强场景，覆盖5种具身形态。

Result: 最强基线策略仍产生不可忽视的安全成本和名义上的不安全成功；结构和视觉变化导致的安全退化显著强于语言变化。

Conclusion: 具身安全与感知、接地和控制能力紧密耦合，而非仅靠事后安全过滤即可解决。

Abstract: In embodied intelligence, safety is a prerequisite for reliable robot deployment in the physical world. Current vision-language-action (VLA) models continue to advance toward general-purpose task capability, yet their embodied safety limits remain poorly understood. To address this gap, we introduce ForesightSafety-VLA, a diagnostic benchmark that makes safety the primary evaluation target for VLA systems. We define a 13-category safety taxonomy covering physical interaction safety (Safe-Core), instruction-side safety (Safe-Lang), and perception-side safety (Safe-Vis), and evaluate policies under three controlled dimensions of variation -- scene structure, language command, and visual observation -- so that failure sources can be diagnosed rather than hidden in a single aggregate score. Beyond binary task success, ForesightSafety-VLA measures process-level risk through cumulative safety cost (CC) and risk exposure time (RET), together with a four-quadrant decomposition of safe/unsafe success and failure. We instantiate 66 safety-augmented base scenarios in RoboTwin across 5 embodiments and report results on representative VLA baselines. Across the evaluated baselines, even the strongest policy incurs non-trivial safety cost and unsafe nominal success, while structure and visual variation induce substantially stronger safety degradation than ordinary language variation. These results suggest that embodied safety is tightly coupled to perception, grounding, and control competence rather than being reducible to post-hoc safety filtering alone.

</details>


### [198] [Proposal-Conditioned Latent Diffusion for Closed-Loop Traffic Scenario Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27123)
*Shubham Vaijanath Phoolari,Aleyna Kara,Christoph Lauer,Steven Peters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于扩散模型的闭环交通场景生成框架，通过实例中心场景上下文和多模态提议先验实现高效采样，并支持测试时引导以权衡安全性、真实性和可控性。在Waymo数据集上取得了良好的性能平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的交通仿真方法虽真实性高，但计算成本大，难以部署在自动驾驶规划等时间受限的重规划循环中，因此需要更高效的交互式多智能体行为生成方法。

Method: 提出基于扩散的场景生成框架，采用(1)实例中心场景上下文和多模态提议先验进行条件生成；(2)紧凑的动作-潜在空间表示提高采样效率；(3)提议初始化减少每步运行时间；(4)可选的测试时引导用于塑造安全关键行为。

Result: 在Waymo Open Motion Dataset上的实验表明，该方法在多样性交互场景中实现了真实性、安全性和可控性之间的良好平衡，且测试时引导能够系统性地权衡竞争目标。

Conclusion: 扩散模型结合紧凑动作潜在表示和提议初始化可显著提升采样效率，测试时引导为自动驾驶仿真中的安全关键场景生成提供了灵活的控制手段。

Abstract: Closed-loop traffic simulation remains challenging because it must generate interactive multi-agent behaviors that are scene-consistent and controllable throughout rollout. Prior diffusion-based approaches achieve strong realism, but their computational cost can hinder deployment in time-constrained replanning loops for autonomous vehicle planning and simulation. We present a diffusion-based scenario generation framework conditioned on instance-centric scene context and multimodal proposal priors, with optional test-time guidance for shaping safety-critical behaviors. A compact action-latent representation and proposal-based initialization improve sampling efficiency and reduce per-step runtime without retraining. Experiments on the Waymo Open Motion Dataset demonstrate a favorable balance among realism, safety, and controllability across diverse interactive scenarios, while showing that test-time guidance enables systematic trade-offs among competing objectives.

</details>


### [199] [PAMAE: Phase-Aware-MoE Action Experts Towards Reliable Flow-Matching Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27144)
*Jiayu Yang,Tao Yang,Xiang Chang,Fei Chao,Changjing Shang,Qiang Shen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对多阶段机器人操作中VLA模型动作生成可靠性问题，提出即插即用的PAMAE模块，通过相位感知的专家混合架构替代原有流匹配动作专家，结合相位路由器、轻量相位预测头和两阶段训练，在仿真任务中最高提升9.2%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流匹配VLA策略使用单一共享动作专家，无法捕捉多阶段操作中不同执行阶段特有的控制模式，导致动作生成可靠性不足。

Method: 提出PAMAE模块：1) 用稀疏专家混合替代原始流匹配动作专家；2) 相位感知路由器利用执行阶段线索分配动作生成；3) 轻量相位预测头及路由对齐目标；4) 两阶段训练方案（先标准流匹配损失预热，再优化相位一致路由）。

Result: 在多阶段操作仿真任务中，相比强VLA基线，任务成功率最高提升9.2%。消融实验表明相位监督路由和分阶段优化对性能提升至关重要。

Conclusion: 相位一致的动作专家分配是提升流匹配VLA策略可靠性和动作质量的有效机制。

Abstract: Reliable action generation for multi-stage robotic manipulation remains challenging for Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models. While existing flow-matching VLA policies offer strong multimodal grounding and generalization, they typically employ a single shared action expert, limiting their ability to capture phase-specific control patterns across distinct execution stages. We propose a plug-and-play Phase-Aware Mixture-of-Experts Action Module (PAMAE), as a step towards more reliable phase-consistent action generation. PAMAE replaces the original flow-matching action expert with a sparse expert mixture while preserving the pretrained VLA backbone. PAMAE introduces a phase-aware router that leverages execution-phase cues to allocate action generation across experts, supported by a lightweight phase prediction head and a routing alignment objective. To stabilize specialization, we adopt a two-stage training scheme that first warms up the expert module under the standard flow-matching loss and then optimizes phase-consistent routing under auxiliary supervision. On multi-stage manipulation simulation tasks, PAMAE improves task success by up to \textbf{9.2\%} over strong VLA baselines. Further ablations show that both phase-supervised routing and staged optimization are essential for the observed gains. Our results highlight phase-consistent expert allocation as an effective mechanism for improving the reliability and action quality of flow-matching VLA policies.

</details>


### [200] [PhysReflect-VLA: Physical Feasibility and Self-Reflective Regulation for Reliable Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27146)
*Jiayu Yang,Tao Yang,Weijun Li,Xiang Chang,Fei Chao,Changjing Shang,Qiang Shen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhysReflect-VLA是一种即插即用的执行时可靠性框架，通过物理可行性评估和结构化自我反思增强VLA策略，在长时域机器人操作任务中实现了5.4%的平均成功率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时域机器人操作对物理不可行的状态转移、接触引起的扰动以及执行过程中缺乏有效的自我纠正高度敏感。现有VLA模型以前馈方式生成动作，未显式检查物理可行性或在线诊断执行错误。

Method: 提出PhysReflect-VLA框架，包含三个核心模块：可行性评估器（评估候选动作是否产生动力学一致的状态转移）、动作解释器（验证转移一致性）、基于LLM的反思模块（分析状态差异并生成纠正指导）；采用两阶段训练流程稳定可行性建模并将反思集成到控制循环中。

Result: 在多阶段、接触丰富的真实世界操作任务上，相比代表性VLA基线，平均成功率提升5.4%；消融实验表明可行性检查和基于反思的纠正均对执行鲁棒性有贡献。

Conclusion: 嵌入物理一致性检查和在线自我反思对于可靠的长时域机器人操作至关重要。

Abstract: Long-horizon robotic manipulation is highly sensitive to physically infeasible transitions, contact-induced disturbances, and the lack of effective self-correction during execution. Although Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models provide strong task grounding through multimodal learning, they typically generate actions in a feed-forward manner without explicitly checking physical feasibility or diagnosing execution errors online. We present PhysReflect-VLA, a plug-and-play execution-time reliability framework that augments VLA policies with physical feasibility evaluation and structured self-reflection in a closed-loop control pipeline. A Feasibility Operator evaluates whether candidate actions induce dynamically consistent state transitions; an Action Explanation Operator verifies transition coherence; and an LLM-based Reflection Module analyzes state discrepancies to generate corrective guidance for subsequent actions. A two-stage training procedure stabilizes feasibility modeling and integrates reflection into the control loop. Experiments on multi-stage, contact-rich real-world manipulation tasks show consistent improvements in stage-wise stability and overall task success compared with representative VLA baselines with an average gain of 5.4\%. Ablation results further indicate that feasibility checking and reflection-based correction both contribute to improved execution robustness. These results highlight the importance of embedding physical consistency checks and online self-reflection for reliable long-horizon robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [201] [Learning to Fold: prizewinning solution at LeHome Challenge 2026 (1st place online, 2nd offline)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27163)
*Ilia Larchenko*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个结合强化学习回路的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略，在2026年LeHome Challenge双臂衣物折叠任务中获得模拟赛第一、真实赛第二。核心贡献包括：AWR+RECAP结合的流匹配VLA、异步分布式训练管线、推理时超参数优化、以及包含相机对齐、数据增强和DAgger式人在回路数据采集的sim-to-real方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决双臂衣物折叠这一复杂操作任务，提升VLA策略在模拟和真实环境中的表现。

Method: 1) 策略网络同时预测动作和多种价值信号（成功、进度等），驱动优势估计、故障检测和候选选择；2) AWR+RECAP结合用于流匹配VLA训练；3) 通过HuggingFace Hub实现异步分布式训练/数据采集管线；4) 推理时采用Thompson采样优化超参数；5) sim-to-real包含相机对齐工具、大量数据增强和DAgger式人在回路数据采集。

Result: 模拟赛62支队伍中排名第一，真实决赛排名第二。

Conclusion: 通过重新组合现有RL思想并加入工程优化，提出了一套可用于双臂衣物折叠的VLA训练方案，各组件既可整体使用也可单独采用。

Abstract: I describe my solution to the LeHome Challenge 2026, an ICRA 2026 competition on bimanual garment folding. The system placed 1st of 62 teams in the online (simulation) round and 2nd in the real-world final. It improves a vision-language-action (VLA) policy with a reinforcement-learning loop. The policy is its own value function: the same network that predicts actions also predicts success, progress, and a few task-relevant future quantities, and those predictions drive advantage estimation, live failure detection, and candidate selection. The work mostly recombines existing RL ideas with engineering and optimization contributions that can be used together as one recipe or individually: AWR + RECAP combined for flow-matching VLA; an asynchronous distributed training / rollout pipeline through HuggingFace Hub; inference-time hyperparameters optimization via Thompson sampling; a sim-to-real recipe with camera-alignment tooling, heavy augmentation and DAgger-like HIL data collection.

</details>


### [202] [HumanoidUMI: Bridging Robot-Free Demonstrations and Humanoid Whole-Body Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27239)
*Hongwu Wang,Chenhao Yu,Youhao Hu,Jiachen Zhang,Yuanyuan Li,Shaqi Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了HumanoidUMI，一个基于VR设备和UMI灵感夹爪的便携式无机器人框架，用于采集人类全身演示数据，训练仿人机器人全身技能学习策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的仿人机器人数据采集方法高度依赖遥操作，受限于硬件可及性、操作员专业知识和效率低下，亟需一种便携、高效且无需机器人的数据采集框架。

Method: 使用轻量级VR设备和UMI灵感夹爪，采集稀疏人体关键点轨迹、腕部视角观测和夹爪动作；训练高层策略预测未来关键点，将其重定向为机器人原生全身参考，再由全身控制器执行。

Result: 在五个真实场景中的实验验证了该框架的有效性，以及采集的演示数据对可迁移的仿人机器人全身技能学习的有效性。

Conclusion: HumanoidUMI提供了一种便携、无机器人的仿人全身数据采集方案，有效支持了可迁移的全身技能学习。

Abstract: High-quality demonstration data are essential for humanoid robot skill learning, especially for whole-body behaviors that require coordinated perception, locomotion, and manipulation. Existing data-collection methods largely rely on robot teleoperation, which is constrained by hardware accessibility, operator expertise, and limited efficiency. Inspired by the Universal Manipulation Interface (UMI), we propose HumanoidUMI, a portable and robot-free framework for humanoid whole-body data collection. HumanoidUMI uses lightweight VR devices and UMI-inspired grippers to collect sparse human keypoint trajectories, wrist-view observations, and gripper actions. These demonstrations train a high-level policy to predict future keypoints, which are retargeted to robot-native whole-body references and executed by a whole-body controller. Experiments in five real-world scenarios demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework and validate the collected demonstrations for transferable humanoid whole-body skill learning.

</details>


### [203] [Advancing Omnimodal Embodied Agents from Isolated Skills to Everyday Physical Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27251)
*Junhao Shi,Zezheng Huai,Siyin Wang,Jia Chen,Yubang Wang,Zhaoye Fei,Hechang Chen,Jingjing Gong,Xipeng Qiu,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OmniAct是一个层次化异步架构，通过统一的语义规划器、自适应分层记忆和异步视觉抢占引擎，实现持久化具身代理在非结构化环境中的自主操作，在40个真实世界长程任务中取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统将工具编排（API、IoT）与物理操作（操控、导航）视为分离问题，VLM规划器缺乏统一动作空间，代理框架累积无界上下文导致时间连贯性退化，VLA策略开环执行无法检测自身故障。持久化自主需要明确的规划、记忆与验证分离。

Method: 提出OmniAct框架，包含三个核心组件：(1)多模态语义规划器，在统一动作空间中进行技能路由；(2)自适应分层记忆，通过事件边界驱动压缩实现亚线性上下文增长；(3)异步视觉抢占引擎，在物理执行期间闭合语义回路。

Result: 在两个机器人平台协调四个IoT设备的40个真实世界长程任务中，OmniAct在所有复杂度级别上实现端到端成功率的一致提升，在10万+累积交互令牌上保持近平坦的令牌消耗，并将中规模开源模型提升至专有模型性能水平。

Conclusion: 持久化自主不需要单一 monolithic 模型，而是一个层次化异步架构，通过规划、记忆与验证的明确分离实现统一编排和自主故障恢复。

Abstract: Building persistent embodied agents in unstructured environments demands unified orchestration of heterogeneous tools spanning both cyber (APIs, IoT) and physical (manipulation, navigation) domains, coupled with autonomous recovery from physical failures that inevitably arise over extended operation. Existing systems treat these as separate problems: VLM-based planners lack a unified cyber-physical action space, agent frameworks accumulate unbounded context that degrades temporal coherence, and VLA policies execute open-loop without detecting their own failures. We argue that persistent autonomy requires not a monolithic model but a hierarchical asynchronous architecture with explicit separation of planning, memory, and verification. To this end, we present OmniAct, a framework integrating a multimodal semantic planner for skill routing across unified action spaces, an adaptive hierarchical memory with event-boundary-driven compression for sub-linear context growth, and an asynchronous visual preemption engine that closes the semantic loop during physical execution. Across 40 real-world long-horizon tasks on two robotic platforms coordinating four IoT devices, OmniAct achieves consistent improvements in end-to-end success across all complexity levels, maintains near-flat token consumption over under 100k+ accumulated interaction tokens, and elevates mid-scale open-weight models to proprietary-level performance.

</details>


### [204] [E-TTS: A New Embodied Test-Time Scaling Framework for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27268)
*Wen Ye,Peiyan Li,Tingyu Yuan,Yuan Xu,Xiangnan Wu,Chaoyang Zhao,Jing Liu,Nianfeng Liu,Yan Huang,Liang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: E-TTS是一个模块化、即插即用的具身测试时扩展框架，通过历史感知的迭代精炼和视觉-语言验证器统一推理与动作缩放，在无需额外数据或重训练的情况下，在仿真和真实场景中分别提升最高33.14%和26.62%的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身任务的测试时缩放研究存在两个未解决挑战：(1) 推理能有效提升策略性能但其缩放机制鲜有研究；(2) 具身任务本质上是长程序列决策的，仅依赖当前观测进行动作缩放缺乏历史上下文利用。

Method: E-TTS框架包含：(1) 推理-动作联合采样与成对评分机制；(2) 历史缓冲区存储历史上下文，由推理和动作验证器评估候选样本；(3) 反馈生成引入采样过程形成闭环迭代精炼机制，提升推理效率与环境适应性；(4) 各组件为独立可组合模块，灵活配置。

Result: 在4个基准、6个环境、3种具身形态和4种基础视觉-语言-动作模型上进行实验，无需额外专家数据收集或重训练，仿真场景提升最高33.14%，真实场景提升最高26.62%。

Conclusion: E-TTS通过统一推理与动作缩放，并有效利用历史信息，显著提升了具身操控任务性能，证明了测试时扩展在具身智能领域的有效性。

Abstract: Recently, a few works have made early attempts to study test-time scaling for embodied tasks. However, two major challenges remain unsolved: (1) reasoning can effectively improve the performance of the policy, but its scaling mechanism has seldom been studied; (2) historical information is essential, as embodied tasks are inherently long-horizon and sequential, making sole reliance on current observations for action scaling inadequate due to the lack of historical context utilization. To address these challenges, we introduce E-TTS, a modular and plug-and-play Embodied Test-Time Scaling framework that unifies reasoning and action scaling for robotic manipulation via history-aware iterative refinement with vision-language verifiers. To support joint reasoning-action scaling, E-TTS performs reasoning-action joint sampling and scoring in a pairwise manner. To better utilize historical information, E-TTS uses a history buffer to store historical context, which is then used by reasoning and action verifiers to evaluate the sampled candidates. Unlike conventional open-loop TTS methods, E-TTS introduces feedback generation into the sampling process to form a closed-loop iterative refinement mechanism, enhancing both inference efficiency and environmental adaptability. Each component functions as an independent and composable module, allowing flexible and adaptive configuration depending on task requirements. To evaluate the advantages of our framework, we conduct experiments across 4 different benchmarks, 6 environments, 3 embodiments, and 4 base vision-language-action models. The experimental results demonstrate that, without requiring additional expert data collection or retraining, E-TTS consistently improves performance, achieving up to a 33.14% increase in simulation and 26.62% in real-world scenarios.

</details>


### [205] [BOWConnect: Parallel Bayesian Optimization over Windows with Learned Local Cost Maps for Sample-Efficient Kinodynamic Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27292)
*Sourav Raxit,Abdullah Al Redwan Newaz,Jose Fuentes,Leonardo Bobadilla*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出BOWConnect，一种双向并行运动规划器，通过集成贝叶斯优化窗口作为学习型转向函数，结合双向树探索和空间哈希机制，在十个基准环境中实现100%成功率，并在窄通道等复杂场景中达到最快或接近最快的规划速度，真实机器人部署验证了其实时无碰撞规划能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于采样的运动规划方法存在三个根本局限：高维状态空间中的样本低效性、动态约束下不可靠的成本启发式、以及在窄通道环境中性能不佳。作者旨在克服这些局限性，提出更高效的规划方法。

Method: BOWConnect将贝叶斯优化窗口(BOW)作为学习型转向函数集成到并行树探索框架中；每个工作线程学习局部成本图和约束以引导采样；采用双向架构在并行线程中从起点和终点区域同时生长前向和后向树；使用空间哈希机制加速连接查询；通过边界值问题求解器生成运动学一致的桥接轨迹。

Result: 在十个基准环境中实现100%成功率，在复杂场景（包括窄通道和非凸空间）中达到最快或接近最快的规划时间，优于现有最先进规划器。在真实地面车辆和四旋翼飞行器上实现实时无碰撞规划。

Conclusion: BOWConnect通过学习型转向函数和双向并行架构有效解决了现有采样规划方法的三个根本局限，在仿真和真实场景中均展现出卓越的性能和实用性。

Abstract: This paper presents BOWConnect, a bidirectional parallel kinodynamic motion planner that addresses three fundamental limitations of existing sampling-based methods: sample inefficiency in high-dimensional state spaces, unreliable cost heuristics under dynamic constraints, and poor performance in narrow passage environments. Unlike classical planners that rely on random control sampling and geometric distance heuristics, BOWConnect integrates Bayesian Optimization over Windows (BOW) as a learning-based steering function within a parallel tree-based exploration framework, enabling each worker to learn local cost maps and constraints to guide sampling toward dynamically feasible and collision-free controls. A bidirectional architecture simultaneously grows forward and backward trees from the start and goal regions in parallel threads, with a spatial hashing mechanism enabling fast connection queries and a boundary value problem solver generating kinodynamically consistent bridge trajectories. Extensive evaluations across ten benchmark environments demonstrate that BOWConnect achieves 100\% success while delivering the fastest or near-fastest planning time in complex scenarios, including narrow passages and non-convex spaces where state-of-the-art planners fail or degrade substantially. Real-world deployment on a ground vehicle and a quadrotor confirms real-time planning with no collisions. Videos of real-world and simulated experiments, high-resolution versions of the figures, and the open-source code are available at https://bow-connect.github.io/.

</details>


### [206] [LA4VLA: Learning to Act without Seeing via Language-Action Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27295)
*Tao Lin,Yuxin Du,Yiran Mao,Zewei Ye,Yilei Zhong,Bing Cheng,Yiming Wang,Jiting Liu,Yang Tian,Junchi Yan,Feiran Wu,Zenan Meng,Hu Wei,Yuqian Fu,Gen Li,Bo Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出LA4VLA框架，通过语言-动作预训练（不依赖视觉观测）让VLA模型学习语言条件化的动作先验，缓解视觉捷径问题，在仿真和真实任务中分别提升最高17.8和45.0个百分点的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在预训练时，密集的视觉-动作监督信号会压倒相对稀疏的语言-动作信号，导致策略依赖视觉捷径（visual shortcuts）而非真正学习语言如何条件化动作执行，从而对视觉变化敏感。

Method: 提出LA4VLA框架，将专家演示轨迹分解为原子动作片段，并为每个片段配对应低层动作描述，构建LA4-33K数据集（33K语言-动作片段）；开发1B参数的LA4VLA-1B模型；探索三种预训练范式：仅LA预训练、顺序LA-to-VLA预训练、混合LA-VLA预训练。

Result: 在仿真和真实任务中，LA预训练策略始终优于匹配的VLA预训练基线；混合LA-VLA预训练相比无预训练基线，在仿真任务中平均成功率提升最高17.8个百分点，在真实任务中提升最高45.0个百分点。

Conclusion: LA4VLA是一种有效且互补的预训练策略，能构建更强、更鲁棒的VLA策略。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are commonly pretrained on robot demonstrations by jointly mapping visual observations and language instructions to actions. However, dense visual-action supervision can dominate the comparatively sparse language-action signal. As a result, policies may rely on visual shortcuts rather than learn how language conditions action execution, making them sensitive to visual variations. To address this limitation, we propose LA4VLA, a language-action pretraining framework that enables policies to acquire language-conditioned action priors without visual observations. These priors capture reusable manipulation skills shared across tasks and scenes, reducing reliance on scene-specific visual cues. Specifically, LA4VLA decomposes expert demonstration trajectories into atomic action segments and pairs each segment with a corresponding low-level action description. This yields LA4-33K, a dataset of 33K Language-Action (LA) episodes derived entirely from existing demonstrations without additional robot data collection. We further develop LA4VLA-1B, a lightweight 1B-parameter VLA model, and investigate three paradigms for incorporating language-action supervision into VLA learning: LA-only pretraining, sequential LA-to-VLA pretraining, and mixed LA-VLA pretraining. Across simulation and real-world tasks, LA-pretrained policies consistently outperform matched VLA-pretrained counterparts, while combining LA and VLA supervision leads to further gains. In particular, mixed LA-VLA pretraining improves the average success rate of LA4VLA-1B over the no-pretraining baseline by up to 17.8 and 45.0 percentage points in simulation and real-world tasks, respectively. These results establish LA4VLA as an effective and complementary pretraining strategy for building stronger and more robust VLA policies.

</details>


### [207] [VibeAct: Vibration to Actions for Contact-Rich Reactive Robot Dexterity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27344)
*Yuemin Mao,Uksang Yoo,Jean Oh,Jonathan Francis,Jeffrey Ichnowski*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VibeAct提出了一种通过共享的接触和滑移物理表征，将真实压电麦克风振动信号与仿真强化学习桥接的框架，实现了灵巧手操作策略的sim-to-real迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作依赖快速、局部且常被视觉遮挡的接触事件，压电麦克风虽能紧凑高带宽地感知这些交互，但产生的振动声学信号难以足够逼真地仿真，无法支持端到端的sim-to-real策略学习。

Method: 在真实灵巧手中嵌入压电麦克风，通过遥操作收集振动数据；在标定的数字孪生中回放录音并自动标注逐指接触与滑移；训练触觉估计器从真实麦克风波形预测接触和滑移；同时在仿真中基于相同表征训练操作策略，避免了对原始音频的仿真。

Result: 在重抓取、手内重定向和插入等五项接触密集型任务中，VibeAct始终优于基于本体感觉和点云的基线方法，在需要持续反应控制的任务中提升最大，连续滑移幅度通道是最具信息量的观测。学到的策略成功迁移到物理灵巧手-臂平台。

Conclusion: 通过将触觉感知解耦为物理表征而非原始信号，VibeAct有效实现了基于振动的触觉反馈在灵巧操作策略学习中的sim-to-real迁移。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation depends on contact events that are fast, local, and often visually occluded. Piezoelectric microphones offer a compact and high-bandwidth way to sense these interactions, but the resulting vibro-acoustic signals are difficult to simulate faithfully enough for end-to-end sim-to-real policy learning on dexterous robot hands. We propose VibeAct, a framework that bridges real vibrotactile sensing and simulation-based reinforcement learning through a shared physical representation of contact and slip. In the real world, we embed piezoelectric microphones into a dexterous robot hand and collect vibro-acoustic data through teleoperation, then replay the recordings in a calibrated digital clone to automatically label per-finger contact and slip. A tactile estimator learns to predict contact and slip from real microphone waveforms, while manipulation policies are trained in simulation on the same representation computed directly from simulated contacts. This decoupling lets policies exploit rapid tactile feedback without simulating raw audio. Across five contact-rich tasks spanning regrasping, in-hand reorientation, and insertion, VibeAct consistently outperforms a proprioception-and-point-cloud baseline in simulation, with the largest gains on tasks requiring sustained reactive control, where the continuous slip-magnitude channel proves the most informative observation. The learned policies transfer to a physical dexterous hand-arm platform, improving success rates on deployed tasks. Project videos and additional details are at https://vibeact.github.io/.

</details>


### [208] [Bridging Performance and Generalization in Reinforcement Learning for Agile Flight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27348)
*Jonathan Green,Jiaxu Xing,Nico Messikommer,Angel Romero,Davide Scaramuzza*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于强化学习的无人机竞速零样本泛化框架，通过结合基于学习进度的任务感知切换和物理启发的程序化赛道生成器，在保持竞争性飞行速度的同时，实现了相比现有方法7.4倍的泛化性能提升，并在仿真和真实世界中得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL-based无人机竞速方法在特定环境中表现优异，但无法泛化到未见过的赛道配置；而现有提升泛化能力的方法又严重牺牲飞行速度。因此，如何在保证高速度的同时实现零样本泛化是该研究要解决的核心问题。

Method: 提出一个包含两个核心组件的框架：(1) 基于学习进度的任务感知切换机制（task-aware switching based on learning progress），(2) 物理启发的程序化赛道生成器（physically informed procedural track generator）。该框架无需测试时自适应即可生成快速且鲁棒的通用策略。

Result: 在真实世界多种未见过的赛道上实现了强零样本泛化性能，相比最先进方法泛化能力提升7.4倍，同时保持竞争性飞行速度。在模拟和真实世界设置中均验证有效，包括所有先前方法均失败的基于视觉的端到端控制设置（无需显式状态估计）。

Conclusion: 提出了一种在敏捷飞行中实现零样本泛化的有效框架，在不牺牲飞行速度的前提下显著提升了泛化性能，填补了RL-based无人机竞速在泛化能力上的空白。

Abstract: Autonomous drone racing is a fundamentally challenging regime for autonomous aerial robots, requiring time-optimal control while operating under persistent actuation saturation. While reinforcement learning (RL) has achieved human-level performance in this domain, current methods fail to generalize; policies trained on specific environments often crash immediately in unseen configurations. This failure reflects the intrinsic difficulty of zero-shot generalization in agile flight, arising from high-dimensional task variation and the tight coupling between safety and performance at high speeds. Existing approaches that improve generalization impose a substantial cost on flight speed: control policies must significantly degrade performance to achieve even modest levels of generalization. In this work, we propose a framework for zero-shot generalization in agile flight for RL-based drone racing. By combining task-aware switching based on learning progress with a physically informed procedural track generator, the framework produces a fast and robust generalist policy without test-time adaptation. Our method achieves strong zero-shot performance across a wide range of unseen racetracks in the real world, demonstrating a 7.4x improvement in generalization over the state-of-the-art approaches, while maintaining competitive racing speeds. We validate our method's results in both simulation and real-world settings, including a challenging vision-based, end-to-end control setting that operates without explicit state estimation, where all prior approaches fail to generalize.

</details>


### [209] [Continual Robot Policy Learning via Variational Neural Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27353)
*Jiaxu Xing,Zhiyuan Zhu,Yunfan Ren,Ismail Geles,Yifan Zhai,Rudolf Reiter,Davide Scaramuzza*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种持续学习框架，通过结合物理先验和神经残差模型，以及循环编码器推断隐式动态条件，使机器人能够在部署后利用真实世界经验持续改进策略，在四旋翼轨迹跟踪任务中比在线残差重拟合快约5倍恢复重复扰动，并将大扰动悬停和跟踪误差分别降低65.7%和53.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中机器人面临的动力学模型多变（风、负载、电池、接触、硬件磨损等），而大多数基于学习的控制器训练一次即部署，无法利用部署过程中的经验进一步提升任务性能。
