Method: 提出持续学习框架：(1) 结合分析物理先验与神经残差构建条件感知动力学模型；(2) 使用循环编码器从近期交互推断当前隐藏条件；(3) 通过可微仿真利用从潜模型采样的多样化动力学进行策略学习；(4) 部署时用在线推断的条件替换采样条件，通过识别而非残差重拟合恢复重复动力学。

Result: 在四旋翼轨迹跟踪任务中，策略在约1秒内从重复扰动中恢复，比在线残差重拟合快约5倍；大扰动悬停和跟踪误差分别比最先进的在线自适应方法降低65.7%和53.3%。

Conclusion: 该框架能有效利用部署经验提升策略在多种未观测扰动下的表现，通过识别而非拟合方式高效应对重复出现的动态变化。

Abstract: Robots deployed in the real world rarely operate under a single fixed dynamics model: wind changes, payloads vary, batteries drain, contacts shift, and hardware wears. Yet most learning-based controllers are trained once and deployed as if learning were complete. This prevents the robot from using deployment experience to further improve task performance. In this work, we propose a continual learning framework that uses real-world experience to improve robot policies under hidden and recurring dynamics. Our method learns a condition-aware dynamics model from real state-action trajectories by combining an analytical physics prior with a neural residual for unmodeled effects. A recurrent encoder infers the current hidden condition from recent interaction, and this estimate conditions both the residual model and the policy. Policy learning is performed via differentiable simulation using diverse learned dynamics sampled from the latent model. At deployment, these sampled conditions are replaced by conditions inferred online from recent real interaction, allowing the policy to recover recurring dynamics by recognition rather than residual re-fitting. Through extensive simulation studies and real-world experiments, we demonstrate that the framework improves policy performance under diverse unobserved disturbances. On real quadrotor trajectory tracking under changing wind, the policy recovers from recurring disturbances in roughly 1s, about 5x faster than online residual re-fitting. It also reduces large-disturbance hover and tracking errors by 65.7% and 53.3% over the state-of-the-art online adaptation approaches

</details>


### [210] [RouterVLA: Turning Smoke Tests into Supervision for Heterogeneous VLA Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27355)
*Xingyu Ren,Chugang Yi,Ge Ma,Youran Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RouterVLA方法，利用预部署评估数据来监督策略选择，通过结果不相交的交叉拟合方法，在34,752条LIBERO-Plus rollout记录上将保留集成功率从0.4686提升至0.6149（+14.64pp），并发现委派（commissioning）本身就携带路由价值，额外的标量评分能力无法创造这种价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究能否复用预部署评估rollout数据来监督策略选择（路由），即用已有的评估记录来训练选择器，避免为路由收集额外的标注数据。

Method: 提出RouterVLA方法，采用结果不相交交叉拟合（outcome-disjoint cross-fitting）：用记录的探测数据构建每个冻结专家的档案，再使用独立的trial评分被选中的专家，确保评分trial的标签不出现在专家档案中。

Result: 在34,752条LIBERO-Plus rollout记录上，透明探测-成功规则将保留集成功率从0.4686提升至0.6149（+14.64pp增益）。在仅使用标量档案的情况下，学习型评分器与这一规则统计上不可区分。重用被评分trial会使测量增益膨胀1.87倍。

Conclusion: 委派（commissioning）本身就携带路由价值，额外的标量评分器容量无法创造这种价值；可信的账本路由需要结果分离；模型扩展提升个体策略，而委派感知路由提升由这些策略构成的系统整体性能。

Abstract: We study whether pre-deployment evaluation rollouts can be reused to supervise policy selection. Robot teams routinely smoke test candidate vision-language-action (VLA) policies, then compress those trials into a global winner. RouterVLA evaluates this idea with outcome-disjoint cross-fitting: recorded probes build a profile for each frozen expert, and a separate trial scores the selected expert without entering its profile. Across 34,752 LIBERO-Plus rollout records, a transparent probe-success rule raises held-out success from 0.4686 to 0.6149, a +14.64pp gain. Under the scalar-only profiles studied here, learned scorers are statistically indistinguishable from this rule, showing that commissioning carries the routing value while extra scalar scorer capacity does not create it. Reusing the scored trial inflates the measured gain by $1.87\times$, so credible ledger routing needs outcome separation; model scaling improves individual policies, while commissioning-aware routing improves the system built from them.

</details>


### [211] [World Action Models Enable Continual Imitation Learning with Recurrent Generative Replays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27374)
*Manish Kumar Govind,Dominick Reilly,Smit Patel,Hieu Le,Srijan Das*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: REGEN是一种基于世界行动模型（WAM）的持续模仿学习框架，通过递归生成伪重放轨迹来缓解灾难性遗忘，无需存储原始人类示范，在仿真和真实实验中减少遗忘达50%，接近需要真实重放数据的特权方法水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决机器人持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题，同时避免存储原始人类示范数据所带来的隐私和存储开销，探索利用世界行动模型的生成能力替代经验重放。

Method: 提出REGEN框架，利用WAM的生成能力，在持续适应过程中递归查询WAM，仅基于先前的任务指令和当前任务观测来合成伪重放轨迹，供机器人策略排练先前学习的任务。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人操作实验中，REGEN相比顺序微调减少了高达50%的灾难性遗忘，性能接近需要真实重放数据的经验重放方法。同时识别了长视距视觉退化和动作-观测不一致性作为生成重放的主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: WAM可以作为无需存储示范的持续机器人学习的有前景基础，REGEN验证了生成式重放在机器人持续学习中的有效性，并指出了未来改进方向。

Abstract: Going beyond predicting robot actions, World Action Models (WAMs) can also generate future visual observations. We build on this generative capability to propose Recurrent Generative Replay (REGEN), a continual imitation learning framework that synthesizes pseudo-replay trajectories, enabling a robot policy to rehearse previously learned tasks without storing their original human demonstrations. During continual adaptation, REGEN recursively queries the WAM to synthesize pseudo-replay trajectories conditioned only on prior task instructions and current-task observations. Experiments in both simulation and real-world manipulation settings show that REGEN reduces catastrophic forgetting by up to $50\%$ relative to sequential fine-tuning, while approaching the performance of privileged experience replay methods that require access to real replay data. Finally, we analyze the factors limiting generated replay, identifying long-horizon visual degradation and action-observation inconsistency as the primary bottlenecks. Our results establish WAMs as a promising foundation for continual robot learning without stored demonstrations.

</details>


### [212] [Scalable Behavior Cloning with Open Data, Training, and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27375)
*Arthur Allshire,Himanshu Gaurav Singh,Ritvik Singh,Adam Rashid,Hongsuk Choi,David McAllister,Justin Yu,Yiyuan Chen,Huang Huang,Pieter Abbeel,Xi Chen,Rocky Duan,Phillip Isola,Jitendra Malik,Fred Shentu,Guanya Shi,Philipp Wu,Angjoo Kanazawa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了ABC，一个完全开源的行为克隆操作栈，包含ABC-130K数据集（迄今最大开源遥操作数据集，3500小时/13万episode/195个任务）、开源硬件、训练基础设施、仿真管线以及400小时仿真遥操作数据，并提供联合训练方案以实现仿真与真实评估的相关性，验证了DiT和VLA架构的设计选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为克隆（Behavior Cloning）领域缺乏大规模、完全开源的数据集和可复现的工具链，研究者难以公平比较不同方法；现有工作往往使用私有数据或封闭硬件，阻碍了社区协作和进步。

Method: 1) 构建ABC-130K大规模遥操作数据集（3500小时，13万+episode，195个任务）；2) 开源硬件设置、训练基础设施和仿真管线；3) 收集400小时仿真遥操作数据并提供联合训练方案，使仿真评估能可靠代理真实评估；4) 对比Diffusion Transformers (DiT) 和 Vision-Language-Action (VLA) 模型的架构选择。

Result: 训练得到的策略能成功执行盒折叠、从钱包中取出信用卡等灵巧操作任务；仿真与真实评估之间建立了可靠的相关性，为模型设计消融提供了有效的代理指标。

Conclusion: 通过提供完全可复现的工具包，ABC将研究者置于平等地位，为社区共同学习行为克隆的基础知识奠定了必要基础。

Abstract: We introduce ABC, a fully open-source stack for manipulation with behavior cloning. At its core is ABC-130K: the largest open-source teleoperation dataset to date, featuring 3,500 hours of data spanning over 130K episodes across 195 diverse tasks. Furthermore, we open-source our accessible hardware setup, training infrastructure, and simulation pipeline. We also release 400 hours of sim-teleop data and provide a co-training recipe that produces correlated simulation and real-world evaluation, offering a reliable proxy for ablating model-design and training decisions before costly real-world evaluation. We explore various training recipes and compare common architectural choices for Diffusion Transformers (DiT) and Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, grounding our findings in real-world evaluations. The resulting policies successfully execute dexterous tasks such as box folding and extracting credit cards from wallets. By providing a reproducible toolkit, we aim to place researchers on an equal footing, establishing the necessary foundation to learn the ABCs of Behavior Cloning together as a community.

</details>


<div id='cs.PL'></div>

# cs.PL [[Back]](#toc)

### [213] [Axon: A Synthesizing Superoptimizer for Tensor Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26344)
*Akash Kothari,Shaowei Zhu,Daniel Kroening,Chungha Sung*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: Axon是一个面向AI加速器中张量程序的合成超级优化器，它通过程序合成自动从语义规范生成目标指令，并通过探索语义等价的程序变体来经验性地选择最佳执行内核。它利用代数变换和SMT验证保证语义保持，优化tiling配置和算子融合以最小化内存流量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为AI加速器编写高性能内核需要深入的平铺、指令选择、数据布局和算子融合专业知识，给程序员带来沉重负担。现有方法依赖手工编写的重写规则，缺乏自动化程度。

Method: 提出Axon框架：(1) 使用程序合成从语义规范自动生成目标指令；(2) 通过计算图传播算子进行代数变换，利用SMT（无界张量）保证语义等价性，无需手工重写规则；(3) 将张量操作降级为目标ISA指令；(4) 基于硬件描述探索tiling配置；(5) 融合算子和指令以最小化内存流量。

Result: Axon能够自动生成语义正确的目标指令，并通过探索语义等价变体经验性选择最优内核，在tiling配置和算子融合方面优化了内存访问模式。

Conclusion: Axon通过程序合成和SMT验证消除了对手工重写规则的依赖，为AI加速器的高性能内核开发提供了自动化解决方案。

Abstract: Writing high performance kernels for AI accelerators requires deep expertise in tiling, instruction selection, data layout, and operator fusion placing a significant burden on programmers. In this paper, we focus on tile based AI accelerator programs and present Axon, a synthesizing superoptimizer for tensor programs: it uses program synthesis to automatically generate target instructions from semantics specifications, and explores semantically equivalent program variants to select the best performing kernel empirically. Axon discovers algebraic transformations by propagating operators through computation graphs and uses SMT over unbounded tensors to guarantee that all transformations preserve semantics without requiring hand crafted rewrite rules. It then lowers tensor operations to target ISA instructions, explores tiling configurations constrained by hardware descriptions, and fuses operators and instructions to minimize memory traffic.

</details>


### [214] [A Typestate Approach to Purpose-aware Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26386)
*Joan Montas,Samuel Dodson,Anitha Gollamudi,Matteo Cimini*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出 PurPL 语言，利用类型状态（typestate）系统在编译时保证敏感数据按预期目的使用，并通过形式化类型系统和实现验证了其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实应用需要验证敏感数据是否按预期目的使用，但现有文献在编译时保证方面成果有限。

Method: 基于类型状态（typestate）方法，将敏感数据类型的状态定义为其可用目的的集合，该集合可在运行时动态增减；设计了面向对象语言 PurPL，包含形式化类型系统及对应的类型检查器。

Result: 实现了 PurPL 的类型检查器，并在多种处理敏感数据的编程场景上进行了类型检查实验。

Conclusion: PurPL 的类型状态系统能够有效推理数据使用目的及合规性，为敏感数据的安全使用提供了编译时保证。

Abstract: Real-world applications often require verification that sensitive data is being used for their intended purpose. However, existing literature offers limited results regarding compile-time guarantees in this domain. In this paper, we explore the use of typestate to reason about the purpose of data. In typestate, types have a state, which can transition to other states in the style of automata. In our approach, the state of the type of sensitive data is defined as the set of purposes for which the data can be used. This set can grow or shrink at runtime as purposes can be added or removed during execution.
  In this paper, we have developed PurPL, an object-oriented programming language that features a typestate system that is capable of reasoning about purposes and data usage compliance according to them.
  We give an overview of PurPL through examples and present a formal type system. We have also implemented PurPL's type checker, and we report on our experiments with type checking various programming scenarios that handle sensitive data.

</details>


### [215] [Compiler-Driven Approximation Tuning for Hyperdimensional Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26547)
*Xavier Routh,Abdul Rafae Noor,Akash Kothari,Zheyu Li,Mahbod Afarin,Tajana Rosing,Vikram Adve*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出ApproxHDC框架，通过自动化识别和应用领域特定近似技术，优化超维计算(HDC)工作负载在异构硬件平台(CPU、GPU、ReRAM、PCM)上的性能，利用高效搜索在软件和硬件层面找到高影响配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摩尔定律面临物理和经济极限，需要领域特定方法来加速机器学习工作负载。HDC作为一种新兴范式，具有硬件效率优先、噪声容忍等优势，但缺乏自动化近似优化框架来充分利用其在异构硬件上的性能潜力。

Method: ApproxHDC框架扩展了HPVM-HDC编译器基础架构，支持跨CPU、GPU、模拟ReRAM和PCM加速器的可重定向编译。通过高效搜索和分析在指数级近似空间中识别高影响力的软件和硬件级近似配置。

Result: ApproxHDC能够在不同硬件后端上实现显著性能提升，同时保持最小的精度损失。

Conclusion: HDC算法天然具有噪声和近似容忍特性，结合ApproxHDC框架的自动化近似搜索，可以充分挖掘HDC在异构硬件平台上的性能优势，为后摩尔时代的机器学习加速提供有效方案。

Abstract: As Moore's law reaches its physical and economic limits, domain-specific approaches are increasingly employed to accelerate machine learning workloads. Hyperdimensional Computing (HDC) represents one such emerging paradigm, offering an alternative to conventional deep learning techniques. Rooted in cognitive models of computation, HDC is designed bottom-up with hardware efficiency as a first-class objective. HDC workloads map naturally to heterogeneous hardware platforms, including CPUs, GPUs, and FPGAs, as well as emerging in-memory computing technologies such as Resistive RAM (ReRAM) and Phase-Change Memory (PCM). HDC algorithms are intrinsically tolerant to noise and approximation, enabling substantial performance gains with minimal accuracy loss. In this work, we introduce ApproxHDC, a framework for automated identification and application of domain-specific approximations in HDC workloads. ApproxHDC extends the HPVM-HDC compiler infrastructure to enable retargetable compilation across diverse hardware backends, including CPUs, GPUs, and simulated ReRAM and PCM-based accelerators. The space of possible approximations is exponentially large; ApproxHDC employs efficient search and analysis to navigate it and identify high-impact configurations spanning both software and hardware levels.

</details>


### [216] [Mostly Automatic Translation of Language Interpreters from C to Safe Rust](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27122)
*Bo Wang,Brandon Paulsen,Joey Dodds,Daniel Kroening,Umang Mathur,Prateek Saxena*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: Reboot 是一种从 C 到安全 Rust 的自动化翻译技术，专门针对解释器程序。通过特征分解和多智能体架构，成功翻译了 6 个解释器（6k-23k 行 C 代码），仅需 1-11 次人工干预，通过了 100% 测试集，并消除了内存漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将 C 程序翻译为安全 Rust 极具挑战性，尤其是解释器程序常处理不可信输入且存在内存漏洞，亟需可靠的自动化翻译方法。

Method: (1) 特征分解：按程序特征将翻译分解为一系列里程碑，每个里程碑都是完整可测试的程序，从最简单版本开始逐步恢复特征；(2) 多智能体架构：通过自动化验证和反馈来协调不可靠的编码智能体，减少人工干预。

Result: 翻译了 6 个解释器（6k-23k 行 C 代码），仅需 1-11 次人工干预；100% 通过测试集，验证测试通过率 62%-92%；消除了堆缓冲区溢出、释放后使用等内存漏洞。

Conclusion: Reboot 能够将真实世界的解释器程序从 C 翻译为安全 Rust，特征分解相比仅用多智能体翻译在验证测试通过率上提升了 6%-20%。

Abstract: Translating C programs to safe Rust is challenging owing to significant differences in typing constraints, ownership, and borrowing rules. Interpreter programs are particularly important targets for such translation, as they often handle untrusted inputs and suffer from memory-related vulnerabilities. We present Reboot, a mostly-automatic technique that translates real-world interpreter programs from C to safe Rust. Using Reboot, we have translated six interpreters ranging from 6k to 23k lines of C code to safe Rust, with each translation requiring only 1 to 11 brief user interventions. All translations pass 100% of the provided test suites, and achieve 62%--92% pass rates on separately created validation tests that were never exposed to the system. A security case study on mujs shows that memory vulnerabilities such as heap buffer overflows and use-after-free present in C are eliminated in the safe Rust translation. Two ideas underpin Reboot. First, feature reduction decomposes the translation by program features, creating a sequence of milestones where each is a complete, testable program; the translation starts from the simplest version and incrementally restores features, with each milestone validated before proceeding. Second, a multi-agent architecture orchestrates inherently unreliable coding agents through automated validation and feedback, keeping long-running translation workflows on track with minimal human involvement. An ablation study confirms that feature reduction improves translation correctness compared to using multi-agent translation alone, with 6%--20% improvements in pass rates on validation test suites.

</details>


### [217] [Welterweight Go: Boxing, Structural Subtyping, and Generics (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27138)
*Raymond Hu,Julien Lange,Bernardo Toninho,Philip Wadler,Robert Griesemer,Keith Randall*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出WG作为Go语言的核心模型，支持结构子类型、泛型、类型联合和类型集等关键特性，并通过LWG低层语言模拟Go的运行时机制（如RTTI），采用基于运行时类型转换和适配器方法的类型导向编译策略，实现与分离编译兼容且无需运行时代码生成的实现方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Go语言中结构子类型、泛型和非统一运行时表示之间的复杂交互，给形式化该语言带来了显著挑战，现有工作未能涵盖底层类型、类型联合、类型集以及泛型方法等关键特性。

Method: 提出WG核心模型捕获Go的关键特性，开发LWG低层语言模拟运行时机制，并设计类型导向的编译策略，使用运行时类型转换和适配器方法处理结构子类型、泛型与运行时基础设施的复杂交互。

Result: 实现了从WG到LWG的编译，兼容分离编译且无需运行时代码生成，优于现有的静态单态化方法。

Conclusion: 通过运行时类型转换和适配器方法的编译策略，能够有效处理Go语言中结构子类型、泛型与运行时机制的复杂交互，满足Go的设计和实现目标。

Abstract: Go's unique combination of structural subtyping between generics and types with non-uniform runtime representations presents significant challenges for formalising the language. We introduce WG (Welterweight Go), a core model of Go that captures key features excluded by prior work, including underlying types, type unions and type sets, and proposed new features, such as generic methods. We also develop LWG, a lower-level language that models Go's runtime mechanisms, notably the distinction between raw struct values and interface values that carry runtime type information (RTTI). We give a type-directed compilation from WG to LWG that demonstrates how the proposed features can be implemented while observing important design and implementation goals for Go: compatibility with separate compilation, and no runtime code generation. Unlike existing approaches based on static monomorphisation, our compilation strategy uses runtime type conversions and adaptor methods to handle the complex interactions between structural subtyping, generics, and Go's runtime infrastructure.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [218] [Having Dog Ears "for Real": Effects of Active and Passive Haptics on Embodying Non-Human Body Parts in VR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26364)
*Omar A. Khan,Bao Han Trinh,Lee Lisle,Tiffany D. Do*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过问卷调查（n=63）和2x2被试内实验（n=28），比较了主动触觉（振动手套）和被动触觉（物理头带）及其组合对VR中非人体部位（狗耳朵）具身感（SoE）的影响。发现被动触觉效果最佳，组合模态会削弱被动触觉的优势，且非人体部位SoE与整体化身SoE正相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VR中非人体部位的化身在亚文化中很常见，但真实与虚拟身体形态差异会降低具身感。触觉反馈可补偿这一损失，但现有文献缺乏对不同触觉模态及其组合效果的比较研究。

Method: 第一步：向社交VR社区发放在线问卷（n=63），确定动物耳朵为常见且生态效度高的非人体部位。第二步：开展2x2被试内用户实验（n=28），自变量为主动触觉（振动手套）和被动触觉（物理头带），因变量为主观SoE评分。

Result: (1) 被动触觉产生最强的整体具身感；(2) 组合两种模态反而削弱了被动触觉的优势；(3) 非人体部位的SoE与整个化身的SoE呈正相关。

Conclusion: 被动触觉是提升非人体部位具身感的最有效方式，叠加主动触觉可能产生干扰。研究结果对VR化身设计、触觉反馈应用及具身感理论有重要启示。

Abstract: Embodying non-human body parts in VR is a prevalent practice among certain subcultures and is a personally important creative outlet to many individuals. However, the discrepant morphology between real and virtual bodies can decrease Sense of Embodiment (SoE). Haptic feedback can compensate by increasing SoE felt towards non-human body parts, but there is a literature gap in comparing the effects of different haptic modalities, and their combinations, on SoE. Through an online survey sent out to social VR communities (n = 63), we determined that animal ears are a commonly embodied and ecologically valid non-human body part to study. We then ran a 2x2 within-subjects user study (n = 28) with two independent variables: active haptics, delivered through vibrotactile gloves, and passive haptics, delivered through a physical headband, for when participants reach up to touch virtual dog ears appended to their avatar in VR. Our findings show that (1) passive haptics produced the strongest overall embodiment outcomes, (2) combining modalities reduced the benefits of passive haptics, and (3) SoE towards non-human body parts positively correlates with SoE towards the entire avatar. We discuss implications of our findings in various domains, and on embodiment literature.

</details>


### [219] [Assistive Visual Cues for Visual Neglect Patients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26407)
*Per Bjerre,Andreas Køllund Pedersen,Hendrik Knoche*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究探讨外源性和内源性线索对视觉忽视患者注意力引导的效果，发现内源性线索在目标位于忽视区域时表现更好，两者结合可进一步改善，但患者间差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既往研究多聚焦于正常视力人群，而视觉忽视患者需要有效的视觉线索来提醒其关注被忽视的视野区域，现有治疗方法对此需求尚无充分研究。

Method: 通过实验比较外源性线索和内源性线索对视觉忽视患者注意力的引导效果，并探索两者组合使用的效果，以单例患者进行详细分析。

Result: 内源性线索在目标位于忽视区域时表现优于外源性线索（最佳平均获取时间3.5秒 vs 6.5秒）；两者结合进一步缩短至2.8秒；但患者间表现差异较大。

Conclusion: 内源性线索更适合视觉忽视患者的注意力引导，线索组合可能带来额外收益，但需考虑个体差异进行个性化治疗。

Abstract: Previous research on exogenous and endogenous cues has shown how they direct attention and improve interaction speed and error rate in applications. However, most studies focus on people with normal sight. People suffering from visual neglect have difficulties attending to parts of the visual field. One treatment method calls for the use of strong visual cues to remind patients of their neglected area and help guide their attention to it. Therefore, we examine the effects of endogenous and exogenous cues on visual neglect patients. Our results showed that visual neglect patients perform better with endogenous cues, when targets are within their neglected area. In some cases, combining exogenous and endogenous cues improve performance further. However, the performance varies greatly between patients. Using one neglect patient as an example, we saw that the best endogenous cue had an average acquisition time of 3.5 seconds compared to 6.5 for the best exogenous. Combining exogenous and endogenous cues further improved acquisition time to 2.8 seconds.

</details>


### [220] [TinyCNNDeep: Lightweight Attention-Based CNN for EEG Classification of Eye States and Sleep Deprivation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26506)
*Thien Nhan Vo,Yen Nhi Tran Thi,Ngan Nguyen Xuan Phuong,Xuan-The Tran*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出TinyCNNDeep轻量卷积神经网络，将5通道EEG短片段转换为224×224灰度图像，结合残差学习和Squeeze-and-Excitation注意力模块，在35名受试者的正常睡眠和睡眠剥夺数据上实现四分类（睡眠状态×睁闭眼状态）平均准确率83.69%，远超基线方法（47.66%），且性能优于EEGNet等现有EEG架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 睡眠剥夺会损害警觉性和认知功能，但联合识别睡眠条件（正常/剥夺）和眼睛状态（睁/闭）这一四分类问题尚未被充分研究，现有方法缺乏对此联合任务的针对性探索。

Method: 使用TinyCNNDeep轻量卷积神经网络，将Fp1、Fp2、O1、Oz、O2五个EEG通道的短片段通过逐通道Z-score归一化、最小-最大缩放和中心填充转换为224×224灰度图像，利用2D卷积联合建模通道间和时间结构，网络结合残差学习和Squeeze-and-Excitation通道注意力模块。

Result: 在35名受试者的数据集上，TinyCNNDeep达到83.69%的受试者平均准确率，比最强基线（随机森林，47.66%）高出36.03个百分点，而EEGNet、ShallowConvNet、DeepConvNet等现有EEG架构接近随机水平。残差误分类集中在不同睡眠条件下的闭眼状态之间。

Conclusion: 基于图像的EEG表示结合残差特征提取和通道注意力，在最小电极配置下为睡眠相关EEG多分类提供了准确且计算高效的框架。

Abstract: Sleep deprivation impairs vigilance and cognitive function, yet jointly identifying the sleep condition (normal vs deprived) and the eye state (open vs closed) from electroencephalography (EEG) remains underexplored. We address this four-class problem with TinyCNNDeep, a lightweight convolutional neural network that combines residual learning with a Squeeze-and-Excitation (SE) attention module. We convert short multi-channel EEG segments from five physiologically relevant channels (Fp1, Fp2, O1, Oz, O2) into 224x224 grayscale images through per-channel Z-score normalization, min-max scaling, and center padding, enabling 2D convolutions to jointly model inter-channel and temporal structure. On a 35-subject dataset recorded under normal-sleep and sleep-deprivation sessions, TinyCNNDeep attains a subject-wise mean accuracy of 83.69%, outperforming the strongest baseline (Random Forest with combined time-frequency features, 47.66%) by 36.03 percentage points, while three established EEG architectures (EEGNet, ShallowConvNet, DeepConvNet) operate near chance. Per-subject analysis quantifies inter-subject variability, and confusion-matrix inspection shows that residual misclassifications concentrate between eyes-closed states across sleep conditions. These results indicate that an image-based EEG representation paired with residual feature extraction and channel attention provides an accurate and computationally efficient framework for multiclass sleep-related EEG classification under a minimal electrode configuration.

</details>


### [221] [DanceDuo: Bridging Human Movement and AI Choreography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26507)
*Gia-Cat Bui-Le,Tuong-Vy Truong-Thuy,Hai-Dang Nguyen,Trung-Nghia Le*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: DanceDuo是一个基于扩散模型的AI舞蹈生成平台，支持用户选择音乐、人形模型并导入个人舞蹈视频进行对比，通过人体姿态估计实现用户表演与AI生成序列的直观比较。用户研究显示界面直观，舞蹈对比功能尤其受好评。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用深度学习和生成模型的进展，开发一个能够根据多种音乐风格生成AI编舞序列的平台，鼓励舞蹈练习，并为用户提供丰富的交互体验。

Method: 采用扩散模型生成与音乐同步的AI编舞序列；集成人体姿态估计模型，支持用户导入个人舞蹈视频并与AI生成序列进行对比。

Result: 用户研究发现用户认为界面直观，舞蹈对比功能获得特别好评。

Conclusion: DanceDuo显著促进了AI在舞蹈编舞中的整合，为娱乐和专业应用提供了新途径。

Abstract: In recent years, advancements in deep learning and generative models have revolutionized music-driven dance generation. This paper introduces a novel platform, namely DanceDuo, leveraging diffusion models to generate AI-choreographed dance sequences synchronized with a variety of music genres, to encourage dancing practice. The system allows users to interact with AI by selecting music tracks, humanoid models, and importing personal dance videos for comparison, fostering a rich and engaging user experience. DanceDuo not only offers dance generation but also integrates human pose estimation models to provide users with insightful comparisons of their own performances with AI-generated sequences. We conducted a comprehensive user study, revealing that users found the interface intuitive, with particular praise for the dance comparison feature. Our DanceDuo contributes significantly to the integration of AI in dance choreography, offering novel avenues for both recreational and professional applications.

</details>


### [222] [Budget-Aware Keyboardless Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26508)
*Quang-Thang Nguyen,Gia-Phuc Song-Dong,Minh-Triet Tran,Trung-Nghia Le*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种仅需普通摄像头和打印键盘布局纸张的虚拟键盘系统，结合现代计算机视觉技术（分割、检测模型）与传统图像处理算法，通过指甲颜色分析实现触控检测，无需复杂校准或特殊光照条件，实验表明该系统在实际应用中具有可行性，用户研究反馈积极。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统交互设备（键盘、鼠标、显示器）限制了用户移动性，而现有虚拟键盘解决方案通常需要复杂设置或昂贵设备，因此需要一种更简便、低成本的虚拟键盘方案。

Method: 仅使用标准摄像头和印有键盘布局的纸张；结合现代分割与检测模型以及传统图像处理算法识别键盘区域；通过分析用户指甲颜色实现触摸检测。

Result: 实验表明该系统在键盘和按键检测方面取得了有前景的结果，适合实际应用；参与用户研究的受试者认为该系统有趣。

Conclusion: 提出的虚拟键盘系统仅需普通摄像头和打印键盘纸即可工作，无需复杂校准或特殊光照条件，在实用性和用户接受度方面表现良好。

Abstract: Interacting with computers typically relies on traditional input devices such as keyboards, mice, and monitors, which can be cumbersome for users seeking greater mobility. Virtual keyboards have been explored to address these limitations, but they often involve complex setups or expensive equipment. This paper proposes a novel virtual keyboard system that leverages only a standard camera and a paper with a printed keyboard layout. Unlike previous methods requiring complex calibration or special lighting conditions, our approach can work on standard environment using modern computer vision technologies. Combining modern segmentation and detection models with traditional image processing algorithms, we efficiently identify the keyboard region. Touch detection is performed using an algorithm analyzing the color of the user's fingernail. Experiments demonstrated a promising results our proposed solution of keyboard and keystroke detection for practical applications. Participants attended our user study also found the proposed system interesting.

</details>


### [223] [Co-Designing Community-Centered AI Education for Adults: A Midwestern Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26565)
*Yao Lyu,Leonymae Aumentado,Holden Winton,Jared Lee Katzman,Sparkle Berry,Zachary Rowe,Kimberly Sanders,Tawanna R. Dillahunt*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究在非裔美国人社区开展了面向非高等教育成年人的AI教育案例研究，发现参与者对AI的担忧从泛化转向具体且具地方性的问题，并提出应基于本地社区情境构建AI素养框架以增强社区能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI教育日益重要，但高等教育以外的成年人群体受到的关注不足，本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在主要中西部城市东侧的非裔美国人社区开展案例研究，与社区合作伙伴共同设计AI教育课程，覆盖54名成人参与者（48人线下，6人线上）。

Result: 参与者对AI的担忧持续存在，但性质从笼统的担忧转变为针对AI设计和部署的具体、具有地方情境的问题。

Conclusion: 应从社区能力视角理解AI素养，主张AI素养框架应当根植于本地社区情境，以增强社区应对AI的能力。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) education is increasingly important, yet adults outside higher education receive less attention. We report a case study of an AI education session with 54 adults (48 in-person and 6 virtual) in a predominantly African American community on the east side of a major Midwestern city. We ask: "What does AI education for adults outside formal educational systems look like in practice?" and "What does this AI education session reveal about AI literacy at the community level?" Through a co-designed session developed with community partners, we found that concerns about AI persisted but shifted to specific, locally grounded questions about AI design and deployment. We also discuss AI literacy from a community capacity perspective and argue for AI literacy frameworks grounded in local community contexts that strengthen community capacity.

</details>


### [224] [An exploratory behavioral and electroencephalographic study of artificial intelligence-assisted learning modes in high school students](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26579)
*Kashika Khurana,Ally Liew*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过脑电图和行为测量，比较了三种AI交互模式（导师、协作者、解决者）对青少年认知参与的影响，发现行为上存在显著差异，脑电模式虽未达统计显著但值得进一步研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将AI接触视为二元条件（有AI vs. 无AI），缺乏对交互模式差异及接触后效应的区分，因此本研究旨在探究不同AI交互模式对认知参与和问题解决行为的差异化影响。

Method: 48名14-18岁参与者（男女各24人）进行两次平衡测试，记录额叶EEG频谱活动和行为指标。使用Friedman检验、重复测量方差分析、配对t检验和效应量计算进行统计分析，并通过包含启始、加工和压力三个特征的观察矩阵分析行为变化。

Result: 三种AI交互模式在所有三个行为测量指标上均表现出显著差异；脑电模式呈现描述性差异但未达统计显著性；初步证据表明存在短期AI携带效应。

Conclusion: 研究提供了初步的行为证据和电生理观察，支持不同AI交互模式可能影响神经活动和行为，并建立了可重复的人机交互研究框架。

Abstract: As artificial intelligence (AI) is rapidly integrating into education, concerns have emerged regarding its potential implications on cognitive engagement and problem-solving behavior. However, existing research largely treats AI exposure as a binary condition (AI vs. no-AI), with limited differentiation between interaction modalities and post-exposure effects. This study investigates whether distinct AI interaction modes (Tutor, Collaborator, Solver) influence frontal EEG spectral activity. Electroencephalography (EEG) data and quantified behavioral metrics were recorded from 48 study participants (24 males, 24 females; ages 14-18) across two counterbalanced quizzes in a within-subject design. Statistical analyses included Friedman tests, repeated-measures ANOVA, paired t-tests, and effect size calculations. Behavioral changes were mathematically analyzed in an observation matrix of three characteristics -Initiation, Processing, and Stress-measured on an ordinal scale. Each mode showed significant differences in all three behavioral measures. Descriptive EEG patterns in AI interaction mode were observed, and the possibility of short-term carryover effects of AI was explored. Although the EEG data did not reach statistical significance, the patterns observed across the three AI interaction modes warrant further investigation. This study provides preliminary behavioral evidence and investigative electrophysiological observations, exploring possible AI-interaction-mode-based differences in neural activity and behavior, while establishing a replicable framework for future human-AI interaction studies.

</details>


### [225] [HiLSVA: Design and Evaluation of a Human-in-the-Loop Agentic System for Scientific Visualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26614)
*Kuangshi Ai,Patrick Phuoc Do,Chaoli Wang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: HiLSVA是一个人在环的代理系统，支持科学可视化中混合主动性工作流，通过计划优先的多智能体架构、显式人类监督、逐步溯源追踪和测试时自适应学习，实现人与智能体的协作，提高任务完成率、用户控制和工作流透明度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的科学可视化代理系统过度强调自主性而牺牲了人类的分析控制能力，缺乏透明度和人工监督，因此需要一种人在环的协作式方法。

Method: 提出HiLSVA系统，采用计划优先的多智能体架构，集成显式人类监督、逐步溯源追踪和测试时自适应学习，支持通过自然语言和直接可视化操作实现人与智能体的流畅切换，并采用沙箱执行确保安全可复现的工作流。

Result: 通过代表性案例研究和12名不同专业水平参与者的控制用户研究，在多种自主性设置下验证了混合主动性交互能提升任务完成率、用户控制和工作流透明度，同时揭示了执行效率与人类监督之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 强调智能体科学可视化中以人为本的设计重要性，将代理系统重新定义为增强而非替代人类分析推理的协作过程，为未来协作式可视化系统的发展提供指导。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents enable natural language interaction for scientific visualization (SciVis). Still, prior systems have essentially prioritized autonomy over human analytical control, thereby limiting transparency and human oversight. We present HiLSVA, a human-in-the-loop agentic system that supports mixed-initiative SciVis workflows. HiLSVA integrates a plan-first multi-agent architecture with explicit human oversight, stepwise provenance tracking, and learn-at-test-time adaptation from user feedback. The system supports fluid handoff between humans and agents through both natural language and direct manipulation of visualizations, while sandboxed execution ensures safe, reproducible workflows. In doing so, HiLSVA reframes agentic SciVis as a collaborative process that augments, rather than replaces, human analytical reasoning. We evaluate HiLSVA through representative case studies and a controlled user study with twelve participants of varying expertise across multiple autonomy settings. Results show that mixed-initiative interaction improves task completion, user control, and workflow transparency across different levels of user expertise, while revealing a tradeoff between execution efficiency and human oversight. These findings highlight the importance of human-centered design in agentic SciVis and guide the development of future collaborative visualization systems. We encourage readers to explore our demo video, case studies, and source code at https://hilsva.github.io/.

</details>


### [226] [Reviving Reflection-in-Action: Instilling Designerly Thinking in AI-Supported Ideation through Multimodal Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26626)
*Samangi Wadinambiarachchi,Jenny Waycott,Greg Wadley*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文开发了SketchifAI原型系统，通过对比文本、草图、草图+标签三种输入模态，发现草图模态倾向于增强设计学生的流畅性，但参与者仍偏好文本提示。研究讨论了如何设计AI工具以支持通过草图进行反思，维护学生的核心设计技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI创造力支持工具主要依赖文本提示生成方案导向的输出，而多模态提示（尤其是引入"生产性摩擦"以鼓励迭代和反思）在设计师-AI交互中的潜力尚未被充分探索。

Method: 开发了SketchifAI原型系统，采用混合方法、被试内设计，研究了不同输入模态（文本、草图、草图+标签）对设计专业学生的意图表达能力、创造力支持感知和发散思维表现的影响。

Result: 草图模态倾向于增强流畅性，但在多样性、原创性或质量方面与文本模态相比无显著差异。然而，参与者表现出对文本提示的强烈偏好。

Conclusion: 需要设计AI工具以重新引入通过草图进行反思的机制，确保设计师-AI交互支持而非削弱学生的核心设计技能。

Abstract: Current AI-powered creativity support tools (AI-CSTs) primarily use text prompting to generate solution-oriented outputs. However, the potential value of multimodal prompting in designer-AI interaction, specifically the introduction of productive friction to encourage iteration and reflection, has not been fully explored. To address this, we developed SketchifAI, a prototype AI-CST, and evaluated it with design students. In a mixed-methods, within-participants study, we examined how different input modalities (text, sketch, and sketch-plus-tags) affected design students' perceived ability to express their intent, their perception of creativity support, and their divergent thinking performance. Our preliminary findings suggest that the sketch modality tended to enhance fluency, with inconclusive evidence for differences in variety, originality, or quality compared to text modality. Yet, paradoxically, participants showed a strong preference for text prompting. We discuss how AI tools might be designed to reintroduce reflection-through-sketching, ensuring that designer-AI interaction supports, rather than erodes, essential design skills in students.

</details>


### [227] [Invisible Impact of Empathy on Behavioral Change: Isolating the Effect of Empathy in Long-term Physical Activity Coaching Chatbot Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26641)
*Li Siyan,Kai-Hui Liang,Shopnil Shahriar,Yilin Ye,Shiyoh Goetsu,Wei-Wei Du,Masahiro Yoshida,Tsunayuki Ohwa,Xuhai Xu,Zhou Yu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过构建三个仅在共情水平上不同的WhatsApp身体活动教练聊天机器人，开展六周被试内实验（N=13），发现参与者难以区分共情条件，非共情版本被认为更具吸引力和实用性，但高共情版本与更高的步数增长和更快的意图改善相关，表明共情作用具有 nuanced 性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对话系统默认共情必不可少，但在行为改变场景中，共情的真实影响尚未被充分评估，尤其是动机与依从性往往依赖于微妙的用户-聊天机器人动态。

Method: 构建三个仅在共情水平上不同的WhatsApp身体活动教练聊天机器人，开展六周被试内研究（N=13），通过定量和定性方法评估用户感知与行为结果。

Result: 参与者难以区分共情条件；非共情版本被评分为更具吸引力和实用性；高共情版本与更大的步数增长和更快的意图改善相关。

Conclusion: 共情作用具有 nuanced 性，用户难以明确识别但能影响动机与信任，通过详尽可能性模型的边缘路径解释，为平衡有效性与人性化连接的下代PA教练聊天机器人提供设计启示。

Abstract: Current dialogue systems, powered by large language models, often treat empathy as essential without assessing its true impact, especially in behavior change, where motivation and adherence often depend on subtle user-chatbot dynamics. We examine this assumption by building three WhatsApp physical-activity (PA) coaching chatbots that differ only in empathy level and evaluating them in a six-week within-subject study (N = 13). Participants struggled to distinguish between the empathy conditions, and the non-empathetic version was often rated as more engaging and useful. However, higher-empathy variants were still associated with a larger overall average increase in step counts and faster improvement in intention to follow advice. These results suggest empathy's role is nuanced: it may be hard for lay users to identify explicitly, but it can still shape motivation and trust that support sustained change. We interpret this pattern through the Elaboration Likelihood Model's peripheral route. We highlight design implications for building next-generation PA coaching chatbots that balance effectiveness with human-like connection.

</details>


### [228] [From Content to Strategy: Understanding the Motivations, Processes, and Impacts of AI-Guided Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26672)
*Chang Wan,Angel Hsing-Chi Hwang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过对26名使用AI制定沟通策略的个体进行深度访谈，发现AI引导的沟通在亲密关系场景中具有促进自我反思、缓解情绪、防止冲突升级、提供多元视角和安全空间等优势，同时能增强同理心和沟通技巧，但也引发自我怀疑和独特性丧失的担忧，对长期关系影响看法不一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI已从单纯的辅助内容生成扩展到引导个体沟通策略，但学界对这一新兴使用模式及其后果的理论和实证理解仍然有限，研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 对26名曾使用AI制定沟通策略的个体进行深度访谈，采用质性研究方法分析其使用体验和感知后果。

Result: 参与者强烈偏好使用AI分析亲密关系中的挑战性场景，认为其能促进自我反思、缓解情绪、防止冲突升级、提供多元视角和安全的自我表露空间；AI引导的沟通增强了同理心和沟通技能，但也引发自我怀疑和独特性丧失的担忧；对长期关系影响的看法取决于AI解决短期人际挑战的感知有用性。

Conclusion: AI引导的沟通在亲密关系场景中具有积极效应，但存在自我认同和长期关系影响的二元性，需进一步研究其边界条件。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence-mediated communication (AI-MC) is conceptualized as applying AI to augment or generate message content (Hancock et al., 2020). However, advances in generative AI have expanded its use beyond generating content to guiding individuals' communication strategies, that is, AI-guided communication, yet theoretical and empirical understandings of this emerging use pattern and its consequences remains limited. To address this gap, this study conducted 26 in-depth interviews with individuals who have used AI to develop their communication strategies. Findings suggest participants strongly preferred using AI to analyze challenging scenarios in close relationships, because it fostered self-reflection, eased emotions, prevented conflict escalation, offered multiple perspectives, and provided a safe, nonjudgmental space for self-disclosure. Participants also stated that AI-guided communication enhanced their empathy and communication skills, though some voiced self-doubt and worried about losing their uniqueness. Views on long-term relational impact were mixed, depending on perceived usefulness of AI for resolving short-term interpersonal challenges.

</details>


### [229] [Modeling Adaptive Visual Search in Semantically Hierarchical Layouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26725)
*Saku Sourulahti,Jussi P. P. Jokinen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个计算认知模型，研究信息分组如何影响视觉搜索。该模型基于计算理性理论，将用户行为视为对认知和任务约束的适应，利用分层任务表征来提升搜索效率。通过两项人类研究验证，发现语义分组与空间分组一致时可改善搜索表现。模型能复现任务时长和眼动模式，并可用于用户界面线框的快速原型评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究信息分组（尤其是语义分组）对视觉搜索效率的影响，理解人类如何利用分层记忆结构在认知和视觉系统约束下优化搜索行为，并将这些认知原理应用于用户界面设计评估。

Method: 基于计算理性（computational rationality）构建计算认知模型，模拟用户行为作为对认知和任务约束的适应；模型利用分层任务表征来整合语义和视觉结构；通过两项人类视觉搜索与语义分类实验验证模型，以任务时长和眼动模式作为评估指标。

Result: 语义分组与空间分组一致时能够显著提升搜索表现；模型成功复现了人类实验中的任务时长和眼动模式。

Conclusion: 该模型扩展了既有视觉搜索模型，深化了对分层记忆结构在人类认知中如何被利用的理解；模型可应用于用户界面线框的快速原型设计与评估，有望推广至更复杂的真实界面设计场景。

Abstract: This paper introduces a computational cognitive model to investigate how information grouping impacts visual search, a key consideration in user interface design. The model uses computational rationality to view user behavior as an adaptation to cognitive and task constraints. Our work highlights that humans use hierarchical task representations, exploiting semantic and visual structures to improve search efficiency within the constraints of the visual system. We validate this model with data from two human studies focused on visual search and semantic categorization, demonstrating that semantic grouping improves search performance when it aligns with spatial grouping. Our model replicates task durations and eye movement patterns. By improving understanding of how hierarchical memory structures are utilized in human cognition, the model extends previous visual search models. We showcase our model in the rapid prototyping and evaluation of semantic visual groupings within user interface wireframes, suggesting a pathway toward applications in more complex, real-world interface design.

</details>


### [230] ['A bit of chaos and madness': The AI Assessment Scale and the work of assessment reform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26729)
*Mike Perkins,Darius Postma,Jasper Roe,Susan Sisay,Craig Holdcroft*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过对越南一所私立国际大学和英国一所公立大学的30名学术人员进行焦点小组访谈，探讨了人工智能评估量表（AIAS）在高等教育中的实施情况。研究发现，AIAS作为一种共享语言有助于规范生成式人工智能的使用，但实施效果受治理、工具访问、教师信心、工作量、诚信问题、学科背景和学习目标一致性等因素制约。当脱离学习目标和教师能力时，AIAS可能沦为合规层而非促进真实评估的工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式人工智能（GenAI）给高校的评估设计带来了压力，需要在不影响诚信、公平和有效性的前提下重新设计评估。虽然AIAS等结构化框架提供了一种应对方案，但关于教师如何体验其实施过程的实证证据仍然有限。

Method: 定性研究，在越南一所私立国际大学和英国一所公立大学进行，数据来自5个焦点小组共30名学术人员，采用混合主题分析，以关键AI素养作为敏化概念。

Result: 形成了六个主题：识别与整合AI、促进条件、能力建设、采纳路径、实践中的伦理、重构教学法。教师重视AIAS作为共享语言，但它可能因脱离学习目标、学科背景和教师能力而沦为合规层。

Conclusion: AIAS能够促进真实评估设计和学生参与，但其从政策采纳到教学实施的转化受到多种机构条件的制约。本研究为GenAI评估框架在制度层面的实施提供了实证证据。

Abstract: Generative artificial intelligence (GenAI) has intensified pressure on universities to redesign assessment while maintaining integrity, equity, and validity. Structured frameworks such as the Artificial Intelligence Assessment Scale (AIAS) offer one response, but evidence of how staff experience their implementation remains limited. This qualitative study examines AIAS implementation at a private international university in Vietnam and a public university in the United Kingdom. Data from five focus groups with 30 academic staff were analysed using hybrid thematic analysis, with Critical AI Literacy used as a sensitising concept. Six themes were developed: recognising and integrating AI, facilitating conditions, building capacity, pathways to adoption, ethics in practice, and reframing pedagogy.
  Staff valued the AIAS as a shared language for legitimising GenAI use, clarifying boundaries, and prompting reflection on assessment design. However, implementation was shaped by governance, tool access, staff confidence, workload, integrity concerns, disciplinary context, and alignment with learning outcomes. The findings show that the AIAS could prompt authentic assessment design and student engagement, but may become a compliance layer when disconnected from learning outcomes, disciplinary context, and staff capacity. This study contributes empirical evidence on the institutional conditions through which GenAI assessment frameworks move from policy adoption to pedagogical enactment.

</details>


### [231] [MedSWFlow: An Open-Source LLM Workflow for Drafting Medical Social Work Case Plans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26884)
*Yulin Mao,Shiyu Li,Shuping Song,Yuling Zhang,Yajun Song*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: MedSWFlow 是一个开源、模型无关的 LLM 工作流，用于起草医疗社会工作计划。它将案例规划任务分为六个阶段（评估、问题分析、目标设定、干预计划、风险预判和效果评估），通过分阶段提示生成结构化案例档案、评估表格和服务计划，输出结果作为从业者审核的草稿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗社会工作计划撰写是一项专业性强、流程复杂的任务，传统方法耗时且依赖人工经验。现有 LLM 应用缺乏针对该领域的标准化、可复现的工作流框架，因此需要开发一个开源、模型无关的系统来辅助从业者高效生成结构化的案例计划草案。

Method: MedSWFlow 采用六阶段流水线架构：评估输入标准化 → 问题分析 → 目标设定 → 干预计划 → 风险预判 → 效果评价。每个阶段通过分阶段提示（staged prompting）引导 LLM 生成对应内容，并构建结构化案例档案。系统设计为模型无关，可接入不同 LLM 提供商。

Result: 论文未报告定量实验结果，但提出了一个完整的开源框架原型，能够将非结构化案例输入转化为结构化的评估表格和服务计划草案，输出可供从业者审阅修改。

Conclusion: MedSWFlow 为医疗社会工作案例规划提供了可复现、标准化的 LLM 辅助生成方案，强调输出仅为初稿，最终决策仍需专业人员审核。代码已开源，便于社区扩展和跨模型验证。

Abstract: We present MedSWFlow, an open-source, model-agnostic LLM workflow for drafting medical social work case plans. The framework translates professional case-planning tasks into six stages: assessment, problem analysis, goal setting, intervention planning, risk anticipation, and planned effect evaluation. Drawing on established social work and behavioral frameworks, MedSWFlow standardizes case inputs, builds structured case profiles, and generates reviewable assessment forms and service plans through staged prompting. The system is released as an open-source research framework for reproducible case-plan generation across LLM providers. Outputs are intended as practitioner-reviewed drafts rather than final service decisions. Source code: https://github.com/santhiyacw-droid/MedSWFlow/tree/main.

</details>


### [232] [Optimizing Human-Machine Interface for Real-Time AI Support in the Operating Room: the CVS Copilot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26886)
*Lorenzo Arboit,Nicolas Chanel,Aditya Murali,Pietro Mascagni,Nicolas Padoy*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过17名外科医生的混合方法用户中心设计研究，优化了腹腔镜胆囊切除术中AI辅助安全手术的人机界面(HMI)，其中16/17支持AI决策支持但拒绝自主决策，最终设计了角色自适应的CVS Copilot界面，提供最小默认可视化和可选覆盖层的AI评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统用于腹腔镜胆囊切除术中关键安全视野(CVS)的自动化评估即将进入临床转化，但临床安全性和有效性不仅取决于算法性能，还取决于人机界面(HMI)的质量。本研究旨在探讨AI生成的预测应如何在术中呈现和控制。

Method: 采用混合方法、用户中心设计研究，对17名外科医生（包括住院医师、主治医师和教授）进行访谈，探索交互模式、辅助时机、可视化策略和控制机制，并使用反思性主题分析和人因启发式方法进行分析。

Result: 16/17支持AI术中决策支持但拒绝自主决策；主治医师偏好关键时刻最小AI反馈(13/14)；住院医师偏好可选指导(3/3)带置信度指示器和按需解剖覆盖；外科医生一致优先选择视觉化、医生控制、最小侵入性显示，最强支持为最小覆盖层(16/17)和按需解剖分割(13/17)；持续覆盖层、触觉反馈和数字置信度显示存在担忧。

Conclusion: 研究结果设计了CVS Copilot，一个外科医生控制、角色自适应的HMI，提供基于AI的CVS评估，具有最小默认可视化和可选覆盖层。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) systems for automated Critical View of Safety (CVS) assessment in laparoscopic cholecystectomy are nearing clinical translation. Beyond algorithmic performance, clinical safety and effectiveness depend on the quality of the human-machine interface (HMI). This work examines how AI-generated predictions should be presented and controlled intraoperatively. Seventeen surgeons, including residents, attending surgeons, and professors, took part in a mixed-methods, user-centered design study to optimize an intraoperative HMI for AI-assisted safe laparoscopic cholecystectomy. Interviews explored interaction modalities, timing of assistance, visualization strategies, and control mechanisms across surgical roles, and were analyzed using reflexive thematic analysis and human-factors heuristics. Most surgeons (16/17) supported the use of AI for intraoperative decision support while rejecting autonomous decision-making. Attendings preferred minimal AI feedback at decisive moments (13/14), whereas residents favored optional guidance (3/3) with confidence indicators and on-demand anatomical overlays. Across interviews, surgeons consistently prioritized visual, surgeon-controlled, minimally intrusive displays, with the strongest support for a minimal overlay (16/17) and on-demand anatomical segmentation (13/17). Recurrent concerns included persistent overlays, haptic feedback, and numeric confidence displays, although these were not uniformly raised across the cohort. These findings informed the design of CVS Copilot, a surgeon-controlled, role-adaptive HMI that provides AI-based CVS assessment with minimal default visualization and optional overlays.

</details>


### [233] [Game Changers: Designing and Measuring Dynamic Feedback To Help Users Self-Regulate in a VR Pointing Game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26925)
*Bastian Ilsø Hougaard,Scott Bateman,Iris Brunner,Lars Evald,Hendrik Knoche*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过虚拟现实指向任务，系统研究了三种性能指标（短完成时间、直线运动、高峰值速度）及其三种反馈时机（持续、动作结束、任务结束）对玩家表现和感知的影响，发现动态反馈平均能帮助玩家更直更快地移动，但对部分玩家效果甚微或相反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 游戏中的动态反馈对玩家表现至关重要，但不同性能指标如何影响玩家在指向/操控等核心任务中的表现和感知尚不清晰。

Method: 在虚拟现实环境中设计指向任务，系统操纵三种性能指标（短完成时间、直线运动、高峰值速度）与三种反馈时机（持续、动作结束、任务结束），定量比较不同形式的动态反馈效果。

Result: 动态反馈平均上帮助玩家指向更直、更快；但对部分玩家效果微弱甚至相反。

Conclusion: 研究为游戏设计师提供了设计动态反馈的依据，帮助他们在符合期望游戏表现目标时选择合适的反馈方案。

Abstract: The way games dynamically convey information through feedback is critical to players' ability to perform, learn, and improve. However, it is poorly understood how performance metrics impact player performance and perception in core game tasks like pointing or steering. With a virtual reality pointing task we systematically explored how three performance metrics driving the feedback affected players when rewarding short completion times, straight movements, or high peak speed. across different points in time - continuously, at end-of-action, or at end-of-task. On average the dynamic feedback helped people point more straight and faster, while for others it had small or opposite effect. The study quantitatively compared dynamic feedback across three forms with the metrics driving the form as the intended locus of quantitative comparison. Our work improves game designers basis for crafting dynamic feedback by helping them know when to employ feedback schemes that align with desirable game performance objectives.

</details>


### [234] [Continuous Behavioral Synthesis for Adaptive Health Dashboards: An LLM-Mediated Architecture Integrating Explicit Preference, Spatial Reorganization, and Attention Allocation Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26937)
*Tiziano Santilli,Mina Alipour,Mahyar T. Moghaddam*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用大语言模型作为行为合成引擎的技术架构，通过整合三种用户行为信号（显式微反馈、空间优先级、注意力投入）实现自适应用户界面的即时个性化，并在健康监测领域进行了实例化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自适应用户界面的工程方法存在两难困境：基于规则的系统依赖设计者对用户需求的直觉编码，而机器学习方法需要大量历史数据才能实现有效个性化。本文旨在填补这一空白，实现从稀疏、异构用户信号中进行即时适应。

Method: 提出分层提示构建方法，将时间上下文确定、行为信号提取、显式偏好强制和用户画像合成分离；整合三种行为通道（界面元素微反馈、拖拽交互的空间优先级、悬停停留时间的注意力投入）；采用手动指定的行为解释和时间启发式与LLM中介合成相结合的方式。

Result: 在个人健康监测领域实现了系统实例化，管理14项健康指标和7种小部件可视化模态；进行了多场景下适应行为的分析评估，提供了代表性适应后截图；比较了画像驱动初始化和多信号行为适应。

Conclusion: 所提架构通过LLM作为行为合成引擎，能够有效从稀疏、异构的用户交互信号中即时生成自适应界面，解决了将低层交互模式转化为高层设计决策的技术挑战，在保持解释连贯性的同时实现了多信号的协调融合。

Abstract: The engineering of adaptive user interfaces has traditionally relied on either rule-based systems encoding designer intuitions about user needs or machine learning approaches requiring substantial historical data before achieving effective personalization. We present a technical architecture that leverages Large Language Models as behavioral synthesis engines to enable immediate adaptation from sparse, heterogeneous user signals. Our system integrates three distinct behavioral channels, i) explicit micro-feedback on individual interface elements, ii) spatial priority inferred from manual widget reorganization through drag-and-drop interaction, iii) and attentional investment measured through dwell time during hover events, within a structured prompt engineering framework that continuously regenerates dashboard layouts while maintaining explanatory coherence. The architecture addresses the technical challenge of translating low-level interaction patterns into high-level design decisions through a layered prompt construction methodology that separates temporal context determination, behavioral signal extraction, explicit preference enforcement, and user profile synthesis. The approach combines manually specified behavioral interpretations and temporal heuristics with LLM-mediated synthesis, enabling the reconciliation of multiple simultaneous signals that would be difficult to encode through explicit rules alone. We demonstrate the system through an instantiation in the personal health monitoring domain, including an analytical evaluation of adaptation behavior across multiple scenarios and a working implementation managing fourteen distinct health metrics across seven widget visualization modalities. The evaluation compares profile-driven initialization, multi-signal behavioral adaptation, and presents the resulting interfaces through representative post-adaptation screenshots.

</details>


### [235] [What Holds Back Brain-Computer Interfaces? Uncovering Challenges and Opportunities in BCI-controlled Games for Cerebral Palsy Rehabilitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26951)
*Bastian Ilsø Hougaard,Kirstine Schultz Dalgaard,Kirstine Johanne Stougaard Klebæk,Hendrik Knoche,Mads Jochumsen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过临床治疗师和10名脑瘫患者的初步研究，探讨了基于脑机接口的脑瘫康复游戏设计中的控制感、游戏辅助体验等关键问题，发现零散帮助虽能缓解单调但会引发控制感疑虑，治疗师认为BCI康复可补充传统训练并向自主训练过渡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前基于BCI的脑瘫康复设计约束尚不明确，尤其是脑瘫患者如何通过BCI体验控制感以及如何体验计算机介导的游戏辅助，现有研究对此存在空白。

Method: 从一名脑瘫治疗师的临床见解和10名脑瘫患者参与BCI游戏原型的体验描述中，收集初步的临床和用户视角数据进行分析。

Result: BCI游戏中的零散帮助虽能缓解单调性，但也引发了患者对自身控制感的疑虑；治疗师认为BCI康复可作为传统训练的补充，帮助患者从趣味性练习过渡到自主训练。

Conclusion: 归纳了关键挑战和机遇，以指导未来BCI训练在脑瘫康复中的设计和研究。

Abstract: Brain-computer interfaces (BCIs) offer promising avenues for cerebral palsy (CP) rehabilitation at home and in the clinic, using games that promote engagement and sustained training effort. Nonetheless, the design constraints of BCI-based CP rehabilitation remain unclear, especially how individuals with CP experience a sense of control through BCI, and how they experience computer-mediated game assistance. To address this gap, we present preliminary clinical and user perspectives on BCI-based CP rehabilitation, drawing on in-clinic insights from a CP therapist and experiential accounts from ten individuals with CP engaging with BCI game prototypes. Sporadic help in BCI games eased monotony, but also fostered doubts regarding agency. The therapist saw BCI rehabilitation as complementary to traditional training, facilitating the transition from playful exercises to autonomous, self-managed training. We outline key challenges and opportunities to inform and empower further design and research of BCI training for CP.

</details>


### [236] [Floor Raiser or Ceiling Limiter? Differential Storytelling Outcomes with a Child-Centric GenAI System Across Individual Differences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27067)
*Min Fan,Wanqing Ma,Xinyue Cui,Xiaolu Dai,Shengyu Huang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过40名7-12岁儿童的混合方法实验，发现GenAI辅助故事创作能缩小83.5%的质量差距（"托底"效应），提升创造力和丰富性，但连贯性和叙事结构无显著改善；年龄影响关键词选择策略，图像再生与结构质量正相关但受基线水平调节；提出"机制依赖型支架"设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GenAI有潜力普及创意素养，但尚不明确是否所有儿童能平等受益，需实证检验其在不同能力儿童中的作用差异。

Method: 混合方法被试内实验，40名2-6年级儿童（7-12岁），对比GenAI辅助与传统故事板条件，分析质量差距变化、关键词选择策略及图像再生行为。

Result: 1) GenAI辅助使质量差距缩小83.5%（"托底"效应），但仅提升创造力和丰富性，连贯性和叙事结构无变化；2) 低龄儿童倾向语义距离大的关键词，高龄儿童反之；3) 图像再生与结构质量正相关，但受基线水平调节。

Conclusion: 提出"机制依赖型支架"设计原则，指导面向多元儿童的适应性GenAI故事创作系统开发。

Abstract: Generative AI (GenAI) holds promise for democratizing creative literacy, yet whether it benefits all children equally remains unclear. Using a child-centric GenAI storytelling system for children aged 7-12, we conducted a mixed-methods within-subjects experiment (N = 40, Grades 2-6) comparing GenAI-assisted and traditional storyboard conditions. Three findings emerged. First, the GenAI-assisted condition was associated with a floor-raising convergence pattern, with the quality gap narrowing by 83.5%, driven by lower-end support and upper-end constraint mechanisms. This convergence was dimension-selective, improving creativity and richness while leaving coherence and narrative structure tied to baseline performance. Second, younger children more often selected semantically distant keywords while older children preferred semantically closer ones, although engagement orientation varied across individuals regardless of age. Third, image regeneration was positively associated with structural quality dimensions, though this association was attenuated after baseline control. We propose mechanism-contingent scaffolding as a design principle for adaptive GenAI storytelling systems serving diverse children.

</details>


### [237] [Urban Context and Travel Experience Events: An Exploratory Comparison of Two German Cities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27077)
*Marie Güntert,Esther Bosch*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过移动应用追踪德国汉堡（城市）和图特林根（乡村）91名参与者的日常出行，运用多层回归分析发现：图特林根地区公共交通运力充足对出行体验正向影响最大，不准时和低个人幸福感负向影响最大；汉堡地区不准时和负面信息事件影响最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究影响城市和乡村地区公共交通出行体验的关键事件，以及这些事件如何影响出行行为和体验，为改善本地公共交通提供决策依据。

Method: 使用移动应用追踪21名图特林根和70名汉堡参与者的日常出行，收集实时体验评价和情境数据，采用多层回归分析评估准点率、运力、信息提供等事件对途中体验的影响。

Result: 图特林根：运力充足正向影响最大，不准时和低个人幸福感负向影响最大；汉堡：不准时和负面信息事件影响最大。

Conclusion: 识别出的关键影响因素可为改善本地公共交通的决策和措施提供基础。

Abstract: The presented study investigates events influencing public transportation experience in both urban (Hamburg) and rural (Tuttlingen) areas in Germany, with the aim of identifying events that affect travel experience and as a result travel behavior. Using a mobile application, 21 participants in Tuttlingen and 70 participants in Hamburg tracked everyday trips, providing real-time evaluations of travel experiences along with situational data. Multi-level regression analyses were applied to assess the impact of events such as punctuality, capacity offer, information about public transportation and others on the ontrip experience. Results indicate that a sufficient public transportation capacity offer has the strongest positive effect in Tuttlingen, whereas a lack of punctuality and low personal well-being have the strongest negative effects. In Hamburg, a lack of punctuality and a negative information event have the largest impacts. These identified effects provide a foundation for decision-making and measures to improve local public transportation.

</details>


### [238] [Behind the Mask: A Taxonomic Analysis of Activities in Online Social Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27111)
*Debora F De Souza,Gabriela Beltrao,Berta Chulvi,Sergio Dantonio,Mehmet Gokay Ozerim,Javier Torregrosa,Adrian Giron,Angel Panizo,Pablo Miralles Gonzalez,Helena Liz,Javier Huertas Tato,Sonia Sousa,Alejandro Martin,Monika Maciuliene,David Camacho*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过与领域专家合作和文献综述，设计了一个用于区分在线社交网络中恶意行为者特征、活动和策略的分类法，并通过分析反移民话语的案例研究验证了其应用价值，旨在帮助研究人员和从业者负责任地设计网络系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线社交网络中虚假信息的传播日益严重，现有研究缺乏系统性的分类方法来识别和分析恶意行为者（如机器人账号、虚假账号等）的特征及行为模式，因此需要设计一个可操作的分类法来区分这些行为者并理解他们如何促进虚假信息的传播。

Method: 采用混合方法：1）与领域专家（SMEs）协作；2）系统回顾学术文献；3）基于上述两方面信息设计分类法；4）通过分析反移民话语社交媒体频道的案例研究来验证分类法的应用效果。

Result: 成功设计了一个能够区分恶意行为者特征、活动和策略的分类法，并在针对反移民话语的案例研究中展示了其实际应用能力，表明该分类法有助于识别虚假信息传播中的恶意行为模式。

Conclusion: 该分类法可为研究人员和从业者在负责任地设计网络系统时提供参考工具，帮助更有效地识别和应对在线社交网络中的虚假信息传播问题。

Abstract: The broadcast of disinformation in online social networks (OSN) is a growing concern examined across several disciplines, including human-computer interaction (HCI). The pervasive issue has been prompting novel approaches to identify the malicious actors behind the dissemination of deceptive and fabricated content. Analyzing the characteristics and activities of these actors, we designed a taxonomy informed by collaboration with subject matter experts (SMEs) and a review of the academic literature. Our study explores how to distinguish the characteristics, activities, and strategies of malicious actors on OSN and examines how they contribute to the spread of disinformation. We describe the design process and the application of the taxonomy in a case study analyzing anti-migration discourse in social media channels, and reflect on its potential to aid researchers and practitioners in the responsible design of network systems.

</details>


### [239] ["Everyone Says Them": Deception Typologies, Probabilistic Trust, and Grassroots Safety Knowledge Among Gay Dating App Users in China](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27284)
*Yibo Meng,Lyumanshan Ye,Yingfangzhong Sun,Bingyi Liu,Huidi Lu,Xiaolan Ding*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过22名中国男同性恋用户的半结构化访谈，识别了约会应用中超越个人资料虚假的多类型欺骗（关系、情感、财务、商业），揭示了用户发展出的分层概率性验证策略，以及风险识别作为社区协作实践的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HCI和CSCW研究对性少数男性在约会应用中遭遇欺骗的体验关注不足，尤其是中国语境下的研究空白。

Method: 对22名跨越Blued、Aloha、Fanka、Soul等平台的用户进行半结构化访谈。

Result: 识别了四种欺骗类型（关系、情感、财务、商业）；验证策略为多信号、概率性的渐进过程；风险识别通过经验流通、策略抽象化和规则编码形成社区协作实践。

Conclusion: 男同性恋用户在约会应用中的信任评估是一个多维度的、社区协作的、非二元判断的持续过程，需从社会技术角度重新审视平台欺骗与信任机制。

Abstract: Gay dating applications have become critical platforms for sexual minority men to seek relationships and community, yet they also expose users to deceptive interactions that remain underexplored in HCI and CSCW research. This study examines how gay male users in China experience, identify, and respond to deception on dating applications. Through semi-structured interviews with 22 participants across platforms including Blued, Aloha, Fanka, and Soul, we make three contributions. First, we identify a typology of deceptive practices extending beyond profile misrepresentation to encompass relational, emotional, financial, and commercial forms of deception. Second, we document the layered, probabilistic verification strategies users develop through long-term platform use, showing that trust assessment operates as a multi-signal, provisional process rather than a binary judgment. Third, we demonstrate that risk recognition is a collaborative practice shaped by the circulation of experience, the abstraction of recurrent tactics, and the codification of shared rules within the community.

</details>


### [240] [Reading the Same Data Differently: Interpretive Labor Across System Boundaries in Electronic Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27301)
*Yibo Meng,Bingyi Liu,Zhiqi Gao,Shuai Ma,Hongyu Zhou*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文研究了中国社区矫正中的电子监控（EM）系统，揭示了被监管者与监管方之间存在“解释性错位”——双方虽共享同一数据流，但因数据访问、上下文和推理过程的不对称，导致对数据的理解产生结构性分歧，进而影响行为模式（如试探、策略性适应、过度合规、脱离和抗争）。研究为CSCW领域提供了持续感知作为分布式解释工作的分析框架，并指出了提升数据到决策流程的可读性、可争议性和可问责性的设计机会。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献多将电子监控视为刑事司法工具或合规机制，但缺乏对感知数据如何在日常实践中产生意义的理解，尤其是被监管者和监管方如何从不同立场解读同一数据流。

Method: 基于对中国社区矫正系统中26名被监管者和12名监管方的半结构化访谈，采用定性分析方法。

Result: 发现了“解释性错位”（interpretive misalignment）现象——被监管者因数据解释过程不透明而通过结果推断系统逻辑，监管方则依赖情境知识、专业经验和制度程序从模糊痕迹中重构行为；这种错位导致了试探、策略适应、过度合规、脱离和抗争等行为模式。

Conclusion: 电子监控是双面感知系统，其核心问题在于数据到决策的过程缺乏透明度；提出了CSCW视角下持续感知作为分布式解释工作的理论贡献，并指出了设计更可读、可争议、可问责的系统的机会。

Abstract: Electronic monitoring (EM) systems are increasingly used in community corrections to enforce spatial, temporal, and behavioral rules through continuous sensing. While prior work has examined EM as a criminal justice tool or as a mechanism for compliance, less is known about how sensed data become meaningful in everyday practice. This poster examines EM as a dual-sided sensing system in which supervised individuals and authorities reason about the same data stream from different positions. Based on semi-structured interviews with 26 supervised individuals and 12 authorities in China's community corrections system, we show that supervised individuals infer system logic from outcomes with limited visibility into how data are interpreted, while authorities reconstruct behavior from ambiguous traces using contextual knowledge, professional experience, and institutional procedures. We call this structural divergence interpretive misalignment. It emerges from asymmetric access to data, context, and reasoning processes, and it shapes behavior through probing, strategic adaptation, over-compliance, disengagement, and contestation. We contribute a CSCW account of continuous sensing as distributed interpretive work and identify design opportunities for making data-to-decision processes more legible, contestable, and accountable across system sides.

</details>


### [241] [AI Healthcare Chatbots as Information Infrastructure: A Large-Scale Study of User-Reported Breakdowns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27302)
*Muhammad Hassan,Ramazan Yener,Ece Gumusel,Masooda Bashir*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究分析了59款AI医疗健康聊天机器人的15,000+用户评论，通过主题建模和解释性分析识别出三大类故障：访问障碍与服务不可靠、用户体验与交互质量、计费与客户支持问题。其中隐私与安全问题与最负面体验相关。研究将AI医疗健康聊天机器人视为信息基础设施，揭示了访问、可用性和信任方面的失败如何影响用户。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI医疗健康聊天机器人越来越多地被用于支持健康信息寻求和自我管理，但其性能和用户影响尚待研究。

Method: 对59款AI医疗健康聊天机器人应用的15,000+条用户评论进行主题建模和解释性分析。

Result: 识别出三大类故障：访问障碍与服务不可靠、用户体验与交互质量、计费与客户支持问题。隐私与安全问题与最负面体验相关。

Conclusion: 通过将AI医疗健康聊天机器人框架化为信息基础设施，研究结果揭示了访问、可用性和信任方面的失败如何影响用户，为设计师、政策制定者和信息专业人员提供了改进数字健康系统的可操作见解。

Abstract: AI healthcare chatbots are increasingly used to support health information seeking and self-management, yet their performance and impact on users remains to be studied. This study examines over 15,000 user reviews from 59 AI healthcare chatbot apps to explore how these systems function in everyday informational and emotional contexts. Topic modeling and interpretive analysis identify three recurring breakdowns: access barriers and service unreliability, user experience and interaction quality, and billing and customer support issues. Privacy and security concerns are associated with the most negative experiences. By framing AI healthcare chatbots as information infrastructures, our findings highlight how failures in access, usability, and trust affect users, offering actionable insights for designers, policymakers, and information professionals aiming to improve digital health systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.ET'></div>

# cs.ET [[Back]](#toc)

### [242] [MPE-Adam: Multi-Population Evolutionary Optimization with Adam Refinement for QAOA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26670)
*Chi Quan Luu,Thai T. Vu,John Le*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 提出MPE-Adam混合优化框架，结合多群体进化搜索（全局探索）与Adam梯度优化（局部精炼），用于QAOA参数优化，在MaxCut问题上优于进化算法和SPSA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分量子算法（如QAOA）中参数优化面临高维非凸空间和测量噪声的挑战。现有单阶段优化器无法分离全局探索与局部精炼的职责，限制了互补策略的利用。

Method: 提出MPE-Adam混合优化框架：采用多群体进化搜索进行全局探索，再使用Adam梯度优化进行局部收敛精炼；设计为模块化组件，适用于量子软件流水线。

Result: 在最多22节点的随机3-正则图MaxCut实例上，MPE-Adam相比仅进化算法和SPSA基线获得更高的近似比和更低的方差，改进具有统计显著性。

Conclusion: 结构化多阶段优化能同时提升变分量子算法的解质量和软件层面的灵活性。

Abstract: Parameter optimization is a central bottleneck in variational quantum algorithms such as the Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA). The classical optimizer must navigate a high-dimensional, non-convex parameter space under measurement noise. From a quantum software perspective, this process forms a multi-stage workflow: global exploration of the parameter space followed by local refinement within the hybrid quantum-classical loop. Most existing approaches, however, employ single-stage optimizers that do not separate these roles, which limits the use of complementary strategies. We propose MPE-Adam, a hybrid optimization framework that integrates multi-population evolutionary search for global exploration with Adam-based gradient refinement for local convergence. The method is structured as a modular component suitable for quantum software pipelines. We evaluate MPE-Adam on MaxCut instances generated from random 3-regular graphs with up to 22 nodes. The results show that MPE-Adam achieves higher approximation ratios and lower variance than evolutionary-only and SPSA-based baselines, with statistically significant improvements. These findings indicate that structured multi-stage optimization improves both solution quality and software-level flexibility in quantum applications.

</details>


### [243] [Generative Models on Analog Hardware with Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27294)
*Yu-Neng Wang,Sara Achour*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文提出模拟交互系统（AIS）框架，通过时变分段参数和隐藏物理状态两种机制缩小硬件可实现的动力学系统与神经网络之间的表达差距，并采用Wasserstein GAN训练方法，在MNIST和Fashion-MNIST上以4-bit稀疏架构实现FID分别为27.6和80.8，每张图像能耗23uJ，比数字基线低两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟硬件（如耦合振荡器、模拟伊辛机）能以极低能量求解微分方程，适合低功耗生成建模，但现代生成模型依赖灵活的软件定义动力学，而模拟硬件受限于固定的物理微分方程且近似能力有限，存在根本性不匹配。

Method: 提出AIS统一框架；引入时变分段参数和隐藏物理状态两种硬件兼容机制；采用Wasserstein GAN训练流程（不要求模型遵循特定轨迹）；分析面积和功耗与连接密度及精度的缩放关系。

Result: 基于振荡器的AIS在MNIST上FID=27.6，Fashion-MNIST上FID=80.8，超越此前最佳硬件可实现的模拟生成模型3-4倍；每张图像能耗23uJ，比数字基线提升两个数量级。

Conclusion: 稀疏连接和低位宽量化参数对实际实现是必要的；所提机制有效缩小了模拟硬件与神经网络之间的表达差距，为低功耗生成建模提供了可行方向。

Abstract: Analog hardware platforms such as coupled oscillators and Analog Ising Machines naturally solve differential equations at a fraction of the energy cost of digital computation, making them attractive for low-power generative modeling, yet a fundamental mismatch exists: modern generative models assume flexible, software-defined dynamics, whereas analog hardware imposes fixed, physics-determined differential equations with limited approximation capacity. This paper introduces Analog Interaction Systems (AIS), a unified framework for hardware-implementable dynamical systems, and empirically characterizes their expressivity gap relative to neural network baselines. Two hardware-compatible mechanisms are proposed to narrow this gap - time-varying piecewise parameters and hidden physical states - and a Wasserstein GAN training procedure is developed to enable training of these models without requiring them to follow a specific trajectory. We characterize how area and power scale with connection density and precision, showing that sparse connectivity and low-bit-width quantized parameters are necessary for practical implementation, and estimate an energy cost of 23uJ per generated image for the chosen architecture, representing a 2-orders-of-magnitude improvement over digital baselines. On MNIST and Fashion-MNIST, our oscillator-based AIS achieves FID scores of 27.6 and 80.8, outperforming the best prior hardware-implementable analog generative models by 3-4x with a 4-bit sparse architecture.

</details>


### [244] [An Instruction Set Architecture for IMPLY-based Memristive Processing-in-Array](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27319)
*Liam Splittgerber,Fabian Seiler,Nima TaheriNejad*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于忆阻器存内计算（IMC）的通用微控制器架构，专为超低功耗边缘计算设计。该架构基于IMPLY状态逻辑范式，实现了与RV32I指令集高度兼容的指令系统，并设计了支持忆阻交叉阵列计算的新型寻址方案。通过仿真评估了能耗等电路级指标，并与传统微控制器进行对比，最后通过智能环境传感器节点案例验证了其功能可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CMOS微控制器受限于冯·诺依曼瓶颈和静态功耗泄漏，无法满足超低功耗边缘计算的严格能耗约束。现有忆阻存内计算研究主要聚焦于数据密集型计算的协处理器加速器，缺乏对通用独立IMC微控制器架构的探索。

Method: 基于IMPLY状态逻辑范式，设计了一套与RV32I标准紧密相关的指令集，并提出了支持忆阻交叉阵列计算的新型寻址方案。通过仿真对架构的能耗和电路级指标进行评估，并与传统微控制器进行对比。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提出的IMC微控制器架构在能耗等关键指标上优于传统CMOS微控制器；通过智能环境传感器节点的应用案例，验证了设计的实际功能可行性。

Conclusion: 忆阻存内计算微控制器架构能够在超低功耗边缘设备中作为独立通用处理器运行，有效克服传统CMOS架构的能效瓶颈，为极端能源受限环境下的边缘计算提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The push towards expanded ultra-low-power edge computing necessitates hardware capable of operating under extremely strict energy constraints. Traditional Complementary Metal-Oxide-Semiconductor (CMOS) microcontrollers are fundamentally limited in this domain by the von Neumann bottleneck and by the static power leakage inherent to volatile memory. Memristive In-Memory Computing (IMC) offers a promising solution to these inefficiencies by unifying data storage and computation into a single non-volatile component. However, the State of the Art (SoA) predominantly focuses on accelerators designed to be a co-processor for data-intensive computation. This leaves the prospect of standalone, general-purpose IMC microcontroller architectures underexplored. This thesis proposes such an architecture tailored for ultra-low-power edge devices, alongside an instruction set closely derived from the RV32I standard. Using the IMPLY stateful logic paradigm, a complete implementation of the proposed instruction set is provided, and the novel addressing schema required to support computation in the memristive crossbar array is described as well. Then, the energy use and other circuit-level metrics of the proposed architecture are evaluated through simulation and compared against those of traditional microcontrollers. Finally, the functional viability of the design is demonstrated through an application case study, describing how the proposed design could be used in an intelligent environmental sensor node.

</details>
